# Studio B1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** Implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (- [ ]) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A tutor or a parent of a child uploads a test + its answer key (PDF or .docx) → AI drafts the exercise (or they type it by hand) → they review and **Duyệt** → **Giao** it as a one-step *Luyện* topic. The child does it on the S4 activity page; closed items are checked at once; items the app cannot check wait in Hộp duyệt → **"Cần thầy/cô xem"**; the child sees the wrong items with the right answer and an explanation (never a score or a count); when every item has a verdict the app writes a **Kết quả bài** + **dấu nhãn** (Điểm cần chú ý) and the S7a engine picks the auto-checked items up through route 2 without any change to `lib/engine`. AI spend per family stays ≤ $10 per month in every case.

**Architecture:**
- **Data** — one hand-written, expand-only migration `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql`, built in four parts (Tasks 1–4):
  1. new tables `ai_settings`, `ai_runs`, `studio_drafts`, `studio_item_meta`, `accepted_additions`, `item_decisions`; widened CHECKs on `inquiry_templates` (`kind`, `family_id`, `learner_id`, id pattern `^[AB]-ST-[0-9]{6}$`, empty `target_ids` for Studio), `results` (`attempt_id`, `not_in_app_items`, Studio may be `source = 'practice'` with a frame), `files` (kinds `studio_test` / `studio_key`, `.docx` MIME) and the bucket; RLS; purge guard;
  2. read policies for Studio templates (adults of that child only), `eng_compute_auto_checks` (transform / short against key ∪ `accepted_additions`, Studio only), `eng_engine_inputs` (decided `needs_person` items, same JSON), `eng_register_file`;
  3. writes: AI settings + consent + gate + run log, drafts (create / save / discard / edit-after-approval / extraction done / failed), **approve** (template + sources + keys + meta + paper frame in one transaction) and **assign**;
  4. `eng_record_studio_result` (results + tag_marks, idempotent per attempt; run by a trigger after the episode of a Studio attempt and after the last adult verdict), `eng_decide_studio_items`, the reads (`eng_studio_pending`, `eng_my_studio_review`, draft reads) and the purge.
- **Server (TypeScript)** — pure modules in `lib/studio/` (zod payload, validation that blocks Duyệt, AI-output normalisation, template build, side-panel counts) and `lib/ai/` (fetch client for the Messages API + `AI_FAKE` fixture, pricing table, cap decision, prompt loader). The extraction runs in the background of the "create draft" request with Next `after()` and `maxDuration = 300`; the review page polls the draft every 3 s.
- **Routes** follow the existing split (`handlers.ts` framework-free + `supabaseStore.ts` service role + `route.ts` glue + thin `app/api/**/route.ts`), always `requireAccess` first.
- **Screens** — `/settings/ai` (parent), `/studio/new`, `/studio/drafts/[id]` (board 19 + assign), Hộp duyệt tab `/review/studio` (board 21), and the student review block on `/activities/[id]` (board 20).

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16 App Router + TypeScript strict + Tailwind 4; Supabase Postgres (schema `english`) + Storage (bucket `english-work`); zod 4; `mammoth` (BSD-2-Clause, pure JS, Node) for .docx → text; plain `fetch` to the Anthropic Messages API (no SDK); Vitest (unit + DB on local Postgres 16); Playwright (CI only).

**Spec:** docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-studio-b1-design.md

Background read for this plan: `CLAUDE.md`; `docs/DESIGN.md` §11 (AI), §17, §19.1–§19.10; `docs/UX_COPY_GUIDE.md`; `docs/PROGRESS.md` (S6, S7a); plans `2026-10-22-s6-feedback-revision.md`, `2026-10-23-s7a-skill-states.md`; mockup boards 19–21 (canvas `StudioDuyet`, `StudioXemLai`, `CanXem`, approved 05/11 — not in the repo, described in Tasks 13–16); the spike `git show origin/feature/spike-studio-ai:spike/studio-extract.ts`.

## Global Constraints

- Everything lives in schema `english` (plus one `update storage.buckets … where id = 'english-work'`, guarded like 0016). Never create or change anything in schema `public`. Never run `supabase db push` or `db reset --linked`.
- **One migration only: `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql`**, hand-written, idempotent, expand-only (new tables/columns; CHECKs replaced only by wider ones), needs 0022 + 0023 applied. Its LAST line is `insert into english.schema_migrations(version) values ('0024') on conflict do nothing;`. Never edit 0001–0023. After this slice the next free number is **0025**.
- New append-only tables (`ai_runs`, `studio_item_meta`, `accepted_additions`, `item_decisions`) get `english.eng_forbid_mutation('learner_id')` for UPDATE/DELETE and `english.eng_forbid_mutation()` for TRUNCATE (`ai_runs` has no `learner_id`, so it can never be changed, not even by the purge — the cost ledger outlives a purged child). `studio_drafts` is mutable only while `status ∈ (extracting, ai_failed, draft)` (trigger `eng_guard_studio_draft`).
- Every callable new function is `security definer` with `set search_path = ''`, `revoke`d from `public, anon, authenticated` and granted to **`service_role` only**; routes and pages call it only after `requireAccess` / `requireStaff`. The one student function `english.eng_my_studio_review(uuid)` is granted to **`authenticated` only** and finds the child with `auth.uid()`. Internal helpers and trigger functions are revoked from everyone (`public, anon, authenticated, service_role`). No `grant … on all tables`; grants table by table.
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is read only in `lib/ai/anthropic.ts` (server-only module). It never goes to the browser, a log line, `app_errors` or a commit.
- **Never send the child's identity to AI:** the request holds only the tutor's test file, the tutor's answer key, the tag list (`key — label`) and the skill list (`id — name`) of the child's track and set, and the prompt. No alias, login, learner/family/member id, answer, score, note or level (unit test on the request body).
- AI never checks the child's answers and never decides a level. AI output is a **draft** for a tutor/parent to review.
- **The student never sees** a score, a total, a ratio, "x/y", a count of right items, a tag or a skill. `eng_my_studio_review` returns only wrong items (stem, own answer, expected answer, explanation) and the printed numbers of items still waiting.
- All UI text comes from `lib/copy/vi.ts` and is listed in `docs/UX_COPY_GUIDE.md` (new §3.5 "Studio"). The person who teaches is always written with `tutorHonorific` / `tutorHonorificCap` ("thầy/cô"). Never the word "TOMO". Always-light UI (no `dark:` class). The student is addressed as "bạn".
- Track is configuration: never `=== "A"` / `=== "B"` / `if (track …)` in business logic or tests (ESLint `no-restricted-syntax`).
- AI cap: `ai_settings.monthly_cap_usd` default **10** (CHECK ≤ 10) and `soft_cap_usd` default **8**; the month is the Vietnamese calendar month (UTC+7). At ≥ soft cap the parent is told (home box + Cài đặt AI); at ≥ cap no call is made (`refused_cap`) and only "Soạn tay" is offered. The parent also sets $10 on the Anthropic Console.
- Tests and CI never call the network for AI: `AI_FAKE=1` returns `tests/fixtures/studio/fake-extract.json`.
- Code, comments and commit messages in English; UI text, PR description and docs for the parent in Vietnamese.
- Before the PR this must be green: `npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm test && npm run gen:config`. `npm test` runs the unit **and** DB projects; DB tests need local Postgres 16: `sudo pg_ctlcluster 16 main start` (default URL `postgres://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/postgres`, or set `TEST_DATABASE_URL`). Then the Opus `reviewer` runs (focus on the tasks marked **RISKY**).
- Every commit message ends with exactly:

```
Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_015ozhb22xjdX1tG2koXkZk1
```

## Decisions for the parent

These are the real contradictions or open points found while reading the code against the spec. The plan builds the **recommended** answer; each row says what changes if the parent answers differently. Rows marked **§17** touch counting rules or what the child sees (DESIGN §17: Claude does not decide alone), so the parent confirms them before the PR is merged. The PR description repeats this table.

| # | Spec says | What the code does today | This plan does (recommended) | If the parent says otherwise | §17 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | B-Q3: "Kỹ năng = mã trong bộ kỹ năng hiện hành của em" | Publishing a template (`eng_check_inquiry_template`, `0007` L325–376) refuses a step whose skill does not list `ACT-SOURCE-QA` in its catalogue `evidence_tasks` (the catalogue's "bài được tính"). In A-v1 only `A-T1-TEXT-R`, `A-T1-TEXT-L` qualify; in B-v1 only `B-T1-EXAM-R`, `B-L2-CLOZE`, `B-T1-ARG-R`, `B-T2-ASSUME-R`, `B-T5-DATAOP`. `B-L1-TRANSFORM-W` and `A-L1-PAST-W` (grammar) are ACT-WRITE only. | The skill list of the review screen and of the AI prompt = **current set ∩ skills whose evidence tasks include ACT-SOURCE-QA** (`eng_studio_skills`, same parsing rule as the trigger). Other items get "Không có kỹ năng phù hợp" and still count as signals (nhãn → Điểm cần chú ý). | Allow every set skill for Studio templates: 0024 would also replace `eng_check_inquiry_template` to skip the evidence-task rule when `kind = 'studio'`. That changes which work counts as evidence for a skill. | §17 |
| P2 | §4.1 "một câu có **đúng một** nhãn dùng cho dấu nhãn và khung đề"; §6 "thiếu câu, số nhảy → Duyệt bị chặn" | `test_frames` (0014 L68–163) requires a `tag_key` that exists for **every** item, items exactly `1..total`, and non-overlapping part ranges. | Duyệt is also blocked when an item has no tag (`no_tag`), when printed numbers are not exactly 1..N (`numbering`), or when a part's numbers are not one block (`part_not_contiguous`). | Allow untagged items: the paper frame could not be built (the table refuses it). | |
| P3 | §4.2 results: "ghi `results` mode `frame` … `wrong_items`" | `results_counts` (0014 L215) forces `correct = total − wrong`. Items done only on paper (`out_of_app`) would count as right. | New column `results.not_in_app_items int[]` (default `{}`); the CHECK becomes `wrong + not_in_app = total − correct` (old rows unchanged). Adults read "đúng x/y câu" with y = items done in the app. | Count paper items as right (no new column) — "đúng" would be too high. | |
| P4 | §4.3 / §3 step 7: "Câu sai" = items `incorrect` | Auto-check gives `blank` for an empty answer (counted as wrong by S7a D9). | "Câu cần xem lại" also lists **blank** items, with "Bạn để trống câu này" instead of "Bạn viết". Still no count, no score. | Hide blank items from the child. | §17 |
| P5 | §3 step 8 "Kết quả bài (tự động)" | `results.recorded_by_member` / `recorded_role` are NOT NULL (tutor / parent). | The member of the last "Đúng/Sai" verdict on that attempt; without any verdict, the member who approved the test. | Another member (e.g. always the approver). | |
| P6 | §5.3 "tool `save_exercise` (forced)" | The spike's last commit switched Sonnet to `tool_choice: auto` ("some models refuse a forced tool"). | Send the forced tool (`{type:"tool", name:"save_exercise"}`). If the API answers 400 about `tool_choice`, the same run retries with `auto` (prompt says "call save_exercise exactly once"); env `STUDIO_TOOL_CHOICE=auto` skips the forced try. A reply without the tool call counts as a failed try (≤ 3). | Always `auto`. | |
| P7 | §5.6 "bảng giá trong `lib/ai/pricing.ts`" | No published price for `claude-sonnet-5-5` was in the repo. | **Resolved 06/11/2026:** `lib/ai/pricing.ts` holds `claude-sonnet-5-5: $2 / $10 per million input / output tokens` (official price, platform.claude.com/docs models/sonnet-5-5/overview). Cost is priced by the REQUESTED model; a model not in the table is refused (`refused_model`). | Different numbers: change one row. | |
| P8 | §3 step 0: switch = `ai_settings.enabled` + `consents(ai_text)` | `learners.ai_enabled` exists (0002) and is false for every child; DESIGN §11 lists it as a kill switch. | B1's gate = `ai_settings.enabled` **and** the newest `consents(scope='ai_text')` of that child is `granted`. `learners.ai_enabled` is not read or written (it stays for the later AI Reviewer/Coach of the child's own work). | Also require `learners.ai_enabled` (the switch would then need a second toggle). | |
| P9 | §4.2 test_frames "khi Duyệt: một khung" | `test_frames_first_once` (0014 L58) makes (family, track, lower(title)) unique for first versions. Two tests both named "Unit 5" would collide. | The frame title is `<tên bài> (<mã mẫu>)`, e.g. "Destination B1 — Unit 5 (B-ST-000012)". "Sửa đề đã duyệt" makes a frame that supersedes the old one (same chain). | Refuse a second test with the same name. | |
| P10 | §3 step 4 "Ngày làm + hạn" | `eng_assign_inquiry` puts a one-step template on the due date (the step date moves with `due − date_to`). | `eng_assign_studio` assigns with the due date, then (when ngày làm ≠ hạn) adds a `reschedule` event to ngày làm with the reason "Studio: ngày làm do người giao chọn" (visible to staff only). | — | |
| P11 | §4.2 "item_decisions đi vào eng_engine_inputs" | S7a's route-2 evidence key is the set of attempt ids (`lib/engine/key.ts`). | A verdict given after the engine already counted that attempt changes the numbers but not the key, so after a "Chưa" on a route-2 proposal the same attempt alone does not re-propose. New attempts do. `lib/engine` is not changed (spec §0 item 3). | Wait until all items of a Studio attempt are decided before the engine reads it (one more filter in `eng_engine_inputs`). | §17 |
| P12 | §3 step 5 "bài đọc cạnh câu hỏi (máy tính) / xếp chồng (điện thoại)" | `/activities/[id]` stacks sources above the questions in a 36 rem column for every step. | Side by side from `lg` (≥ 1024 px) for **every** in-app question step that has a reading text (weekly topics too); stacked on phones as today. | Only Studio steps. | |

## How each item of spec §10 was resolved (read in the code)

1. **`ACT-SOURCE-QA` without a source** — works already. `eng_step_supported` (0009 L51) only checks type + mode; `eng_my_step_sources` (0009 L888) returns `[]` when the step has no `template_step_sources` row; the template trigger never requires a source; `SourceReader` renders nothing for `[]`. No change: a grammar-only test publishes with `sources = []`.
2. **`results` frame forces `source='test'`** — confirmed: `results_source_frame check (mode <> 'frame' or source = 'test')` (0014 L218) and `eng_record_result` L245. 0024 replaces the CHECK with `mode <> 'frame' or source = 'test' or attempt_id is not null`; `eng_record_result` is not changed (manual results keep the old rule); Studio results are written only by `eng_record_studio_result`.
3. **`gen:config --check` and config tests skip Studio templates** — nothing to skip: `gen:config` (`scripts/gen-config-sql.ts`, `scripts/lib/configLock.ts`) reads only `content/` and the migration files, never the database, and Studio templates exist only in the database (they are never written to `content/` or `config.lock.json`). DB config tests run on a fresh database per file (`tests/db/helpers.ts` `createTestDb`), so their "10 templates" counts are untouched; the new DB test file makes its own templates. One reader needs a filter: `listPublishedTemplates` (`lib/inquiries/supabaseStore.ts` L18–28) adds `.eq("kind", "topic")` so Studio tests never show up in "Giao chủ đề" (Task 10).
4. **Background time on Vercel** — one request: `POST …/studio/drafts` answers 201 at once and runs the extraction inside `after()` (`next/server`, stable since 15.1) with `export const maxDuration = 300` on that route; the AI call has its own 240 s timeout, three tries share a 280 s budget. Vercel functions run up to 300 s with Fluid compute (default for new projects; the parent checks Project → Settings → Functions). A draft still `extracting` 6 minutes after its last change is marked `ai_failed` (`timeout`) on the next read, so nothing hangs. If the parent's plan turns out to stop functions earlier, the documented fallback (spec §10) is the Message Batches API: send in the POST, collect in the polling GET — not built in B1.
5. **Branch / next migration** — `feature/studio-b1` from `main` (S7a merged, 0022 + 0023 applied). This slice adds `0024`; next is `0025`.

## Decisions taken in this plan (no parent answer needed)

| # | Point | Choice | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Spec column lists | Extra columns: `inquiry_templates.learner_id` (Studio only, with `family_id`); `learner_id` on `studio_item_meta`, `accepted_additions`, `item_decisions`; `studio_drafts.client_id`, `error_code`, `frame_id`, `created_at`. | RLS (`eng_is_staff_of(learner_id)` = parent of the family or tutor **of that child**, §7 "gia sư em khác ✗"), the purge gate of `eng_forbid_mutation('learner_id')`, idempotent create, and the template → frame link. |
| D2 | Result when nothing waits | An `AFTER INSERT` trigger on `english.episodes` calls `eng_record_studio_result` (the episode is written last in `eng_submit_attempt`, 0010 L404, after the auto-check row). Errors are caught and written to `app_errors` (`studio_result`) so a child's submission never fails because of it; `eng_decide_studio_items` calls the same idempotent function. | No rewrite of `eng_submit_attempt`; atomic with the submission. |
| D3 | Who builds the template | The server builds it in TypeScript (`lib/studio/buildTemplate.ts`, unit-tested) from the **stored** draft payload after `validateDraft` passes; `eng_approve_studio_draft` checks the shape, the actor and an optimistic lock (`updated_at` string), then inserts. The existing template triggers (student-safe keys, evidence tasks, keys present, frame rules) still run. | One place for the rules (TS), the database keeps the invariants. |
| D4 | MCQ storage | Options are stored as strings `"A. text"`; the key is the letter (`eng_resolve_option` resolves labelled options, 0009 L67). Other closed items: `answer = [key, ...accepted].join(" / ")` (S4 convention). `open` → key row `sealed`. Item ids `q<n>`. | S4 auto-check and `eng_check_work_content` work unchanged. |
| D5 | Purge of published Studio rows | The four "published / insert-only" triggers on `inquiry_templates`, `sources`, `template_item_keys`, `template_step_sources` are re-pointed to new wrapper functions that behave exactly like the old ones, except that DELETE is allowed inside `eng_purge_learner` for rows of a Studio template of the child being purged (`eng_purge_allows_studio`). The shared functions of 0005/0007/0009 are not touched. | Spec §4.2 purge list; no change for weekly topics. |
| D6 | Actions | `requireAccess` gets `studio:read` and `studio:write` (tutor + parent). AI settings use `requireStaff` + role `parent`. | B-Q2: tutor or parent of that child. |
| D7 | Polling | The waiting screen asks `GET /api/studio/drafts/:id` every 3 s, up to 6 minutes, then shows the timeout text. | §3 step 2. |
| D8 | What the AI receives as lists | Tags = the child's track tags of the skill map (not hidden), around the current level ± 1 (`nearLevel`, SM-2 P4-c) + family tags with a group; the reading-skill tags (`error_tags.group_vi = 'Kỹ năng đọc'`) are marked "reading". Skills = `eng_studio_skills` (P1). | §5.2. |
| D9 | Reading item default skill | When an item has a reading-skill tag and no skill, the first `related` target of that tag (SM-1 leaf) that is in the skill list is filled in. | B-Q3. |
| D10 | e2e home | The Studio e2e suite (`tests/e2e/studio.spec.ts`) runs in the Playwright project that runs last (`purge`), so the extra assignment of "Nguyên (thử)" never meets the other specs. | Other specs count the child's steps. |

## File Structure

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql` | §1 tables + alters + bucket + RLS + guards; §2 read policies, auto-check, engine inputs, file register; §3 AI + drafts + approve + assign; §4 result, verdicts, reads; §5 purge + preview; last line records `0024`. |
| `tests/unit/migration0024.test.ts` | Static checks of the migration text (grants, triggers, kept lines). |
| `tests/db/studio.test.ts` | DB tests on real Postgres (RLS matrix, functions, purge, apply twice). |
| `tests/db/migrations.test.ts` | Two table lists gain the new tables. |
| `lib/studio/schema.ts` | zod `studioDraftSchema` (§4.4), `aiExtractSchema` (lenient AI output), limits, kinds, `EMPTY_DRAFT`. |

| `tests/e2e/studio.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/student-no-scores.spec.ts` | E2E (CI). |

Task order: 0 (setup) → 1–4 (migration 0024, in order) → 5–6 (pure Studio libs) → 7 (whole flow on Postgres) → 8 (.docx + storage) → 9 (AI) → 10 (API) → 11 (copy) → 12–16 (screens) → 17 (e2e) → 18 (docs, checks, review, PR). Each task leaves `npm run typecheck && npm run lint` green; Task 5's `studioValidate.test.ts` turns green in Task 6 (it needs `normalize.ts`).
**RISKY (reviewer focus):** Task 1 (RLS, purge guard), Task 2 (template read policy, auto-check, engine input), Task 4 (what the child can read, results), Task 7 (the end-to-end DB proof), Task 9 (what is sent to AI, cap), Task 10 (access on every route, tutors never see cost), Task 16 (student page).

Migration 0024 is built in four parts. Task 1 creates the file with sections 1 and 5 (purge) and the final line. Tasks 2, 3 and 4 each paste their section **immediately above the line that starts with `-- 5. Purge:`**, so the purge stays last and the file ends with the `schema_migrations` insert. `tests/unit/migration0024.test.ts` grows the same way (each task adds an `it` block above the marker comment `// (later tasks add their tests here)`).

### Task 0: Setup — branch, dependencies, local Postgres, green baseline

**Files:**
- Modify: `package.json`, `package-lock.json` (dependency `mammoth`)

The mockups (boards 19–21) were approved by the parent on 05/11; there is no design gate. Only the rows marked §17 in "Decisions for the parent" wait for the parent, and they do not block the build (the PR asks them).

- [ ] **Step 3: Start Postgres 16.** `sudo pg_ctlcluster 16 main start` (or `pg_ctlcluster 16 main start` when already root), then `psql postgres://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/postgres -c 'select 1'` → `1`. If the password differs, export `TEST_DATABASE_URL`.

Check it loads on the server runtime: `node -e "const m=require('mammoth'); console.log(typeof m.extractRawText)"` → `function`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `english.eng_forbid_mutation` (0004 L25), `english.eng_is_staff_of` (0007 L37), `eng_forbid_published_change` / `eng_forbid_published_parent_change` / `eng_forbid_row_change` behaviour (0005 L159, 0007 L193, 0009 L330), `english.eng_valid_item_list` (0014 L165), the 0023 purge / preview bodies (`0023_skill_states.sql` L953–1210 and L1213–1277), `files` CHECKs (0016 L76–87, 0020 L96–104), bucket insert (0016 L29–43).
- Produces:
  - Tables `english.ai_settings`, `english.ai_runs`, `english.studio_drafts`, `english.studio_item_meta`, `english.accepted_additions`, `english.item_decisions`; sequence `english.studio_template_seq`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing unit test** — `tests/unit/migration0024.test.ts`

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from "../../scripts/lib/migrate";

const sql = readFileSync(path.join(MIGRATIONS_DIR, "0024_studio_b1.sql"), "utf8");
const code = sql.split("\n").filter((l) => !l.trim().startsWith("--")).join("\n");
const fnText = (src: string, name: string) => {
  const start = src.indexOf(`create or replace function english.${name}(`);
  expect(start, name).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  return src.slice(start, src.indexOf("\n$$;", start));
};
const lines = (s: string) => s.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith("--"));
const grantsOf = (name: string) =>
  [...code.matchAll(new RegExp(`grant execute on function english\\.${name}\\([^)]*\\) to ([a-z_, ]+);`, "g"))].map((m) => m[1]);
const NEW_TABLES = ["ai_settings", "ai_runs", "studio_drafts", "studio_item_meta", "accepted_additions", "item_decisions"];

describe("0024_studio_b1.sql", () => {
  it("ends by recording its own version, is expand-only, never touches schema public, grants table by table", () => {
    expect(sql.trim().endsWith("insert into english.schema_migrations(version) values ('0024') on conflict do nothing;")).toBe(true);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/drop\s+table|drop\s+column|rename\s+(to|column)|drop\s+function|alter\s+column\s+\w+\s+type/i);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/\bpublic\./);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/grant\s+[a-z, ]+on all tables/i);
    // the only statement on another schema: the private bucket's MIME list (guarded like 0016)
    expect([...code.matchAll(/\bstorage\.\w+/g)].map((m) => m[0]).sort()).toEqual(["storage.buckets", "storage.buckets"]);
  });

  it("new tables: RLS on, adults of THAT child read (eng_is_staff_of), nobody writes directly; AI tables are readable by no app role", () => {
    for (const t of NEW_TABLES) expect(code, t).toContain(`alter table english.${t} enable row level security;`);
    expect(code).toContain(
      "revoke all on english.ai_settings, english.ai_runs, english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions from public, anon, authenticated;",
    );
    expect(code).toContain(
      "revoke insert, update, delete, truncate on english.ai_settings, english.ai_runs, english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions from service_role;",
    );
    expect(code).toContain("grant select on english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions to authenticated;");
    for (const t of ["studio_drafts", "studio_item_meta", "accepted_additions", "item_decisions"]) {
      expect(code).toMatch(new RegExp(`create policy ${t}_select on english\\.${t}\\s+for select to authenticated\\s+using \\(english\\.eng_is_staff_of\\(learner_id\\)\\);`));
    }
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/create policy ai_(settings|runs)/);
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/grant select on [^;]*english\.ai_(settings|runs)/);
  });

  it("append-only tables use eng_forbid_mutation for update/delete and truncate", () => {
    for (const t of ["ai_runs", "studio_item_meta", "accepted_additions", "item_decisions"]) {
      expect(code).toContain(`'${t}'`);
    }
    expect(code).toContain("array['ai_runs', 'studio_item_meta', 'accepted_additions', 'item_decisions']");
    expect(code).toContain("'create trigger %I before update or delete on english.%I for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_mutation(%L)'");
  });

  it("CHECKs are only widened: weekly topics keep their exact rules", () => {
    expect(code).toContain("(kind = 'topic' and id ~ '^[AB]-W[0-9]+-[A-Z0-9-]+$') or (kind = 'studio' and id ~ '^[AB]-ST-[0-9]{6}$')");
    expect(code).toContain("check (kind = 'studio' or cardinality(target_ids) > 0)");
    expect(code).toContain("check (mode <> 'frame' or source = 'test' or attempt_id is not null)");
    expect(code).toContain("and cardinality(wrong_items) + cardinality(not_in_app_items) = total_items - correct");
    expect(code).toContain("check (attempt_id is not null or cardinality(not_in_app_items) = 0)");
    expect(code).toContain("or (kind in ('studio_test', 'studio_key') and mime in ('application/pdf', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document'))");
    expect(code).toContain("when 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document' then '.docx'");
  });

  it("published Studio rows can be deleted only inside eng_purge_learner, for the child being purged; weekly topics never", () => {
    const allow = fnText(sql, "eng_purge_allows_studio");
    expect(allow).toContain("pg_catalog.to_regprocedure('english.eng_purge_learner(uuid,uuid)')");
    expect(allow).toContain("current_user <> v_owner");
    expect(allow).toContain("p_old ->> 'kind' = 'studio' and p_old ->> 'learner_id' = v_purge");
    for (const [trigger, fn] of [
      ["inquiry_templates_immutable", "eng_forbid_published_change_studio"],
      ["sources_immutable", "eng_forbid_published_change_studio"],
      ["template_item_keys_immutable", "eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio"],
      ["template_step_sources_immutable", "eng_forbid_row_change_studio"],
    ]) {
      expect(code).toMatch(new RegExp(`create trigger ${trigger}\\s+before [a-z ]+ on english\\.\\w+\\s+for each row execute function english\\.${fn}\\(\\);`));
    }
    for (const fn of ["eng_forbid_published_change_studio", "eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio", "eng_forbid_row_change_studio"]) {
      expect(fnText(sql, fn)).toContain("if tg_op = 'DELETE' and english.eng_purge_allows_studio(tg_table_name, pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old)) then return old; end if;");
    }
  });

  it("internal helpers and trigger functions are executable by nobody", () => {
    for (const fn of [
      "eng_purge_allows_studio(text, jsonb)",
      "eng_forbid_published_change_studio()",
      "eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio()",
      "eng_forbid_row_change_studio()",
      "eng_guard_studio_draft()",
      "eng_check_item_decision()",
      "eng_check_studio_instance()",
    ]) {
      expect(code).toContain(`revoke execute on function english.${fn} from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;`);
    }
  });

  it("the purge keeps EVERY line of the 0023 version and its preview; Studio rows go before attempts / files, templates after the instances", () => {
    const old23 = readFileSync(path.join(MIGRATIONS_DIR, "0023_skill_states.sql"), "utf8");
    for (const fn of ["eng_purge_learner", "eng_purge_preview"]) {
      const now = new Set(lines(fnText(sql, fn)));
      for (const l of lines(fnText(old23, fn))) expect(now.has(l), `${fn}: ${l}`).toBe(true);
    }
    const purge = fnText(sql, "eng_purge_learner");
    const at = (s: string) => purge.indexOf(s);
    expect(at("delete from english.item_decisions idc")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(at("delete from english.item_decisions idc")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.attempts t"));
    expect(at("delete from english.accepted_additions aa")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.studio_item_meta sim"));
    expect(at("delete from english.studio_drafts sd")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.files fl"));
    expect(at("delete from english.inquiry_instances i")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.template_step_sources tss"));
    expect(at("delete from english.template_step_sources tss")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.sources so"));
    expect(at("delete from english.sources so")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.inquiry_templates it"));
    expect(at("delete from english.template_item_keys tik")).toBeLessThan(at("delete from english.inquiry_templates it"));
    for (const k of ["item_decisions", "accepted_additions", "studio_item_meta", "studio_drafts", "studio_step_sources", "studio_sources", "studio_template_keys", "studio_templates"]) {
      expect(purge).toContain(`'${k}', n_${k},`);
    }
    const preview = fnText(sql, "eng_purge_preview");
    for (const k of ["item_decisions", "accepted_additions", "studio_item_meta", "studio_drafts"]) {
      expect(preview).toContain(`'${k}', (select pg_catalog.count(*) from english.${k} x where x.learner_id = p_learner),`);
    }
    expect(preview).toContain("'studio_templates', (select pg_catalog.count(*) from english.inquiry_templates x where x.kind = 'studio' and x.learner_id = p_learner),");
  });

  // (later tasks add their tests here)
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it.** `npx vitest run tests/unit/migration0024.test.ts` → FAIL (ENOENT `0024_studio_b1.sql`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the migration (part 1).** Create `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql` with exactly this content:

```sql
-- 0024_studio_b1.sql
-- Studio B1 · đề PDF/Word → bài trong app (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-studio-b1-design.md;
-- kế hoạch docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-05-studio-b1.md):
--   1. Bảng mới: ai_settings (công tắc + trần của gia đình), ai_runs (sổ chi phí, chỉ thêm, không có nội dung), studio_drafts (bản nháp
--      đề, sửa được tới khi Duyệt), studio_item_meta (nhãn của từng câu, chỉ người lớn đọc), accepted_additions ("Đúng" cho câu lạ → áp
--      cho lần làm sau), item_decisions (Đúng/Sai của người lớn cho câu app không tự chấm). Nới CHECK (chỉ rộng hơn): inquiry_templates
--      (loại 'studio'), results (attempt_id, câu chỉ làm trên giấy), files (.docx cho đề / đáp án Studio), bucket english-work.
--   2. Mẫu Studio: chỉ phụ huynh và gia sư CỦA EM đọc; tự chấm transform/short theo đáp án ∪ đáp án chấp nhận (chỉ mẫu Studio);
--      eng_engine_inputs nhận Đúng/Sai của người lớn (hình JSON không đổi → lib/engine không đổi).
--   3. Ghi: cài đặt AI + đồng ý ai_text, cổng chi phí, sổ chạy AI, bản nháp, Duyệt (mẫu + nguồn + khóa + nhãn + khung đề), Giao.
--   4. Kết quả bài + dấu nhãn tự động, Đúng/Sai của người lớn, đọc cho Hộp duyệt và cho con (chỉ câu sai: không điểm, không tổng).
--   5. eng_purge_learner + eng_purge_preview thêm các bảng Studio.
-- Không đụng schema public. Idempotent; chỉ thêm (expand). Cần 0022 + 0023 đã áp.

-- 1. Bảng, CHECK, bảo vệ, RLS ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- 1a. Cài đặt AI của gia đình: một dòng mỗi gia đình, sửa được, chỉ qua eng_set_ai_settings (phụ huynh). Trần ≤ $10 (spec §5.5).
create table if not exists english.ai_settings (
  family_id uuid primary key references english.families(id),
  enabled boolean not null default false,
  monthly_cap_usd numeric(8, 2) not null default 10 check (monthly_cap_usd > 0 and monthly_cap_usd <= 10),
  soft_cap_usd numeric(8, 2) not null default 8 check (soft_cap_usd > 0),
  -- id của english.members, KHÔNG có khóa ngoại (như các bảng khác).
  updated_by_member uuid,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  constraint ai_settings_soft_below_cap check (soft_cap_usd <= monthly_cap_usd)
);

-- 1b. Sổ chạy AI: chỉ thêm, KHÔNG có nội dung đề, đáp án hay bài làm. draft_id không có khóa ngoại: sổ chi phí còn sau khi xóa dữ liệu
-- một em (chi phí tháng phải đúng). Không có learner_id nên eng_forbid_mutation chặn mọi sửa / xóa, kể cả trong purge.
create table if not exists english.ai_runs (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  family_id uuid not null references english.families(id),
  purpose text not null check (purpose in ('studio_extract')),
  draft_id uuid,
  model text check (model is null or pg_catalog.length(model) between 1 and 100),
  prompt_version text check (prompt_version is null or prompt_version ~ '^[a-z0-9_.-]{1,60}$'),
  input_tokens int not null default 0 check (input_tokens >= 0),
  output_tokens int not null default 0 check (output_tokens >= 0),
  cost_usd numeric(10, 6) not null default 0 check (cost_usd >= 0),
  status text not null check (status in ('ok', 'error', 'refused_cap', 'refused_off')),
  error_code text check (error_code is null or error_code ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  by_member uuid,
  at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  constraint ai_runs_refused_free check (status not in ('refused_cap', 'refused_off') or (cost_usd = 0 and input_tokens = 0 and output_tokens = 0))
);
create index if not exists ai_runs_family_at_idx on english.ai_runs(family_id, at desc);
create index if not exists ai_runs_draft_idx on english.ai_runs(draft_id);

-- 1c. Mẫu chủ đề: thêm loại 'studio' = mẫu riêng của MỘT em, sinh lúc Duyệt (không nằm trong content/ hay config.lock.json).
alter table english.inquiry_templates add column if not exists kind text not null default 'topic';
alter table english.inquiry_templates add column if not exists family_id uuid references english.families(id);
alter table english.inquiry_templates add column if not exists learner_id uuid references english.learners(id);
alter table english.inquiry_templates drop constraint if exists inquiry_templates_kind_check;
alter table english.inquiry_templates add constraint inquiry_templates_kind_check check (kind in ('topic', 'studio'));
alter table english.inquiry_templates drop constraint if exists inquiry_templates_studio_owner;
alter table english.inquiry_templates add constraint inquiry_templates_studio_owner
  check ((kind = 'studio') = (family_id is not null and learner_id is not null));
-- Nới (chỉ rộng hơn): mẫu chủ đề giữ đúng luật cũ; mẫu Studio có mã riêng và được phép không có kỹ năng nào (đề chỉ có nhãn).
alter table english.inquiry_templates drop constraint if exists inquiry_templates_id_check;
alter table english.inquiry_templates add constraint inquiry_templates_id_check check (
  (kind = 'topic' and id ~ '^[AB]-W[0-9]+-[A-Z0-9-]+$') or (kind = 'studio' and id ~ '^[AB]-ST-[0-9]{6}$'));
alter table english.inquiry_templates drop constraint if exists inquiry_templates_target_ids_check;
alter table english.inquiry_templates add constraint inquiry_templates_target_ids_check check (kind = 'studio' or cardinality(target_ids) > 0);
create index if not exists inquiry_templates_learner_idx on english.inquiry_templates(learner_id) where learner_id is not null;
create sequence if not exists english.studio_template_seq;
revoke all on sequence english.studio_template_seq from public, anon, authenticated;

-- 1d. Bản nháp đề. Sửa được khi status ∈ (extracting, ai_failed, draft); đã duyệt / đã bỏ thì bất biến (eng_guard_studio_draft).
-- "Sửa đề đã duyệt" = nháp mới có supersedes_draft_id → mẫu phiên bản mới (bài đã giao giữ phiên bản cũ).
create table if not exists english.studio_drafts (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  family_id uuid not null references english.families(id),
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  track_id text not null references english.tracks(id),
  title text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(title)) between 1 and 160),
  test_file_id uuid references english.files(id),
  key_file_id uuid references english.files(id),
  status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('extracting', 'ai_failed', 'draft', 'approved', 'discarded')),
  payload jsonb not null default '{"parts": [], "passages": [], "items": []}'::jsonb,
  error_code text check (error_code is null or error_code ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{1,40}$'),
  ai_run_id uuid references english.ai_runs(id),
  created_by_member uuid not null,
  updated_by_member uuid not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  approved_by_member uuid,
  approved_at timestamptz,
  template_id text,
  template_version int,
  frame_id uuid references english.test_frames(id),
  supersedes_draft_id uuid references english.studio_drafts(id),
  client_id text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(client_id)) between 8 and 100),
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  unique (learner_id, client_id),
  foreign key (template_id, template_version) references english.inquiry_templates(id, version),
  constraint studio_drafts_payload_shape check (
    pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'
    and pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'parts') = 'array'
    and pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'passages') = 'array'
    and pg_catalog.jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'items') = 'array'
    and pg_catalog.length(payload::text) <= 1000000),
  constraint studio_drafts_approved_shape check (
    (status = 'approved') = (approved_at is not null and approved_by_member is not null and template_id is not null
                             and template_version is not null and frame_id is not null))
);
create index if not exists studio_drafts_learner_idx on english.studio_drafts(learner_id, updated_at desc);
create unique index if not exists studio_drafts_supersedes_once on english.studio_drafts(supersedes_draft_id)
  where supersedes_draft_id is not null and status <> 'discarded';

-- 1e. Nhãn của từng câu của mẫu Studio (chỉ người lớn đọc; con không thấy nhãn). Đúng MỘT nhãn mỗi câu: câu đọc dùng
-- reading_skill_key (nhóm "Kỹ năng đọc"), câu khác dùng tag_key. Câu out_of_app chỉ nằm ở khung đề giấy.
create table if not exists english.studio_item_meta (
  template_id text not null,
  template_version int not null,
  item_id text not null check (item_id ~ '^q[1-9][0-9]{0,2}$'),
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  n int not null check (n between 1 and 300),
  part text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(part)) between 1 and 20),
  tag_key text check (tag_key is null or pg_catalog.length(tag_key) between 1 and 80),
  reading_skill_key text check (reading_skill_key is null or pg_catalog.length(reading_skill_key) between 1 and 80),
  out_of_app boolean not null,
  primary key (template_id, template_version, item_id),
  unique (template_id, template_version, n),
  foreign key (template_id, template_version) references english.inquiry_templates(id, version),
  constraint studio_item_meta_one_tag check ((tag_key is null) <> (reading_skill_key is null)),
  constraint studio_item_meta_id_is_n check (item_id = 'q' || n::text)
);
create index if not exists studio_item_meta_learner_idx on english.studio_item_meta(learner_id);

-- 1f. "Đúng" của người lớn cho một câu lạ (transform / short) → đáp án chấp nhận thêm của mẫu, áp cho các lần làm SAU (không chấm lại).
create table if not exists english.accepted_additions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id text not null,
  template_version int not null,
  item_id text not null,
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  answer text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(answer)) between 1 and 2000),
  by_member uuid not null,
  at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  from_attempt_id uuid references english.attempts(id),
  foreign key (template_id, template_version, item_id) references english.studio_item_meta(template_id, template_version, item_id)
);
create unique index if not exists accepted_additions_once
  on english.accepted_additions(template_id, template_version, item_id, english.eng_norm_answer(answer));
create index if not exists accepted_additions_learner_idx on english.accepted_additions(learner_id);

-- 1g. Đúng/Sai của người lớn: chỉ cho câu 'needs_person' của một lần làm bài Studio (eng_check_item_decision), một lần mỗi câu.
create table if not exists english.item_decisions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  attempt_id uuid not null references english.attempts(id),
  learner_id uuid not null references english.learners(id),
  item_id text not null check (item_id ~ '^q[1-9][0-9]{0,2}$'),
  result text not null check (result in ('correct', 'incorrect')),
  by_member uuid not null,
  by_role text not null check (by_role in ('tutor', 'parent')),
  client_id text not null check (pg_catalog.length(pg_catalog.btrim(client_id)) between 8 and 100),
  at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  unique (attempt_id, item_id)
);
create index if not exists item_decisions_learner_idx on english.item_decisions(learner_id);

-- 1h. Kết quả bài: lần làm Studio (attempt_id, mỗi lần làm tối đa một kết quả) và câu chỉ làm trên giấy (không tính đúng, không tính sai).
alter table english.results add column if not exists attempt_id uuid references english.attempts(id);
alter table english.results add column if not exists not_in_app_items int[] not null default '{}';
create unique index if not exists results_attempt_once on english.results(attempt_id) where attempt_id is not null;
alter table english.results drop constraint if exists results_counts;
alter table english.results add constraint results_counts check (
  correct between 0 and total_items
  and cardinality(wrong_items) + cardinality(not_in_app_items) = total_items - correct
  and english.eng_valid_item_list(wrong_items, total_items)
  and english.eng_valid_item_list(not_in_app_items, total_items)
  and not (wrong_items && not_in_app_items));
alter table english.results drop constraint if exists results_source_frame;
alter table english.results add constraint results_source_frame check (mode <> 'frame' or source = 'test' or attempt_id is not null);
alter table english.results drop constraint if exists results_studio_shape;
alter table english.results add constraint results_studio_shape check (attempt_id is not null or cardinality(not_in_app_items) = 0);

-- 1i. File: đề và đáp án Studio (PDF hoặc Word .docx). .docx CHỈ cho hai loại này. Bản cũ của mỗi CHECK là trường hợp riêng của bản mới.
alter table english.files drop constraint if exists files_kind_check;
alter table english.files add constraint files_kind_check
  check (kind in ('assignment_pdf', 'answer_key', 'result_image', 'work_photo', 'external_result', 'studio_test', 'studio_key'));
alter table english.files drop constraint if exists files_mime_check;
alter table english.files add constraint files_mime_check check (mime in (
  'application/pdf', 'image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document'));
alter table english.files drop constraint if exists files_kind_mime;
alter table english.files add constraint files_kind_mime check (
  (kind = 'assignment_pdf' and mime = 'application/pdf')
  or (kind in ('answer_key', 'work_photo', 'external_result') and mime <> 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document')
  or (kind = 'result_image' and mime in ('image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp'))
  or (kind in ('studio_test', 'studio_key') and mime in ('application/pdf', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document')));
alter table english.files drop constraint if exists files_path_shape;
alter table english.files add constraint files_path_shape check (
  path = family_id::text || '/' || coalesce(learner_id::text, 'shared') || '/' || id::text ||
    case mime
      when 'application/pdf' then '.pdf'
      when 'image/png' then '.png'
      when 'image/jpeg' then '.jpg'
      when 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document' then '.docx'
      else '.webp' end);

-- Kho file: thêm .docx vào danh sách loại file của bucket english-work (chỉ bucket này; CI chỉ có Postgres trơn thì bỏ qua).
do $$
begin
  if pg_catalog.to_regclass('storage.buckets') is not null then
    begin
      update storage.buckets
        set allowed_mime_types = array['application/pdf', 'image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp',
                                       'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document']
        where id = 'english-work';
    exception when others then
      raise warning 'english-work bucket MIME list was not updated here (%): the app sets it when it (re)creates the bucket', sqlerrm;
    end;
  end if;
end
$$;

-- 1j. Chỉ thêm (theo hướng dẫn đầu 0004). ai_runs không có learner_id: không bao giờ sửa / xóa được.
do $$
declare
  t text;
begin
  foreach t in array array['ai_runs', 'studio_item_meta', 'accepted_additions', 'item_decisions'] loop
    execute format('drop trigger if exists %I on english.%I', t || '_forbid_mutation', t);
    execute format(
      'create trigger %I before update or delete on english.%I for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_mutation(%L)',
      t || '_forbid_mutation', t, 'learner_id');
    execute format('drop trigger if exists %I on english.%I', t || '_forbid_truncate', t);
    execute format(
      'create trigger %I before truncate on english.%I for each statement execute function english.eng_forbid_mutation()',
      t || '_forbid_truncate', t);
  end loop;
end
$$;

-- 1k. Bản nháp: sửa khi còn là nháp, đúng các bước chuyển; xóa chỉ trong eng_purge_learner (cùng "cửa" với eng_forbid_mutation).
create or replace function english.eng_guard_studio_draft() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_purge text := nullif(pg_catalog.current_setting('app.purge_learner', true), '');
  v_owner text;
begin
  if tg_op = 'TRUNCATE' then
    raise exception 'english.studio_drafts: TRUNCATE is forbidden' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    if v_purge is not null and old.learner_id::text = v_purge then
      select r.rolname into v_owner
      from pg_catalog.pg_proc p join pg_catalog.pg_roles r on r.oid = p.proowner
      where p.oid = pg_catalog.to_regprocedure('english.eng_purge_learner(uuid,uuid)');
      if v_owner is not null and current_user = v_owner then return old; end if;
    end if;
    raise exception 'english.studio_drafts: DELETE is forbidden (discard the draft instead)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if old.status in ('approved', 'discarded') then
    raise exception 'english.studio_drafts: an approved or discarded draft cannot change (make a new draft instead)' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if new.id <> old.id or new.family_id <> old.family_id or new.learner_id <> old.learner_id or new.track_id <> old.track_id
     or new.created_by_member <> old.created_by_member or new.created_at <> old.created_at or new.client_id <> old.client_id
     or new.test_file_id is distinct from old.test_file_id or new.key_file_id is distinct from old.key_file_id
     or new.supersedes_draft_id is distinct from old.supersedes_draft_id then
    raise exception 'english.studio_drafts: owner, files, origin and client id of a draft never change' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not (new.status = old.status
          or (old.status = 'extracting' and new.status in ('draft', 'ai_failed', 'discarded'))
          or (old.status in ('ai_failed', 'draft') and new.status in ('draft', 'approved', 'discarded'))) then
    raise exception 'english.studio_drafts: % → % is not allowed', old.status, new.status using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  new.updated_at := pg_catalog.clock_timestamp();
  return new;
end
$$;
drop trigger if exists studio_drafts_guard on english.studio_drafts;
create trigger studio_drafts_guard before update or delete on english.studio_drafts
  for each row execute function english.eng_guard_studio_draft();
drop trigger if exists studio_drafts_guard_truncate on english.studio_drafts;
create trigger studio_drafts_guard_truncate before truncate on english.studio_drafts
  for each statement execute function english.eng_guard_studio_draft();

-- 1l. Đúng/Sai chỉ cho câu 'needs_person' của chính lần làm bài Studio đó.
create or replace function english.eng_check_item_decision() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if not exists (
    select 1
    from english.attempt_auto_checks c
    join english.attempts t on t.id = c.attempt_id
    join english.activity_instances a on a.id = t.activity_instance_id
    join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id
    join english.inquiry_templates tp on tp.id = i.template_id and tp.version = i.template_version
    where c.attempt_id = new.attempt_id and t.learner_id = new.learner_id and tp.kind = 'studio'
      and c.checks -> 'items' -> new.item_id ->> 'result' = 'needs_person') then
    raise exception 'item_decisions: only an item waiting for a person in a Studio attempt can be decided (not_pending_item)'
      using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end
$$;
drop trigger if exists item_decisions_check on english.item_decisions;
create trigger item_decisions_check before insert on english.item_decisions
  for each row execute function english.eng_check_item_decision();

-- 1m. Một mẫu Studio chỉ giao được cho chính em của nó (B1: muốn dùng cho em khác thì tải lại cho em đó).
create or replace function english.eng_check_studio_instance() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if exists (
    select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
    where t.id = new.template_id and t.version = new.template_version and t.kind = 'studio' and t.learner_id <> new.learner_id) then
    raise exception 'inquiry_instances: a Studio template belongs to one learner (studio_other_learner)' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end
$$;
drop trigger if exists inquiry_instances_studio_owner on english.inquiry_instances;
create trigger inquiry_instances_studio_owner before insert on english.inquiry_instances
  for each row execute function english.eng_check_studio_instance();

-- 1n. Xóa dữ liệu một em cũng xóa mẫu Studio của em (đã công bố). Bốn trigger "đã công bố / chỉ thêm" của mẫu, nguồn, khóa và nối nguồn
-- được trỏ sang hàm mới: y hệt hàm cũ (cùng thông báo, cùng mã lỗi), chỉ thêm một ngoại lệ — DELETE bên trong eng_purge_learner, cho dòng
-- thuộc mẫu Studio của đúng em đang xóa. Mẫu chủ đề tuần không bao giờ xóa được. Ba hàm dùng chung của 0005/0007/0009 không đổi.
create or replace function english.eng_purge_allows_studio(p_table text, p_old jsonb) returns boolean
language plpgsql stable set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_purge text := nullif(pg_catalog.current_setting('app.purge_learner', true), '');
  v_owner text;
begin
  if v_purge is null then return false; end if;
  select r.rolname into v_owner
  from pg_catalog.pg_proc p join pg_catalog.pg_roles r on r.oid = p.proowner
  where p.oid = pg_catalog.to_regprocedure('english.eng_purge_learner(uuid,uuid)');
  if v_owner is null or current_user <> v_owner then return false; end if;
  return case p_table
    when 'inquiry_templates' then coalesce(p_old ->> 'kind' = 'studio' and p_old ->> 'learner_id' = v_purge, false)
    when 'template_item_keys' then exists (
      select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
      where t.id = p_old ->> 'template_id' and t.version = (p_old ->> 'template_version')::int
        and t.kind = 'studio' and t.learner_id::text = v_purge)
    when 'template_step_sources' then exists (
      select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
      where t.id = p_old ->> 'template_id' and t.version = (p_old ->> 'template_version')::int
        and t.kind = 'studio' and t.learner_id::text = v_purge)
    when 'sources' then exists (
      select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
      where t.kind = 'studio' and t.learner_id::text = v_purge and (p_old ->> 'id') like ('ST-' || t.id || '-%'))
    else false
  end;
end
$$;

-- = eng_forbid_published_change('self') (0005) + ngoại lệ purge Studio.
create or replace function english.eng_forbid_published_change_studio() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if tg_op = 'TRUNCATE' then
    raise exception 'english.%: TRUNCATE is forbidden on versioned configuration', tg_table_name using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then return new; end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' and english.eng_purge_allows_studio(tg_table_name, pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old)) then return old; end if;
  if old.published_at is not null then
    raise exception 'english.%: % is forbidden on a published configuration row (publish a new version instead)', tg_table_name, tg_op
      using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then return old; end if;
  return new;
end
$$;

-- = eng_forbid_published_parent_change('template') (0007) + ngoại lệ purge Studio.
create or replace function english.eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_id text;
  v_ver int;
  v_published boolean;
begin
  if tg_op = 'TRUNCATE' then
    raise exception 'english.%: TRUNCATE is forbidden on versioned content', tg_table_name using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' and english.eng_purge_allows_studio(tg_table_name, pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old)) then return old; end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    v_id := new.template_id; v_ver := new.template_version;
  else
    v_id := old.template_id; v_ver := old.template_version;
  end if;
  select t.published_at is not null into v_published from english.inquiry_templates t where t.id = v_id and t.version = v_ver;
  if coalesce(v_published, false) then
    raise exception 'english.%: % is forbidden once the parent % %/% is published (publish a new version instead)',
      tg_table_name, tg_op, 'template', v_id, v_ver using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then return old; end if;
  return new;
end
$$;

-- = eng_forbid_row_change() (0009) + ngoại lệ purge Studio.
create or replace function english.eng_forbid_row_change_studio() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' and english.eng_purge_allows_studio(tg_table_name, pg_catalog.to_jsonb(old)) then return old; end if;
  raise exception 'english.%: % is forbidden (rows are insert-only)', tg_table_name, tg_op using errcode = '42501';
end
$$;

drop trigger if exists inquiry_templates_immutable on english.inquiry_templates;
create trigger inquiry_templates_immutable
  before update or delete on english.inquiry_templates
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_published_change_studio();
drop trigger if exists sources_immutable on english.sources;
create trigger sources_immutable
  before update or delete on english.sources
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_published_change_studio();
drop trigger if exists template_item_keys_immutable on english.template_item_keys;
create trigger template_item_keys_immutable
  before insert or update or delete on english.template_item_keys
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio();
drop trigger if exists template_step_sources_immutable on english.template_step_sources;
create trigger template_step_sources_immutable
  before update or delete on english.template_step_sources
  for each row execute function english.eng_forbid_row_change_studio();

revoke execute on function english.eng_purge_allows_studio(text, jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_forbid_published_change_studio() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_forbid_published_parent_change_studio() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_forbid_row_change_studio() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_guard_studio_draft() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_check_item_decision() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke execute on function english.eng_check_studio_instance() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 1o. RLS: nháp, nhãn câu, đáp án chấp nhận, Đúng/Sai — phụ huynh và gia sư CỦA EM đọc (gia sư em khác, học sinh: không). Bảng AI: không
-- vai nào của app đọc thẳng (chỉ qua hàm, chỉ phụ huynh). Không ai (kể cả service_role) ghi thẳng: chỉ các hàm security definer.
alter table english.ai_settings enable row level security;
alter table english.ai_runs enable row level security;
alter table english.studio_drafts enable row level security;
alter table english.studio_item_meta enable row level security;
alter table english.accepted_additions enable row level security;
alter table english.item_decisions enable row level security;
revoke all on english.ai_settings, english.ai_runs, english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke insert, update, delete, truncate on english.ai_settings, english.ai_runs, english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions from service_role;
grant select on english.studio_drafts, english.studio_item_meta, english.accepted_additions, english.item_decisions to authenticated;
drop policy if exists studio_drafts_select on english.studio_drafts;
create policy studio_drafts_select on english.studio_drafts
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));
drop policy if exists studio_item_meta_select on english.studio_item_meta;
create policy studio_item_meta_select on english.studio_item_meta
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));
drop policy if exists accepted_additions_select on english.accepted_additions;
create policy accepted_additions_select on english.accepted_additions
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));
drop policy if exists item_decisions_select on english.item_decisions;
create policy item_decisions_select on english.item_decisions
  for select to authenticated
  using (english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id));

-- 5. Purge: thêm các bảng Studio (giữ nguyên mọi dòng của 0023; có test đối chiếu từng dòng).
<<PURGE FUNCTIONS — Step 4>>

insert into english.schema_migrations(version) values ('0024') on conflict do nothing;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the purge section.** Replace the placeholder line `<<PURGE FUNCTIONS — Step 4>>` with the two functions `english.eng_purge_learner(p_learner_id uuid, p_actor_user_id uuid default null)` and `english.eng_purge_preview(p_learner uuid)` **copied verbatim** from `db/migrations/0023_skill_states.sql` (L953–1210 including its `revoke`/`grant` lines, and L1213–1277 including its `revoke`/`grant` lines; do not copy the 0023 `schema_migrations` line), then make exactly these edits to the copies:
  1. Comment line above `eng_purge_learner`: keep it as `-- 5. Purge: …` (the line above, already in the file) — delete the copied 0023 comment line `-- 8. Purge: …`.
  2. In the `declare` block of `eng_purge_learner`, after `  n_episode_marks int := 0;` add:

```sql
  n_item_decisions int := 0;
  n_accepted_additions int := 0;
  n_studio_item_meta int := 0;
  n_studio_drafts int := 0;
  n_studio_step_sources int := 0;
  n_studio_sources int := 0;
  n_studio_template_keys int := 0;
  n_studio_templates int := 0;
```

  3. After the two lines `  delete from english.episode_marks em where em.learner_id = p_learner_id;` / `  get diagnostics n_episode_marks = row_count;` add:

```sql

  -- Studio B1: Đúng/Sai của người lớn và đáp án chấp nhận (trỏ tới attempts → trước attempts; đáp án chấp nhận trỏ tới nhãn câu),
  -- nhãn của từng câu, bản nháp đề (trỏ tới files và mẫu → trước files). Mẫu Studio của em: sau inquiry_instances (bên dưới).
  delete from english.item_decisions idc where idc.learner_id = p_learner_id;
  get diagnostics n_item_decisions = row_count;

  delete from english.accepted_additions aa where aa.learner_id = p_learner_id;
  get diagnostics n_accepted_additions = row_count;

  delete from english.studio_item_meta sim where sim.learner_id = p_learner_id;
  get diagnostics n_studio_item_meta = row_count;

  delete from english.studio_drafts sd where sd.learner_id = p_learner_id;
  get diagnostics n_studio_drafts = row_count;
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { applyMigrations } from "../../scripts/lib/migrate";
import { asCommit, createTestDb, migrate, rowsAs, seed, type Seeded, type TestDb } from "./helpers";

/** Studio B1 (migration 0024): tables, guards, RLS; then approve → assign → work → verdicts → result → purge. One database for the file. */
describe("Studio B1 (migration 0024)", () => {
  let db: TestDb;
  let s: Seeded;
  const q1 = async <R extends Record<string, unknown>>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) => (await db.client.query<R>(sql, params)).rows;
  const fail = async (p: Promise<unknown>, code: string, re?: RegExp) => {
    const err = await p.then(() => null, (e: unknown) => e as { code?: string; message?: string });
    expect(err, "expected an error").not.toBeNull();
    expect(err?.code, err?.message).toBe(code);
    if (re) expect(err?.message).toMatch(re);
  };
  /** A bare draft row written as the table owner (tests only: the app writes through the functions of Task 3). */
  const rawDraft = async (learner: string, status = "draft") =>
    (await q1<{ id: string }>(
      `insert into english.studio_drafts(family_id, learner_id, track_id, title, status, created_by_member, updated_by_member, client_id)
       select l.family_id, l.id, l.track_id, 'Đề thử', $2, $3, $3, $4 from english.learners l where l.id = $1 returning id`,
      [learner, status, s.members.parent, `raw-${randomUUID()}`],
    ))[0]!.id;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDb();
    await migrate(db.client);
    s = await seed(db.client);
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db?.drop();
  });

  describe("1. tables, guards, RLS", () => {
    it("applies twice without error (idempotent)", async () => {
      await applyMigrations(db.client, { all: true });
      expect((await q1("select 1 from english.schema_migrations where version = '0024'")).length).toBe(1);
    });

    it("weekly topics keep their rules; Studio ids and owners are checked", async () => {
      expect(await q1("select count(*)::int as n from english.inquiry_templates where kind <> 'topic'")).toEqual([{ n: 0 }]);
      const tpl = (await q1<Record<string, unknown>>("select * from english.inquiry_templates where id = 'B-W1-HOMEWORK' and version = 1"))[0]!;
      // a Studio row needs its own id pattern and an owner; a topic row may not have an owner
      await fail(
        q1(
          `insert into english.inquiry_templates(id, version, track_id, title_vi, big_question_en, big_question_vi, week, date_from, date_to, domain_bucket,
             target_ids, steps, sources, catalogue_version, source_hash, kind)
           values ('B-W9-X', 1, 'B', 't', 't', 't', 0, current_date, current_date, 'studio', '{}', $1, '[]', $2, 'h', 'studio')`,
          [JSON.stringify(tpl.steps), tpl.catalogue_version],
        ),
        "23514",
      );
      await fail(
        q1(
          `insert into english.inquiry_templates(id, version, track_id, title_vi, big_question_en, big_question_vi, week, date_from, date_to, domain_bucket,
             target_ids, steps, sources, catalogue_version, source_hash, kind, family_id, learner_id)
           values ('B-ST-000999', 1, 'B', 't', 't', 't', 0, current_date, current_date, 'studio', '{}', $1, '[]', $2, 'h', 'topic', $3, $4)`,
          [JSON.stringify(tpl.steps), tpl.catalogue_version, s.familyId, s.learners.nguyen],
        ),
        "23514",
      );
    });

    it("a draft changes only while it is a draft, along the allowed steps; it is never deleted outside the purge", async () => {
      const id = await rawDraft(s.learners.nguyen, "extracting");
      await q1("update english.studio_drafts set status = 'draft', payload = '{\"parts\":[],\"passages\":[],\"items\":[]}' where id = $1", [id]);
      await fail(q1("update english.studio_drafts set status = 'extracting' where id = $1", [id]), "23514");
      await fail(q1("update english.studio_drafts set learner_id = $2 where id = $1", [id, s.learners.an]), "42501");
      await fail(q1("update english.studio_drafts set payload = '[]' where id = $1", [id]), "23514");
      await q1("update english.studio_drafts set status = 'discarded' where id = $1", [id]);
      await fail(q1("update english.studio_drafts set title = 'x' where id = $1", [id]), "42501");
      await fail(q1("delete from english.studio_drafts where id = $1", [id]), "42501");
      await fail(q1("truncate english.studio_drafts"), "42501");
    });

    it("AI tables: no app role reads them; nobody writes them directly; ai_runs never changes", async () => {
      for (const role of ["anon", "authenticated"]) {
        for (const t of ["ai_settings", "ai_runs"]) {
          expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_table_privilege('${role}', 'english.${t}', 'select') as ok`))[0]?.ok, `${role} ${t}`).toBe(false);
        }
      }
      for (const t of ["ai_settings", "ai_runs", "studio_drafts", "studio_item_meta", "accepted_additions", "item_decisions"]) {
        for (const priv of ["insert", "update", "delete"]) {
          expect((await q1<{ ok: boolean }>(`select has_table_privilege('service_role', 'english.${t}', '${priv}') as ok`))[0]?.ok, `${t} ${priv}`).toBe(false);
        }
      }
      const run = (await q1<{ id: string }>("insert into english.ai_runs(family_id, purpose, status) values ($1, 'studio_extract', 'refused_off') returning id", [s.familyId]))[0]!.id;
      await fail(q1("update english.ai_runs set error_code = 'x' where id = $1", [run]), "42501");
      await fail(q1("delete from english.ai_runs where id = $1", [run]), "42501");
      await fail(q1("insert into english.ai_runs(family_id, purpose, status, cost_usd) values ($1, 'studio_extract', 'refused_cap', 0.01)", [s.familyId]), "23514");
      await fail(q1("insert into english.ai_settings(family_id, monthly_cap_usd) values ($1, 11)", [s.familyId]), "23514");
    });

    it("students read no Studio table; the other child's tutor reads nothing of this child", async () => {
      await rawDraft(s.learners.nguyen);
      for (const u of [s.users.studentAn, s.users.studentNguyen, s.users.tutorAn]) {
        for (const t of ["studio_drafts", "studio_item_meta", "accepted_additions", "item_decisions"]) {
          expect(await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", u, `select * from english.${t} where learner_id = $1`, [s.learners.nguyen]), `${t}`).toEqual([]);
        }
      }
      expect((await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.tutorNguyen, "select id from english.studio_drafts")).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect((await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.parent, "select id from english.studio_drafts")).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    it("files: .docx only for Studio kinds; old kinds keep their rules", async () => {
      const docx = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document";
      const ins = (kind: string, mime: string) => {
        const id = randomUUID();
        const ext = mime === docx ? "docx" : mime === "application/pdf" ? "pdf" : "png";
        return q1(
          "insert into english.files(id, family_id, learner_id, kind, path, mime, bytes, uploaded_by_member) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 10, $7)",
          [id, s.familyId, s.learners.nguyen, kind, `${s.familyId}/${s.learners.nguyen}/${id}.${ext}`, mime, s.members.parent],
        );
      };
      await ins("studio_test", docx);
      await ins("studio_key", "application/pdf");
      await fail(ins("answer_key", docx), "23514");
      await fail(ins("studio_test", "image/png"), "23514");
      await ins("answer_key", "image/png");
    });

    it("results: a Studio result may be a practice with a frame; paper-only items need an attempt", async () => {
      await fail(
        q1(
          `insert into english.results(learner_id, mode, context, attempt, source, title, total_items, correct, wrong_items, not_in_app_items, taken_on, recorded_by_member, recorded_role, client_id)
           values ($1, 'quick', 'home', 'first', 'practice', 't', 5, 2, '{1,2}', '{3}', current_date, $2, 'parent', $3)`,
          [s.learners.nguyen, s.members.parent, `r-${randomUUID()}`],
        ),
        "23514",
      );
    });
  });

  // (later tasks add describe blocks here)
});
```

### Task 2: Migration 0024 part 2 — who reads Studio templates, auto-check, engine input, file register (RISKY: template RLS, auto-check, engine input)

**Files:**
- Modify: `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql` (paste section 2 above `-- 5. Purge:`), `tests/unit/migration0024.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `inquiry_templates_select` (0007 L872), `eng_staff_may_read_template` / `eng_staff_may_read_source` (0007, after the purge function), `eng_compute_auto_checks` (0009 L240–308), `eng_engine_inputs` (0023 L302–402), `eng_register_file` (0016 L232–268; its revoke at L711).
- Produces (same names and signatures, new bodies):
  - policy `inquiry_templates_select`: topic → `eng_staff_has_track(track_id)` (unchanged); studio → `eng_is_staff_of(learner_id)`.
  - `eng_staff_may_read_template(text, int)`, `eng_staff_may_read_source(text, int)`: same split (keys, step sources, sources of a Studio template: adults of that child only).
  - `eng_compute_auto_checks(uuid, jsonb, jsonb)`: Studio only — `transform` / `short` with a key: blank → `blank`; equal (`eng_norm_answer`) to an alternative of the key or to an `accepted_additions` row of that template version → `correct`; else `needs_person`. `open` blank → `blank`. Weekly topics: byte-for-byte the old behaviour.
  - `eng_engine_inputs(uuid, text[])`: an auto item whose check is `needs_person` takes the result of its `item_decisions` row; without a row it is left out. Same JSON shape.
  - `eng_register_file(uuid, uuid, text, jsonb, uuid)`: `.docx` only for `studio_test` / `studio_key`; those two only PDF or `.docx`; path `….docx`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Paste section 2** into `0024_studio_b1.sql` immediately above `-- 5. Purge:`:

```sql
-- 2. Đọc mẫu Studio, tự chấm, đầu vào engine, đăng ký file ---------------------------------------------------------------------

-- 2a. Mẫu Studio (và khóa, nối nguồn, bài đọc của nó): CHỈ phụ huynh và gia sư của chính em đó (spec §7). Mẫu chủ đề tuần: như 0007.
drop policy if exists inquiry_templates_select on english.inquiry_templates;
create policy inquiry_templates_select on english.inquiry_templates
  for select to authenticated
  using (published_at is not null and ((kind = 'topic' and english.eng_staff_has_track(track_id)) or (kind = 'studio' and english.eng_is_staff_of(learner_id))));

create or replace function english.eng_staff_may_read_template(p_id text, p_version int) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select exists (
    select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
    where t.id = p_id and t.version = p_version
      and t.published_at is not null
      and ((t.kind = 'topic' and english.eng_staff_has_track(t.track_id))
           or (t.kind = 'studio' and english.eng_is_staff_of(t.learner_id))))
$$;

create or replace function english.eng_staff_may_read_source(p_id text, p_version int) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select exists (
    select 1 from english.inquiry_templates t
    where t.published_at is not null
      and t.sources @> pg_catalog.jsonb_build_array(pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('id', p_id, 'version', p_version))
      and ((t.kind = 'topic' and english.eng_staff_has_track(t.track_id))
           or (t.kind = 'studio' and english.eng_is_staff_of(t.learner_id))))
$$;
revoke execute on function english.eng_staff_may_read_template(text, int) from public, anon;
revoke execute on function english.eng_staff_may_read_source(text, int) from public, anon;
grant execute on function english.eng_staff_may_read_template(text, int) to authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function english.eng_staff_may_read_source(text, int) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 2b. Tự chấm. Nhánh của mẫu chủ đề tuần = đúng bản 0009. CHỈ mẫu Studio: transform / short có đáp án → so như gap (eng_norm_answer) với
-- đáp án (" / ") ∪ đáp án chấp nhận của mẫu (accepted_additions, áp cho lần làm SAU khi được thêm); không khớp → 'needs_person'
-- (người lớn Đúng/Sai). open: để trống → 'blank', còn lại 'needs_person'. Không bao giờ ra điểm 0–4.
create or replace function english.eng_compute_auto_checks(p_instance uuid, p_step jsonb, p_answers jsonb) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_item jsonb;
  v_id text;
  v_kind text;
  v_opts jsonb;
  v_key text;
  v_given text;
  v_res text;
  v_ki int;
  v_gi int;
  v_items jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_total int := 0;
  v_checked int := 0;
  v_correct int := 0;
  v_studio boolean := false;
  v_tpl text;
  v_ver int;
begin
  select t.kind = 'studio', t.id, t.version into v_studio, v_tpl, v_ver
  from english.inquiry_instances i
  join english.inquiry_templates t on t.id = i.template_id and t.version = i.template_version
  where i.id = p_instance;
  v_studio := coalesce(v_studio, false);

  for v_item in select x from pg_catalog.jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_step -> 'items', '[]'::jsonb)) as x loop
    v_id := v_item ->> 'id';
    v_kind := v_item ->> 'kind';
    v_opts := coalesce(v_item -> 'options', '[]'::jsonb);
    v_given := coalesce(p_answers ->> v_id, '');
    v_total := v_total + 1;
    v_res := 'needs_person';

    if v_kind in ('mcq', 'gap', 'word_form') then
      select k.answer into v_key
      from english.instance_item_keys k
      where k.inquiry_instance_id = p_instance and k.item_id = v_id and k.kind = 'answer';

      if v_key is not null and pg_catalog.btrim(v_key) <> '' then
        if pg_catalog.jsonb_array_length(v_opts) > 0 then
          v_ki := english.eng_resolve_option(v_opts, v_key);
          if v_ki is not null then
            v_gi := english.eng_resolve_option(v_opts, v_given);
            v_res := case when english.eng_clean_text(v_given) = '' then 'blank'
                          when v_gi is not null and v_gi = v_ki then 'correct'
                          else 'incorrect' end;
          end if;
        else
          v_res := case
            when english.eng_clean_text(v_given) = '' then 'blank'
            when exists (
              select 1 from pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_table(english.eng_clean_text(v_key), ' / ') as a
              where english.eng_norm_answer(a) = english.eng_norm_answer(v_given)) then 'correct'
            else 'incorrect' end;
        end if;
      end if;
    elsif v_studio and v_kind in ('transform', 'short') then
      select k.answer into v_key
      from english.instance_item_keys k
      where k.inquiry_instance_id = p_instance and k.item_id = v_id and k.kind = 'answer';
      if v_key is not null and pg_catalog.btrim(v_key) <> '' then
        v_res := case
          when english.eng_clean_text(v_given) = '' then 'blank'
          when exists (
            select 1 from pg_catalog.regexp_split_to_table(english.eng_clean_text(v_key), ' / ') as a
            where english.eng_norm_answer(a) = english.eng_norm_answer(v_given))
            or exists (
              select 1 from english.accepted_additions aa
              where aa.template_id = v_tpl and aa.template_version = v_ver and aa.item_id = v_id
                and english.eng_norm_answer(aa.answer) = english.eng_norm_answer(v_given)) then 'correct'
          else 'needs_person' end;
      end if;
    elsif v_studio and v_kind = 'open' and english.eng_clean_text(v_given) = '' then
      v_res := 'blank';
    end if;

    if v_res <> 'needs_person' then
      v_checked := v_checked + 1;
      if v_res = 'correct' then v_correct := v_correct + 1; end if;
    end if;
    v_items := v_items || pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object(v_id, pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('kind', v_kind, 'result', v_res));
  end loop;

  return pg_catalog.jsonb_build_object('version', 1, 'total', v_total, 'checked', v_checked, 'correct', v_correct, 'items', v_items);
end
$$;
revoke execute on function english.eng_compute_auto_checks(uuid, jsonb, jsonb) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

<<ENGINE INPUTS — Step 4>>

<<REGISTER FILE — Step 5>>
```
