```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildStudioBuild } from "@/lib/studio/buildTemplate";
import { normAnswer, normalizeExtract } from "@/lib/studio/normalize";
import { aiExtractSchema, type StudioDraft } from "@/lib/studio/schema";
import { sortForReview, summarizeDraft } from "@/lib/studio/summary";
import { validateDraft } from "@/lib/studio/validate";
import { CTX_B, FAKE_EXTRACT } from "./fixtures/studio";

const draft = (): StudioDraft => normalizeExtract(aiExtractSchema.parse(FAKE_EXTRACT), CTX_B);
const fixed = (): StudioDraft => {
  const d = draft();
  d.items = d.items.map((i) => (i.n === 8 ? { ...i, key: "is said to be" } : i));
  return d;
};

describe("normalizeExtract (AI → draft)", () => {
  it("keeps the fixture, fills the reading item's skill from its tag's related target, sorts by n", () => {
    const d = draft();
    expect(d.items.map((i) => i.n)).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9]);
    expect(d.items.find((i) => i.n === 5)).toMatchObject({ reading_skill_key: "doc-inference", tag_key: null, target_id: "B-T2-ASSUME-R" });
    expect(d.items.find((i) => i.n === 8)).toMatchObject({ needs_review: true, key: null });
  });

  it("repairs what the model got wrong and says why", () => {
    const raw = aiExtractSchema.parse({
      parts: [{ key: "A", name: "A", instructions: "" }],
      passages: [],
      items: [
        { n: 2, part: "A", kind: "wordform", stem: "x (HAPPY)", key: "happiness", options: [{ letter: "a", text: "x" }], tag_key: "doc-main-idea" },
        { n: 1, part: "A", kind: "other", stem: "Write.", tag_key: "nope", target_id: "B-L1-TRANSFORM-W", explanation_vi: "Được 2 điểm" },
        { n: 1, part: "A", kind: "mcq", stem: "dup" },
      ],
    });
    const d = normalizeExtract(raw, CTX_B);
    expect(d.items.map((i) => [i.n, i.kind])).toEqual([[1, "open"], [2, "word_form"]]);
    const [one, two] = d.items;
    expect(one).toMatchObject({ tag_key: null, target_id: null, explanation_vi: "", needs_review: true });
    expect(two).toMatchObject({ options: [], reading_skill_key: "doc-main-idea", tag_key: null, needs_review: true });
  });

  it("normAnswer mirrors english.eng_norm_answer", () => {
    expect(normAnswer("  Wish I Could. ")).toBe("wish i could");
    expect(normAnswer("it’s fine!!")).toBe("it's fine");
  });
});

describe("buildStudioBuild (draft → template, keys, meta, paper frame)", () => {
  it("in-app items become q<n> items without answers; paper-only items only reach meta and the frame", () => {
    const b = buildStudioBuild(fixed());
    expect(b.template.items.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(["q1", "q2", "q3", "q4", "q5", "q6", "q7", "q8"]);
    expect(b.template.items[0]).toEqual({ id: "q1", kind: "mcq", stem: "I wish I ____ more time to read.", options: ["A. have", "B. had", "C. will have", "D. am having"], target_id: null });
    expect(b.template.target_ids).toEqual(["B-L2-CLOZE", "B-T1-EXAM-R", "B-T2-ASSUME-R"]);
    expect(JSON.stringify(b.template)).not.toMatch(/"(answer|explanation_vi|text|body|transcript|prompt_vi|review_note)":/);
    expect(b.keys.find((k) => k.item_id === "q1")).toEqual({ item_id: "q1", kind: "answer", answer: "B", explanation_vi: "Sau wish (ước điều trái với hiện tại) dùng quá khứ đơn: had." });
    expect(b.keys.find((k) => k.item_id === "q8")?.answer).toBe("is said to be");
    expect(b.keys).toHaveLength(8);
    expect(b.meta).toHaveLength(9);
    expect(b.meta.find((m) => m.n === 9)).toEqual({ n: 9, part: "E", tag_key: "tu-vung-ngu-canh", reading_skill_key: null, out_of_app: true });
    expect(b.frame).toEqual({
      total: 9,
      parts: [
        { key: "A", name: "Part A — Grammar", from: 1, to: 3 },
        { key: "B", name: "Part B — Reading", from: 4, to: 5 },
        { key: "C", name: "Part C — Word formation", from: 6, to: 6 },
        { key: "D", name: "Part D — Sentence transformation", from: 7, to: 8 },
        { key: "E", name: "Part E — Picture", from: 9, to: 9 },
      ],
      items: b.meta.map((m) => ({ n: m.n, part: m.part, tag_key: m.tag_key ?? m.reading_skill_key })),
    });
    expect(b.passages.map((p) => p.ref)).toEqual(["P1"]);
    expect(b.template.time_min).toBe(8);
  });

  it("accepted answers join the key with ' / '; refuses a blocked draft", () => {
    const d = fixed();
    d.items = d.items.map((i) => (i.n === 7 ? { ...i, accepted: ["I wish I could"] } : i));
    expect(buildStudioBuild(d).keys.find((k) => k.item_id === "q7")?.answer).toBe("wish I could / I wish I could");
    expect(() => buildStudioBuild(draft())).toThrow("studio_build_blocked");
  });
});

describe("summarizeDraft / sortForReview (side panel of board 19)", () => {
  it("counts: app-checked, may need a person, counts for skills, signal only, paper only", () => {
    const d = fixed();
    expect(summarizeDraft(d, validateDraft(d, CTX_B))).toEqual({
      total: 9, inApp: 8, auto: 6, mayNeedPerson: 2, counted: 4, signalOnly: 4, paperOnly: 1, needsReview: 1, blocking: 0,
    });
  });
  it("items to look at come first", () => {
    const d = draft();
    expect(sortForReview(d.items, validateDraft(d, CTX_B)).map((i) => i.n)[0]).toBe(8);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `lib/studio/normalize.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * AI tool output → a strict draft (spec §5.3–§5.4). The model may use the spike's kind names, put a reading tag in tag_key, propose a
 * tag or skill outside the lists, add options to a non-MCQ item or write a score-like explanation: each is repaired and the item is
 * marked "cần xem" with the reason. Blocking issues of validateDraft are marked too. Pure.
 */
import { studioReviewReasons, studioIssueText } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { hasScoreWords } from "@/lib/revision/scoreWords";
import { tagOf, type StudioContext } from "./context";
import { STUDIO_LIMITS, type AiExtract, type StudioDraft, type StudioItem, type StudioItemKind, type StudioPart, type StudioPassage } from "./schema";
import { validateDraft, type Issue } from "./validate";

const KIND_ALIASES: Record<string, StudioItemKind> = {
  mcq: "mcq", gap: "gap", word_form: "word_form", wordform: "word_form", transform: "transform", short: "short", open: "open", other: "open",
};
const PART_KEY = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,20}$/;
const REF = /^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,10}$/;

/** Same rule as english.eng_norm_answer (0009): clean, drop end punctuation, lower case. */
export function normAnswer(s: string): string {
  return s
    .normalize("NFC")
    .replace(/[​-‍﻿]/g, "")
    .replace(/[   -  　]/g, " ")
    .replace(/[‘’‛]/g, "'")
    .replace(/[“”]/g, '"')
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .replace(/[.!?]+\s*$/, "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

const clip = (s: string | null | undefined, max: number) => (s ?? "").trim().slice(0, max);

export function normalizeExtract(raw: AiExtract, ctx: StudioContext): StudioDraft {
  const parts: StudioPart[] = [];
  for (const [k, p] of raw.parts.entries()) {
    const key = PART_KEY.test(p.key.trim()) ? p.key.trim() : String.fromCharCode(65 + (k % 26));
    if (parts.some((x) => x.key === key)) continue;
    parts.push({ key, name: clip(p.name, STUDIO_LIMITS.partName) || key, instructions: clip(p.instructions, STUDIO_LIMITS.instructions) });
  }
  const passages: StudioPassage[] = [];
  for (const [k, p] of raw.passages.entries()) {
    const text = clip(p.text, STUDIO_LIMITS.passageText);
    if (!text) continue;
    const ref = REF.test(p.ref.trim()) && !passages.some((x) => x.ref === p.ref.trim()) ? p.ref.trim() : `P${k + 1}`;
    passages.push({ ref, title: clip(p.title, STUDIO_LIMITS.passageTitle) || ref, text });
  }
  if (parts.length === 0) parts.push({ key: "A", name: "A", instructions: "" });
  const refs = new Set(passages.map((p) => p.ref));
  const skills = new Set(ctx.skills.map((s) => s.id));

  const seen = new Set<number>();
  const items: StudioItem[] = [];
  for (const r of [...raw.items].sort((a, b) => a.n - b.n)) {
    if (r.n < 1 || r.n > STUDIO_LIMITS.items || seen.has(r.n)) continue;
    seen.add(r.n);
    const reasons: string[] = [];
    let kind = KIND_ALIASES[r.kind.trim().toLowerCase()];
    if (!kind) {
      kind = "open";
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.kind);
    }
    let options = kind === "mcq"
      ? r.options
          .map((o) => ({ letter: o.letter.trim().toUpperCase(), text: clip(o.text, STUDIO_LIMITS.option) }))
          .filter((o) => /^[A-H]$/.test(o.letter) && o.text)
          .slice(0, STUDIO_LIMITS.options)
      : [];
    if (kind !== "mcq" && r.options.length > 0) reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.options);
    const rawKey = clip(r.key, STUDIO_LIMITS.key);
    const key = rawKey ? (kind === "mcq" && /^[a-h]$/i.test(rawKey) ? rawKey.toUpperCase() : rawKey) : null;
    const accepted = kind === "mcq"
      ? []
      : [...new Set(r.accepted.map((a) => clip(a, STUDIO_LIMITS.key)).filter((a) => a && (key === null || normAnswer(a) !== normAnswer(key))))].slice(0, STUDIO_LIMITS.accepted);

    let tagKey = r.tag_key ?? null;
    let readingKey = r.reading_skill_key ?? null;
    if (tagKey !== null && tagOf(ctx, tagKey)?.reading && readingKey === null) {
      readingKey = tagKey;
      tagKey = null;
    }
    if (tagKey !== null && readingKey !== null) {
      if (r.passage_ref) tagKey = null;
      else readingKey = null;
    }
    if (tagKey !== null && (!tagOf(ctx, tagKey) || tagOf(ctx, tagKey)?.reading)) {
      tagKey = null;
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.tag);
    }
    if (readingKey !== null && !tagOf(ctx, readingKey)?.reading) {
      readingKey = null;
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.tag);
    }
    let target = r.target_id ?? null;
    if (target !== null && !skills.has(target)) {
      target = null;
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.skill);
    }
    if (target === null && readingKey !== null) target = tagOf(ctx, readingKey)?.related.find((t) => skills.has(t)) ?? null;
    let passageRef = r.passage_ref ? r.passage_ref.trim() : null;
    if (passageRef !== null && !refs.has(passageRef)) {
      passageRef = null;
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.passage);
    }
    let explanation = clip(r.explanation_vi, STUDIO_LIMITS.explanation);
    if (hasScoreWords(explanation)) {
      explanation = "";
      reasons.push(studioReviewReasons.explanation);
    }
    const part = parts.some((p) => p.key === r.part.trim()) ? r.part.trim() : (parts[0] as StudioPart).key;
    if (kind !== "mcq") options = [];
    items.push({
      n: r.n,
      part,
      kind,
      out_of_app: r.out_of_app ?? false,
      stem: clip(r.stem, STUDIO_LIMITS.stem),
      options,
      passage_ref: passageRef,
      key,
      accepted,
      tag_key: tagKey,
      target_id: target,
      reading_skill_key: readingKey,
      explanation_vi: explanation,
      needs_review: Boolean(r.needs_review) || reasons.length > 0,
      review_reason: (clip(r.review_reason, STUDIO_LIMITS.reason) || reasons.join(" ").slice(0, STUDIO_LIMITS.reason)) || null,
    });
  }
  const draft: StudioDraft = { parts, passages, items };
  return markIssues(draft, validateDraft(draft, ctx));
}

/** Items with a blocking issue are marked "cần xem" (spec §5.4: vi phạm → needs_review + lý do). */
export function markIssues(d: StudioDraft, issues: Issue[]): StudioDraft {
  return {
    ...d,
    items: d.items.map((i) => {
      const own = issues.filter((x) => x.blocking && x.n === i.n);
      if (own.length === 0) return i;
      return { ...i, needs_review: true, review_reason: i.review_reason ?? studioIssueText[own[0]!.code] };
    }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `lib/studio/buildTemplate.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * A validated draft → what english.eng_approve_studio_draft inserts (spec §4.4, plan D3–D4): one PRACTICE step of ACT-SOURCE-QA with
 * the in-app items (ids q<n>, MCQ options as "A. text", NO answer / explanation keys — the template triggers refuse them), the key rows
 * (MCQ: the letter; other closed kinds: key and accepted answers joined by " / "; open: a sealed model answer), the item meta (one tag
 * per item), the reading texts used by in-app items, and the paper frame {total, parts with ranges, items with their tag}. Pure.
 */
import { CLOSED_STUDIO_KINDS, type StudioDraft, type StudioItem, type StudioItemKind, type StudioPassage } from "./schema";

export type BuildItem = { id: string; kind: StudioItemKind; stem: string; options: string[]; target_id: string | null };
export type BuildKey = { item_id: string; kind: "answer" | "sealed"; answer: string | null; explanation_vi: string | null };
export type BuildMeta = { n: number; part: string; tag_key: string | null; reading_skill_key: string | null; out_of_app: boolean };
export type StudioBuild = {
  template: { prompt_en: string; time_min: number; target_ids: string[]; items: BuildItem[] };
  keys: BuildKey[];
  meta: BuildMeta[];
  passages: StudioPassage[];
  frame: { total: number; parts: { key: string; name: string; from: number; to: number }[]; items: { n: number; part: string; tag_key: string }[] };
};

/** Shown above the questions when no part has instructions (exercise content, English like every prompt_en). */
const DEFAULT_PROMPT_EN = "Answer the questions.";
export const itemIdOf = (n: number) => `q${n}`;
const isClosed = (i: StudioItem) => (CLOSED_STUDIO_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(i.kind);

export function buildStudioBuild(d: StudioDraft): StudioBuild {
  const items = [...d.items].sort((a, b) => a.n - b.n);
  const app = items.filter((i) => !i.out_of_app);
  if (app.some((i) => isClosed(i) && !(i.key ?? "").trim()) || items.some((i) => (i.tag_key ?? i.reading_skill_key) === null)) {
    throw new Error("studio_build_blocked");
  }
  const usedRefs = new Set(app.map((i) => i.passage_ref).filter((r): r is string => r !== null));
  const appParts = d.parts.filter((p) => app.some((i) => i.part === p.key));
  const prompt = appParts.map((p) => (p.instructions.trim() ? `${p.name}: ${p.instructions.trim()}` : p.name)).join("\n").slice(0, 2000);
  const explanation = (i: StudioItem) => i.explanation_vi.trim() || null;

  return {
    template: {
      prompt_en: prompt || DEFAULT_PROMPT_EN,
      time_min: Math.min(120, Math.max(5, app.length)),
      target_ids: [...new Set(app.map((i) => i.target_id).filter((t): t is string => t !== null))],
      items: app.map((i) => ({
        id: itemIdOf(i.n),
        kind: i.kind,
        stem: i.stem.trim(),
        options: i.kind === "mcq" ? i.options.map((o) => `${o.letter}. ${o.text}`) : [],
        target_id: i.target_id,
      })),
    },
    keys: app.map((i) =>
      i.kind === "open"
        ? { item_id: itemIdOf(i.n), kind: "sealed" as const, answer: (i.key ?? "").trim() || null, explanation_vi: explanation(i) }
        : {
            item_id: itemIdOf(i.n),
            kind: "answer" as const,
            answer: i.kind === "mcq" ? (i.key ?? "").trim().toUpperCase() : [(i.key ?? "").trim(), ...i.accepted].join(" / "),
            explanation_vi: explanation(i),
          },
    ),
    meta: items.map((i) => ({ n: i.n, part: i.part, tag_key: i.tag_key, reading_skill_key: i.reading_skill_key, out_of_app: i.out_of_app })),
    passages: d.passages.filter((p) => usedRefs.has(p.ref)),
    frame: {
      total: items.length,
      parts: d.parts
        .filter((p) => items.some((i) => i.part === p.key))
        .map((p) => {
          const ns = items.filter((i) => i.part === p.key).map((i) => i.n);
          return { key: p.key, name: p.name, from: Math.min(...ns), to: Math.max(...ns) };
        }),
      items: items.map((i) => ({ n: i.n, part: i.part, tag_key: (i.tag_key ?? i.reading_skill_key) as string })),
    },
  };
}
```

  Note: `void isClosed` is used; `BuildKey` keeps `kind: "sealed"` for `open` because `template_item_keys_answer_needed` requires an answer only for `kind = 'answer'`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `lib/studio/summary.ts`**

```ts
/** Counts of the side panel "Bài sẽ tạo" (board 19) and the review order ("Chỉ câu cần xem" first). Pure. */
import { AUTO_STUDIO_KINDS, type StudioDraft, type StudioItem } from "./schema";
import type { Issue } from "./validate";

export type DraftSummary = {
  total: number;
  inApp: number;
  auto: number;
  mayNeedPerson: number;
  counted: number;
  signalOnly: number;
  paperOnly: number;
  needsReview: number;
  blocking: number;
};

export function summarizeDraft(d: StudioDraft, issues: Issue[]): DraftSummary {
  const app = d.items.filter((i) => !i.out_of_app);
  const flagged = new Set(issues.filter((x) => x.n !== null).map((x) => x.n as number));
  return {
    total: d.items.length,
    inApp: app.length,
    auto: app.filter((i) => (AUTO_STUDIO_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(i.kind)).length,
    mayNeedPerson: app.filter((i) => !(AUTO_STUDIO_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(i.kind)).length,
    counted: app.filter((i) => i.target_id !== null).length,
    signalOnly: app.filter((i) => i.target_id === null).length,
    paperOnly: d.items.length - app.length,
    needsReview: d.items.filter((i) => flagged.has(i.n)).length,
    blocking: issues.filter((x) => x.blocking).length,
  };
}

export function sortForReview(items: StudioItem[], issues: Issue[]): StudioItem[] {
  const flagged = new Set(issues.filter((x) => x.n !== null).map((x) => x.n as number));
  return [...items].sort((a, b) => Number(flagged.has(b.n)) - Number(flagged.has(a.n)) || a.n - b.n);
}
```

```ts
  describe("3–5. AI gate, drafts, approve, assign, work, verdicts, result, engine, purge", () => {
    const svc = async <T = unknown>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) =>
      asCommit(db.client, "service_role", null, async (q) => ((await q(sql, params)).rows[0] as { r: T }).r);
    const asStudent = async <T = unknown>(user: string, sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) =>
      asCommit(db.client, "authenticated", user, async (q) => ((await q(sql, params)).rows[0] as { r: T }).r);
    /** The review context of Nguyên, read from the database the way lib/studio/load.ts does (all tags "near"). */
    const ctxOf = async (learner: string): Promise<StudioContext> => {
      const tags = await q1<{ key: string; label: string; group_vi: string | null; related: string[] | null }>(
        `select t.key, t.label_vi as label, t.group_vi,
                (select sl.related from english.skill_leaves sl where sl.kind = 'tag' and sl.track_id = t.track_id and sl.ref_key = t.key limit 1) as related
         from english.error_tags t join english.learners l on l.track_id = t.track_id where l.id = $1 and t.retired_at is null`,
        [learner],
      );
      const skills = (await q1<{ s: { id: string; title_vi: string }[] }>("select english.eng_studio_skills($1) as s", [learner]))[0]!.s;
      return {
        tags: tags.map((t) => ({ key: t.key, label: t.label, reading: t.group_vi === "Kỹ năng đọc", near: true, related: t.related ?? [], group: t.group_vi })),
        skills: skills.map((x) => ({ id: x.id, label: x.title_vi })),
      };
    };
    let draftId = "";
    let templateId = "";
    let activityId = "";
    let attempt1 = "";

    it("AI settings: only the parent reads / changes them; enabling writes an ai_text consent for each child; the gate follows switch, consent and cap", async () => {
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_ai_settings($1) as r", [s.members.tutorNguyen]), "42501", /\(parent_only\)/);
      expect(await svc<{ allowed: boolean; reason: string }>("select english.eng_ai_gate($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen])).toMatchObject({ allowed: false, reason: "off" });
      const on = await svc<{ enabled: boolean; learners: { ai_text: boolean }[] }>("select english.eng_set_ai_settings(true, 'ai-studio-v1', $1) as r", [s.members.parent]);
      expect(on.enabled).toBe(true);
      expect(on.learners.every((l) => l.ai_text === true)).toBe(true);
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_ai_gate($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen])).toMatchObject({ allowed: true, reason: null, soft_reached: false });
      // month of Viet Nam: last month's spend does not count; ≥ $8 warns; ≥ $10 refuses
      await q1("insert into english.ai_runs(family_id, purpose, status, cost_usd, at) values ($1, 'studio_extract', 'ok', 9.5, now() - interval '40 days')", [s.familyId]);
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_ai_gate($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen])).toMatchObject({ allowed: true, soft_reached: false });
      await q1("insert into english.ai_runs(family_id, purpose, status, cost_usd) values ($1, 'studio_extract', 'ok', 8.2)", [s.familyId]);
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_ai_gate($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen])).toMatchObject({ allowed: true, soft_reached: true });
      await q1("insert into english.ai_runs(family_id, purpose, status, cost_usd) values ($1, 'studio_extract', 'ok', 1.9)", [s.familyId]);
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_ai_gate($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen])).toMatchObject({ allowed: false, reason: "cap" });
    });

    it("skills offered by Studio = current set ∩ ACT-SOURCE-QA evidence (P1)", async () => {
      const ids = async (l: string) => (await svc<{ id: string }[]>("select english.eng_studio_skills($1) as r", [l])).map((x) => x.id).sort();
      expect(await ids(s.learners.an)).toEqual(["A-T1-TEXT-L", "A-T1-TEXT-R"]);
      expect(await ids(s.learners.nguyen)).toEqual(["B-L2-CLOZE", "B-T1-ARG-R", "B-T1-EXAM-R", "B-T2-ASSUME-R", "B-T5-DATAOP"]);
    });

    it("draft: the child's tutor creates (idempotent), saves with the optimistic lock; the other child's tutor cannot", async () => {
      const file = { id: randomUUID(), mime: "application/pdf", bytes: 1234 };
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_create_studio_draft($1, 'Đề', $2::jsonb, null, true, $3, 'c-aaaaaaaa') as r", [s.learners.nguyen, JSON.stringify(file), s.members.tutorAn]), "42501");
      const made = await svc<{ id: string; status: string; replayed: boolean }>(
        "select english.eng_create_studio_draft($1, 'Destination B1 — Unit 5', $2::jsonb, null, true, $3, 'c-bbbbbbbb') as r",
        [s.learners.nguyen, JSON.stringify(file), s.members.tutorNguyen],
      );
      expect(made).toMatchObject({ status: "extracting", replayed: false });
      expect(await svc("select english.eng_create_studio_draft($1, 'Destination B1 — Unit 5', $2::jsonb, null, true, $3, 'c-bbbbbbbb') as r", [s.learners.nguyen, JSON.stringify(file), s.members.tutorNguyen])).toMatchObject({ id: made.id, replayed: true });
      draftId = made.id;
      const ctx = await ctxOf(s.learners.nguyen);
      const payload = normalizeExtract(aiExtractSchema.parse(FAKE_EXTRACT), ctx);
      expect((await svc<{ applied: boolean }>("select english.eng_finish_studio_extraction($1, $2::jsonb, null) as r", [draftId, JSON.stringify(payload)])).applied).toBe(true);
      expect((await svc<{ applied: boolean }>("select english.eng_finish_studio_extraction($1, $2::jsonb, null) as r", [draftId, JSON.stringify(payload)])).applied).toBe(false);
      const d1 = await svc<{ updated_at: string; status: string }>("select english.eng_studio_draft($1) as r", [draftId]);
      expect(d1.status).toBe("draft");
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_save_studio_draft($1, 'x', $2::jsonb, '2000-01-01 00:00:00+00', $3) as r", [draftId, JSON.stringify(payload), s.members.tutorNguyen]), "23505", /\(draft_changed\)/);
    });

    it("approve: blocked draft is fixed in TS; the template is a published Studio template of Nguyên with keys, reading text, meta and frame", async () => {
      const ctx = await ctxOf(s.learners.nguyen);
      const cur = await svc<{ payload: StudioDraft; updated_at: string }>("select english.eng_studio_draft($1) as r", [draftId]);
      expect(canApprove(validateDraft(cur.payload, ctx))).toBe(false);
      const fixed: StudioDraft = {
        ...cur.payload,
        // item 8 gets its key; item 7 counts for B-L2-CLOZE so a decided needs_person item reaches the engine (Step: engine)
        items: cur.payload.items.map((i) => (i.n === 8 ? { ...i, key: "is said to be" } : i.n === 7 ? { ...i, target_id: "B-L2-CLOZE" } : i)),
      };
      expect(canApprove(validateDraft(fixed, ctx))).toBe(true);
      const saved = await svc<{ updated_at: string }>("select english.eng_save_studio_draft($1, 'Destination B1 — Unit 5', $2::jsonb, $3, $4) as r", [draftId, JSON.stringify(fixed), cur.updated_at, s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      const build = buildStudioBuild(fixed);
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_approve_studio_draft($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4) as r", [draftId, saved.updated_at, JSON.stringify(build), s.members.tutorAn]), "42501");
      const ok = await svc<{ template_id: string; version: number; frame_id: string }>("select english.eng_approve_studio_draft($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4) as r", [draftId, saved.updated_at, JSON.stringify(build), s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      expect(ok.template_id).toMatch(/^B-ST-[0-9]{6}$/);
      expect(ok.version).toBe(1);
      templateId = ok.template_id;
      const tpl = (await q1<{ kind: string; learner_id: string; target_ids: string[]; steps: unknown; published: boolean }>(
        "select kind, learner_id, target_ids, steps, published_at is not null as published from english.inquiry_templates where id = $1", [templateId]))[0]!;
      expect(tpl).toMatchObject({ kind: "studio", learner_id: s.learners.nguyen, published: true, target_ids: ["B-L2-CLOZE", "B-T1-EXAM-R", "B-T2-ASSUME-R"] });
      expect(JSON.stringify(tpl.steps)).not.toMatch(/"(answer|explanation_vi)":/);
      expect((await q1("select 1 from english.template_item_keys where template_id = $1", [templateId])).length).toBe(8);
      expect((await q1("select 1 from english.studio_item_meta where template_id = $1", [templateId])).length).toBe(9);
      expect((await q1<{ id: string }>("select source_id as id from english.template_step_sources where template_id = $1", [templateId])).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([`ST-${templateId}-P1`]);
      expect((await q1<{ total_items: number; title: string }>("select total_items, title from english.test_frames where id = $1", [ok.frame_id]))[0]).toEqual({ total_items: 9, title: `Destination B1 — Unit 5 (${templateId})` });
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_approve_studio_draft($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4) as r", [draftId, saved.updated_at, JSON.stringify(build), s.members.tutorNguyen]), "23514");
      await fail(q1("update english.studio_drafts set title = 'x' where id = $1", [draftId]), "42501");
    });

    it("who reads a Studio template: the parent and Nguyên's tutor; never An's tutor or a student; it never shows up in 'Giao chủ đề'", async () => {
      for (const t of ["inquiry_templates", "template_item_keys", "template_step_sources", "studio_item_meta"]) {
        const col = t === "inquiry_templates" ? "id" : "template_id";
        const sql = `select 1 from english.${t} where ${col} = $1`;
        expect((await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.parent, sql, [templateId])).length, `${t} parent`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        expect((await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.tutorNguyen, sql, [templateId])).length, `${t} tutor`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
        for (const u of [s.users.tutorAn, s.users.studentNguyen, s.users.studentAn]) expect(await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", u, sql, [templateId]), t).toEqual([]);
      }
      expect(await rowsAs(db.client, "authenticated", s.users.tutorAn, "select 1 from english.sources where id like $1", [`ST-${templateId}-%`])).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("assign: start day and due day; the child sees the step from the start day, items without keys", async () => {
      const today = (await q1<{ d: string }>("select english.eng_today_vn()::text as d"))[0]!.d;
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_assign_studio($1, $2::date + 3, $2::date, $3, 'as-11111111') as r", [draftId, today, s.members.parent]), "23514", /\(start_after_due\)/);
      const a = await svc<{ activity_id: string; replayed: boolean }>("select english.eng_assign_studio($1, $2::date, $2::date + 3, $3, 'as-22222222') as r", [draftId, today, s.members.parent]);
      expect(a.replayed).toBe(false);
      expect((await svc<{ replayed: boolean }>("select english.eng_assign_studio($1, $2::date, $2::date + 3, $3, 'as-22222222') as r", [draftId, today, s.members.parent])).replayed).toBe(true);
      activityId = a.activity_id;
      const act = await asStudent<{ step: { items: Record<string, unknown>[] } }>(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_my_activity($1) as r", [activityId]);
      expect(act.step.items).toHaveLength(8);
      expect(JSON.stringify(act)).not.toMatch(/"answer":|explanation_vi|target_id|tag_key/);
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_assign_inquiry($1, $2, 1, current_date + 3, $3, 'as-33333333') as r", [s.learners.an, templateId, s.members.parent]), "23514");
    });

    it("work: app checks the closed items; transform not in the key waits for a person; no result yet; the child sees wrong + waiting, never a count", async () => {
      const answers = {
        q1: "B. had", q2: "B. can't", q3: "A. off", q4: "C. A visit that changed Lan's plans",
        q6: "happyness", q7: "I wish I could", q8: "is said to be a",
      };
      const r = await asStudent<{ attempt_id: string }>(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_submit_attempt($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4::text[], 'in_app') as r", [activityId, randomUUID(), JSON.stringify({ answers }), ["none"]]);
      attempt1 = r.attempt_id;
      const checks = (await q1<{ c: { items: Record<string, { result: string }> } }>("select checks as c from english.attempt_auto_checks where attempt_id = $1", [attempt1]))[0]!.c;
      expect(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(checks.items).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.result]))).toEqual({
        q1: "correct", q2: "incorrect", q3: "correct", q4: "correct", q5: "blank", q6: "incorrect", q7: "needs_person", q8: "needs_person",
      });
      expect(await q1("select 1 from english.results where attempt_id = $1", [attempt1])).toEqual([]);
      const review = await asStudent<{ wrong: { n: number; given: string; expected: string | null }[]; pending: number[] }>(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_my_studio_review($1) as r", [activityId]);
      expect(review.wrong.map((w) => w.n)).toEqual([2, 5, 6]);
      expect(review.wrong[0]).toMatchObject({ given: "B. can't", expected: "A. must" });
      expect(review.wrong.find((w) => w.n === 6)?.expected).toBe("happiness");
      expect(review.pending).toEqual([7, 8]);
      expect(JSON.stringify(review)).not.toMatch(/"(correct|total|checked|score|tag_key|target_id)"/);
      expect(await asStudent(s.users.studentAn, "select english.eng_my_studio_review($1) as r", [activityId])).toBeNull();
      // a weekly-topic step gives {}
      const topicStep = (await q1<{ id: string }>("select a.id from english.activity_instances a join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id where a.learner_id = $1 and i.template_id not like '%-ST-%' limit 1", [s.learners.nguyen]))[0];
      if (topicStep) expect(await asStudent(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_my_studio_review($1) as r", [topicStep.id])).toEqual({});
    });

    it("Hộp duyệt lists the waiting items for Nguyên's tutor and the parent, not for An's tutor", async () => {
      const p = await svc<{ count: number; items: { attempt_id: string; items: { n: number; expected: string; given: string }[] }[] }>("select english.eng_studio_pending($1, false) as r", [s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      expect(p.count).toBe(2);
      expect(p.items[0]?.items.map((i) => [i.n, i.expected, i.given])).toEqual([[7, "wish I could", "I wish I could"], [8, "is said to be", "is said to be a"]]);
      expect((await svc<{ count: number }>("select english.eng_studio_pending($1, false) as r", [s.members.parent])).count).toBe(2);
      expect((await svc<{ count: number }>("select english.eng_studio_pending($1, false) as r", [s.members.tutorAn])).count).toBe(0);
    });

    it("verdicts: once per item; the last one writes the result + tag marks (first attempt counts); 'Đúng' adds an accepted answer; the engine reads decided items", async () => {
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_decide_studio_items($1, $2::jsonb, $3, 'dc-00000000') as r", [attempt1, JSON.stringify([{ item_id: "q2", result: "correct" }]), s.members.tutorNguyen]), "23514", /\(not_pending_item\)/);
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_decide_studio_items($1, $2::jsonb, $3, 'dc-00000001') as r", [attempt1, JSON.stringify([{ item_id: "q7", result: "correct" }]), s.members.tutorAn]), "42501");
      const one = await svc<{ decided: number; pending: number; result: { recorded: boolean } }>("select english.eng_decide_studio_items($1, $2::jsonb, $3, 'dc-11111111') as r", [attempt1, JSON.stringify([{ item_id: "q7", result: "correct" }]), s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      expect(one).toMatchObject({ decided: 1, pending: 1, result: { recorded: false } });
      await fail(svc("select english.eng_decide_studio_items($1, $2::jsonb, $3, 'dc-22222222') as r", [attempt1, JSON.stringify([{ item_id: "q7", result: "incorrect" }]), s.members.tutorNguyen]), "23505", /\(already_decided\)/);
      const two = await svc<{ pending: number; result: { recorded: boolean; correct: number; in_app: number } }>("select english.eng_decide_studio_items($1, $2::jsonb, $3, 'dc-33333333') as r", [attempt1, JSON.stringify([{ item_id: "q8", result: "correct" }]), s.members.parent]);
      expect(two).toMatchObject({ pending: 0, result: { recorded: true, correct: 5, in_app: 8 } });
      const res = (await q1<Record<string, unknown>>("select mode, source, attempt, total_items, correct, wrong_items, not_in_app_items, recorded_role from english.results where attempt_id = $1", [attempt1]))[0];
      expect(res).toEqual({ mode: "frame", source: "practice", attempt: "first", total_items: 9, correct: 5, wrong_items: [2, 5, 6], not_in_app_items: [9], recorded_role: "parent" });
      expect((await q1<{ tag_key: string; counts: boolean }>("select tag_key, counts from english.tag_marks where source = 'practice' and learner_id = $1 order by item_no", [s.learners.nguyen]))).toEqual([
        { tag_key: "modal-suy-doan", counts: true }, { tag_key: "doc-inference", counts: true }, { tag_key: "dang-tu", counts: true },
      ]);
      expect((await q1<{ answer: string }>("select answer from english.accepted_additions where template_id = $1 order by item_id", [templateId])).map((r) => r.answer)).toEqual(["I wish I could", "is said to be a"]);
      // the engine input carries the decided q7 for B-L2-CLOZE, in the same JSON shape
      const inputs = await svc<{ auto: { attempt_id: string; items: { item_id: string; result: string }[] }[] }>("select english.eng_engine_inputs($1, array['B-L2-CLOZE']) as r", [s.learners.nguyen]);
      expect(inputs.auto.find((a) => a.attempt_id === attempt1)?.items).toEqual([
        { item_id: "q3", target_key: "B-L2-CLOZE", result: "correct" },
        { item_id: "q6", target_key: "B-L2-CLOZE", result: "incorrect" },
        { item_id: "q7", target_key: "B-L2-CLOZE", result: "correct" },
      ]);
      const r = await recomputeLearner(pgEngineStore(db.client), s.learners.nguyen, null);
      expect(r.results.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      // the child now sees only the wrong items, nothing waits
      const review = await asStudent<{ wrong: { n: number }[]; pending: number[] }>(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_my_studio_review($1) as r", [activityId]);
      expect(review).toMatchObject({ pending: [] });
      expect(review.wrong.map((w) => w.n)).toEqual([2, 5, 6]);
    });

    it("retry: accepted answers check the next attempt at once; the result is written at submit, as a retry that does not count", async () => {
      const answers = { q1: "B. had", q2: "A. must", q3: "A. off", q4: "C. A visit that changed Lan's plans", q5: "B. The robot room was interesting", q6: "happiness", q7: "I wish I could", q8: "is said to be a" };
      const r = await asStudent<{ attempt_id: string }>(s.users.studentNguyen, "select english.eng_submit_attempt($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4::text[], 'in_app') as r", [activityId, randomUUID(), JSON.stringify({ answers }), ["none"]]);
      const res = (await q1<Record<string, unknown>>("select attempt, correct, wrong_items from english.results where attempt_id = $1", [r.attempt_id]))[0];
      expect(res).toEqual({ attempt: "retry", correct: 8, wrong_items: [] });
      expect(await q1("select 1 from english.app_errors where code = 'studio_result'")).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("edit after approval → a new version of the same template; the assigned work keeps version 1", async () => {
      const e = await svc<{ id: string; replayed: boolean }>("select english.eng_edit_studio_draft($1, $2, 'ed-11111111') as r", [draftId, s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      expect((await svc<{ id: string }>("select english.eng_edit_studio_draft($1, $2, 'ed-22222222') as r", [draftId, s.members.parent])).id).toBe(e.id);
      const cur = await svc<{ payload: StudioDraft; updated_at: string }>("select english.eng_studio_draft($1) as r", [e.id]);
      const ok = await svc<{ template_id: string; version: number }>("select english.eng_approve_studio_draft($1, $2, $3::jsonb, $4) as r", [e.id, cur.updated_at, JSON.stringify(buildStudioBuild(cur.payload)), s.members.tutorNguyen]);
      expect(ok).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ template_id: templateId, version: 2 }));
      expect((await q1<{ v: number }>("select template_version as v from english.inquiry_instances where template_id = $1", [templateId])).map((x) => x.v)).toEqual([1]);
    });

    it("purge of Nguyên removes every Studio row (templates included), keeps An, keeps the AI cost ledger; weekly topics stay protected", async () => {
      const preview = await svc<{ counts: Record<string, number> }>("select english.eng_purge_preview($1) as r", [s.learners.nguyen]);
      expect(preview.counts).toMatchObject({ studio_templates: 2, studio_drafts: 4, item_decisions: 2, accepted_additions: 2, studio_item_meta: 18 });
      const runsBefore = (await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.ai_runs"))[0]!.n;
      const receipt = await svc<{ deleted: Record<string, number> }>("select english.eng_purge_learner($1, null) as r", [s.learners.nguyen]);
      expect(receipt.deleted).toMatchObject({ studio_templates: 2, studio_sources: 2, studio_template_keys: 16, studio_step_sources: 2, studio_drafts: 4 });
      for (const sql of [
        "select 1 from english.inquiry_templates where kind = 'studio'",
        "select 1 from english.sources where id like 'ST-%'",
        "select 1 from english.studio_drafts",
        "select 1 from english.item_decisions",
      ]) expect(await q1(sql), sql).toEqual([]);
      expect((await q1<{ n: number }>("select count(*)::int as n from english.ai_runs"))[0]!.n).toBe(runsBefore);
      expect((await q1("select 1 from english.learners where id = $1", [s.learners.an])).length).toBe(1);
      await fail(q1("delete from english.inquiry_templates where id = 'A-W1-VOLCANOES'"), "42501");
    });
  });
```

  `studio_drafts: 4` = the two raw drafts of section 1 + the approved draft + its edit; `studio_item_meta: 18` = 9 items × 2 versions; `studio_template_keys: 16` = 8 × 2; `studio_sources` / `studio_step_sources: 2` = one reading text per version. If the edit test's approve fails because the copied payload of version 2 still has the item 7 skill set for the engine test, keep it (it is allowed); do not change the expected numbers without reason.

### Task 8: Word files — storage rules, download, .docx → text

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/studio/docx.ts`, `tests/unit/fixtures/docx.ts`, `tests/unit/studioDocx.test.ts`
- Modify: `lib/storage/rules.ts`, `lib/storage/types.ts`, `lib/storage/server.ts`, `tests/unit/storageRules.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mammoth.extractRawText({ buffer })`; Supabase Storage `download(path)`.
- Produces:
  - `rules.ts`: `DOCX_MIME`, `STUDIO_MIMES = ["application/pdf", DOCX_MIME]`, `ALLOWED_MIMES` gains `DOCX_MIME`, `FILE_KINDS` gains `"studio_test"`, `"studio_key"`, `extensionFor(DOCX_MIME) === "docx"`, `checkFile`: `.docx` only for the two Studio kinds; Studio kinds only PDF or `.docx`; `PATH_RE` accepts `.docx`.
  - `types.ts`: `export interface FileDownloader { download(path: string): Promise<Uint8Array> }`.
  - `server.ts`: `createFileStorage()` returns `FileStorage & FileDownloader`.
  - `docx.ts`: `class DocxError extends Error { code: "docx_unreadable" | "docx_empty" }`, `docxToText(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<string>` (no `server-only` import so unit tests can load it; only server code imports it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests.** Append to `tests/unit/storageRules.test.ts`:

```ts
describe("Studio files (B1): PDF or Word .docx, only for the test and its answer key", () => {
  const DOCX = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document";
  it("accepts .docx only for studio_test / studio_key; Studio kinds refuse images", () => {
    expect(checkFile("studio_test", DOCX, 10)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(checkFile("studio_key", "application/pdf", 10)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(checkFile("studio_test", "image/png", 10)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "kind" });
    expect(checkFile("answer_key", DOCX, 10)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "kind" });
    expect(checkFile("work_photo", DOCX, 10)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "kind" });
    expect(checkFile("studio_test", "application/msword", 10)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "type" });
    expect(checkFile("studio_test", DOCX, MAX_FILE_BYTES + 1)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "too_big" });
  });
  it("paths end in .docx and parse back", () => {
    const p = buildPath({ familyId: "e0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001", learnerId: "e0000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000b001", fileId: "e0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000f1", mime: DOCX });
    expect(p.endsWith(".docx")).toBe(true);
    expect(parsePath(p)?.ext).toBe("docx");
  });
});
```

  Create `tests/unit/fixtures/docx.ts` — a minimal Word file (stored zip, no compression) so tests need no binary fixture:

```ts
import { crc32 } from "node:zlib";

const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");

/** A tiny valid .docx with one paragraph per string (zip entries stored, CRC from node:zlib, Node ≥ 20.15). */
export function makeDocx(paragraphs: string[]): Buffer {
  const files: [string, string][] = [
    [
      "[Content_Types].xml",
      '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/></Types>',
    ],
    [
      "_rels/.rels",
      '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/></Relationships>',
    ],
    [
      "word/document.xml",
      `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"><w:body>${paragraphs
        .map((p) => `<w:p><w:r><w:t xml:space="preserve">${esc(p)}</w:t></w:r></w:p>`)
        .join("")}</w:body></w:document>`,
    ],
  ];
  const locals: Buffer[] = [];
  const centrals: Buffer[] = [];
  let offset = 0;
  for (const [name, text] of files) {
    const data = Buffer.from(text, "utf8");
    const nameBuf = Buffer.from(name, "utf8");
    const crc = crc32(data);
    const local = Buffer.alloc(30);
    local.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0);
    local.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
    local.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14);
    local.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 18);
    local.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 22);
    local.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 26);
    locals.push(local, nameBuf, data);
    const central = Buffer.alloc(46);
    central.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0);
    central.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
    central.writeUInt16LE(20, 6);
    central.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16);
    central.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 20);
    central.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 24);
    central.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 28);
    central.writeUInt32LE(offset, 42);
    centrals.push(central, nameBuf);
    offset += 30 + nameBuf.length + data.length;
  }
  const centralBuf = Buffer.concat(centrals);
  const end = Buffer.alloc(22);
  end.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0);
  end.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 8);
  end.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 10);
  end.writeUInt32LE(centralBuf.length, 12);
  end.writeUInt32LE(offset, 16);
  return Buffer.concat([...locals, centralBuf, end]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `lib/studio/docx.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * A tutor's Word test or answer key (.docx) → plain text for the AI (spec §5.2). mammoth (BSD-2-Clause) reads the document XML; images,
 * tables of contents and styles are dropped. Imported only by server code (lib/studio/extract.ts); no server-only marker so unit tests can
 * load it. Never logs the text.
 */
import mammoth from "mammoth";

export class DocxError extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly code: "docx_unreadable" | "docx_empty") {
    super(code);
    this.name = "DocxError";
  }
}

/** At most this much text goes to the AI (a 60-item test is ~15 000 characters). */
export const MAX_DOCX_TEXT = 200_000;

export async function docxToText(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<string> {
  let value: string;
  try {
    ({ value } = await mammoth.extractRawText({ buffer: Buffer.from(bytes) }));
  } catch {
    throw new DocxError("docx_unreadable");
  }
  const text = value.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n").trim();
  if (!text) throw new DocxError("docx_empty");
  return text.slice(0, MAX_DOCX_TEXT);
}
```

  If TypeScript complains about the default import, use `import * as mammoth from "mammoth";` (both work with the package's own types).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { gateDecision, refusalStatus, vnMonthStart } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { estimateCostUsd, FALLBACK_PRICE, MODEL_PRICES } from "@/lib/ai/pricing";

describe("pricing (estimate, one table)", () => {
  it("cost = tokens × price per million; unknown models use the most expensive row", () => {
    const p = MODEL_PRICES["claude-sonnet-5-5"]!;
    expect(estimateCostUsd("claude-sonnet-5-5", 1_000_000, 0)).toBe(p.inputPerMTok);
    expect(estimateCostUsd("claude-sonnet-5-5", 20_000, 8_000)).toBeCloseTo((20_000 * p.inputPerMTok + 8_000 * p.outputPerMTok) / 1e6, 6);
    expect(estimateCostUsd("some-new-model", 0, 1_000_000)).toBe(FALLBACK_PRICE.outputPerMTok);
  });
});

describe("cap (spec §5.5)", () => {
  const base = { enabled: true, consent: true, spentUsd: 0, monthlyCapUsd: 10, softCapUsd: 8 };
  it("off, no consent, at the cap → refused with a reason; soft cap only warns", () => {
    expect(gateDecision({ ...base, enabled: false })).toEqual({ allowed: false, reason: "off", softReached: false });
    expect(gateDecision({ ...base, consent: false })).toEqual({ allowed: false, reason: "no_consent", softReached: false });
    expect(gateDecision({ ...base, spentUsd: 8 })).toEqual({ allowed: true, reason: null, softReached: true });
    expect(gateDecision({ ...base, spentUsd: 9.999 })).toMatchObject({ allowed: true });
    expect(gateDecision({ ...base, spentUsd: 10 })).toEqual({ allowed: false, reason: "cap", softReached: true });
    expect(refusalStatus("cap")).toBe("refused_cap");
    expect(refusalStatus("off")).toBe("refused_off");
    expect(refusalStatus("no_consent")).toBe("refused_off");
  });
  it("the month starts at 00:00 in Viet Nam (UTC+7)", () => {
    expect(vnMonthStart(new Date("2026-11-30T17:30:00Z")).toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-30T17:00:00.000Z"); // 1 Dec 00:30 VN
    expect(vnMonthStart(new Date("2026-11-30T16:59:59Z")).toISOString()).toBe("2026-10-31T17:00:00.000Z"); // still 30 Nov VN
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { AiCallError, buildRequestBody, postToolCall, type ToolCall } from "@/lib/ai/request";

const call: ToolCall = {
  model: "claude-sonnet-5-5",
  documents: [{ kind: "pdf", title: "ĐỀ", base64: "JVBERi0=" }, { kind: "text", title: "ĐÁP ÁN", text: "1B" }],
  instructions: "Gọi save_exercise đúng một lần.",
  tool: { name: "save_exercise", description: "d", input_schema: { type: "object" } },
  maxTokens: 32000,
  toolChoice: "tool",
};
const reply = (status: number, body: unknown) => async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });

describe("Messages API request (server only)", () => {
  it("documents first, then the instructions; forced tool or auto", () => {
    const b = buildRequestBody(call) as { tool_choice: unknown; messages: { content: { type: string; source?: { type: string } }[] }[] };
    expect(b.tool_choice).toEqual({ type: "tool", name: "save_exercise" });
    expect(b.messages[0]!.content.map((c) => [c.type, c.source?.type])).toEqual([["document", "base64"], ["document", "text"], ["text", undefined]]);
    expect((buildRequestBody({ ...call, toolChoice: "auto" }) as { tool_choice: unknown }).tool_choice).toEqual({ type: "auto" });
  });
  it("returns the tool input + usage; maps errors to codes without leaking the key", async () => {
    const ok = await postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "k", fetchImpl: reply(200, { model: "claude-sonnet-5-5", usage: { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 5 }, content: [{ type: "tool_use", name: "save_exercise", input: { items: [] } }] }) });
    expect(ok).toEqual({ input: { items: [] }, usage: { inputTokens: 10, outputTokens: 5 }, model: "claude-sonnet-5-5" });
    await expect(postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "secret-key", fetchImpl: reply(200, { usage: {}, content: [{ type: "text", text: "hi" }] }) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "no_tool" });
    await expect(postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "k", fetchImpl: reply(400, { error: { message: "tool_choice is not supported" } }) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "tool_choice" });
    await expect(postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "k", fetchImpl: reply(429, {}) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "http_429" });
    await expect(postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "k", fetchImpl: reply(529, {}) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "http_5xx" });
    const err = await postToolCall(call, { apiKey: "secret-key", fetchImpl: async () => { throw new Error("boom secret-key"); } }).catch((e: unknown) => e as AiCallError);
    expect(err.code).toBe("network");
    expect(err.message).not.toContain("secret-key");
  });
});

describe("config + prompt", () => {

    expect(studioModel({})).toBe("claude-sonnet-5-5");
    expect(studioModel({ STUDIO_EXTRACT_MODEL: "x" })).toBe("x");
    expect(studioToolChoice({})).toBe("tool");
    expect(studioToolChoice({ STUDIO_TOOL_CHOICE: "auto" })).toBe("auto");
    expect(aiFake({ AI_FAKE: "1" })).toBe(true);
    expect(aiFake({ AI_FAKE: "1", VERCEL_ENV: "production" })).toBe(false);

  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `lib/ai/pricing.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * USD per million tokens, ONE place (spec §5.6). These are ESTIMATES (plan P7): check the Anthropic pricing page / Console before merging
 * and after any model change. An unknown model is priced with the most expensive row, so the monthly cap errs on the safe side.
 */
export type ModelPrice = { inputPerMTok: number; outputPerMTok: number };

export const MODEL_PRICES: Record<string, ModelPrice> = {
  "claude-sonnet-5-5": { inputPerMTok: 3, outputPerMTok: 15 },
};

export const FALLBACK_PRICE: ModelPrice = Object.values(MODEL_PRICES).reduce((a, b) => (b.outputPerMTok > a.outputPerMTok ? b : a));

export function estimateCostUsd(model: string, inputTokens: number, outputTokens: number): number {
  const p = MODEL_PRICES[model] ?? FALLBACK_PRICE;
  return Math.round(((inputTokens * p.inputPerMTok + outputTokens * p.outputPerMTok) / 1e6) * 1e6) / 1e6;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `lib/ai/cap.ts`**

```ts
/** The AI gate of one child (spec §5.5) as the database computes it (english.eng_ai_gate), typed; plus the same rule in TS for tests. Pure. */
import { z } from "zod";

export type GateReason = "off" | "no_consent" | "cap";
export type GateState = {
  familyId: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  consent: boolean;
  spentUsd: number;
  monthlyCapUsd: number;
  softCapUsd: number;
  softReached: boolean;
  allowed: boolean;
  reason: GateReason | null;
};

const num = z.union([z.number(), z.string()]).transform(Number);
const gateRow = z.object({
  family_id: z.string(),
  enabled: z.boolean(),
  consent: z.boolean(),
  spent_usd: num,
  monthly_cap_usd: num,
  soft_cap_usd: num,
  soft_reached: z.boolean(),
  allowed: z.boolean(),
  reason: z.enum(["off", "no_consent", "cap"]).nullable(),
});

export function parseGate(raw: unknown): GateState {
  const g = gateRow.parse(raw);
  return {
    familyId: g.family_id, enabled: g.enabled, consent: g.consent, spentUsd: g.spent_usd, monthlyCapUsd: g.monthly_cap_usd,
    softCapUsd: g.soft_cap_usd, softReached: g.soft_reached, allowed: g.allowed, reason: g.reason,
  };
}

/** Mirror of eng_ai_gate: switch, then consent, then the cap; the soft cap only warns. */
export function gateDecision(s: { enabled: boolean; consent: boolean; spentUsd: number; monthlyCapUsd: number; softCapUsd: number }) {
  const reason: GateReason | null = !s.enabled ? "off" : !s.consent ? "no_consent" : s.spentUsd >= s.monthlyCapUsd ? "cap" : null;
  return { allowed: reason === null, reason, softReached: s.spentUsd >= s.softCapUsd };
}

export function refusalStatus(reason: GateReason): "refused_cap" | "refused_off" {
  return reason === "cap" ? "refused_cap" : "refused_off";
}

/** First instant of the Vietnamese calendar month (UTC+7, no daylight saving) that contains `now`. */
export function vnMonthStart(now: Date): Date {
  const vn = new Date(now.getTime() + 7 * 3600_000);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(vn.getUTCFullYear(), vn.getUTCMonth(), 1) - 7 * 3600_000);
}
```

```ts
// lib/ai/config.ts
/** AI settings read from the environment (no secret here). Pure: tests pass their own env. */
export const DEFAULT_STUDIO_MODEL = "claude-sonnet-5-5";
export const AI_FAKE_FIXTURE = "tests/fixtures/studio/fake-extract.json";
/** Version of the short consent text on /settings/ai (consents.notice_version). Change the text → new version. */
export const AI_NOTICE_VERSION = "ai-studio-v1";
type Env = Record<string, string | undefined>;

export function studioModel(env: Env = process.env): string {
  return env.STUDIO_EXTRACT_MODEL?.trim() || DEFAULT_STUDIO_MODEL;
}
export function studioToolChoice(env: Env = process.env): "tool" | "auto" {
  return env.STUDIO_TOOL_CHOICE === "auto" ? "auto" : "tool";
}
/** AI_FAKE=1 → the fixture instead of the network (tests, CI). Never in Vercel production. */
export function aiFake(env: Env = process.env): boolean {
  return env.AI_FAKE === "1" && env.VERCEL_ENV !== "production";
}
```

```ts
// lib/ai/request.ts
/**
 * One call of the Anthropic Messages API with ONE tool (spec §5.1–§5.3). Pure apart from `fetch` (injectable): the API key is a parameter,
 * never logged, never part of an error. lib/ai/anthropic.ts (server-only) supplies the key.
 */
export const ANTHROPIC_URL = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages";
export const ANTHROPIC_VERSION = "2023-06-01";

export type AiDocument = { kind: "pdf"; title: string; base64: string } | { kind: "text"; title: string; text: string };
export type ToolSpec = { name: string; description: string; input_schema: Record<string, unknown> };
export type ToolCall = { model: string; documents: AiDocument[]; instructions: string; tool: ToolSpec; maxTokens: number; toolChoice: "tool" | "auto" };
export type Usage = { inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number };
export type ToolResult = { input: unknown; usage: Usage; model: string };
export type AiErrorCode = "http_400" | "tool_choice" | "http_401" | "http_429" | "http_5xx" | "network" | "timeout" | "no_tool";

export class AiCallError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: AiErrorCode,
    public readonly usage: Usage = { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0 },
  ) {
    super(`ai call failed: ${code}`);
    this.name = "AiCallError";
  }
}

export function buildRequestBody(c: ToolCall): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    model: c.model,
    max_tokens: c.maxTokens,
    tools: [c.tool],
    tool_choice: c.toolChoice === "tool" ? { type: "tool", name: c.tool.name } : { type: "auto" },
    messages: [
      {
        role: "user",
        content: [
          ...c.documents.map((d) =>
            d.kind === "pdf"
              ? { type: "document", title: d.title, source: { type: "base64", media_type: "application/pdf", data: d.base64 } }
              : { type: "document", title: d.title, source: { type: "text", media_type: "text/plain", data: d.text } },
          ),
          { type: "text", text: c.instructions },
        ],
      },
    ],
  };
}

type ApiBody = { model?: string; usage?: { input_tokens?: number; output_tokens?: number }; content?: { type: string; name?: string; input?: unknown }[]; error?: unknown };

export async function postToolCall(c: ToolCall, opts: { apiKey: string; fetchImpl?: typeof fetch; timeoutMs?: number }): Promise<ToolResult> {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), opts.timeoutMs ?? 240_000);
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await (opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch)(ANTHROPIC_URL, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "x-api-key": opts.apiKey, "anthropic-version": ANTHROPIC_VERSION, "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(buildRequestBody(c)),
      signal: ctrl.signal,
    });
  } catch {
    throw new AiCallError(ctrl.signal.aborted ? "timeout" : "network");
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
  const body = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as ApiBody | null;
  const usage: Usage = { inputTokens: Number(body?.usage?.input_tokens ?? 0) || 0, outputTokens: Number(body?.usage?.output_tokens ?? 0) || 0 };
  if (!res.ok) {
    const msg = JSON.stringify(body?.error ?? "").toLowerCase();
    if (res.status === 400 && msg.includes("tool_choice")) throw new AiCallError("tool_choice", usage);
    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) throw new AiCallError("http_401", usage);
    if (res.status === 429) throw new AiCallError("http_429", usage);
    throw new AiCallError(res.status >= 500 ? "http_5xx" : "http_400", usage);
  }
  const use = body?.content?.find((x) => x.type === "tool_use" && x.name === c.tool.name);
  if (!use) throw new AiCallError("no_tool", usage);
  return { input: use.input, usage, model: String(body?.model ?? c.model) };
}
```

```ts
// lib/ai/anthropic.ts
import "server-only";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { AI_FAKE_FIXTURE, aiFake } from "./config";
import { AiCallError, postToolCall, type ToolCall, type ToolResult } from "./request";

/**
 * The only place that reads ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (Vercel env, server only). With AI_FAKE=1 (tests, CI) the fixture comes back and nothing
 * leaves the machine. Without a key every call fails with http_401 (the draft then offers "Soạn tay").
 */
export function createToolCaller(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): (c: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResult> {
  if (aiFake(env)) {
    return async (c) => ({
      input: JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), AI_FAKE_FIXTURE), "utf8")) as unknown,
      usage: { inputTokens: 1000, outputTokens: 500 },
      model: c.model,
    });
  }
  const key = env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY;
  if (!key) return async () => Promise.reject(new AiCallError("http_401"));
  return (c) => postToolCall(c, { apiKey: key });
}
```

```ts
// lib/ai/prompts.ts
/** Versioned prompts live in prompts/<name>.v<N>.md (DESIGN §11): changing the wording = a new file + a new version string. */
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

export const STUDIO_PROMPT = { file: "studio-extract.v1", version: "studio-extract.v1" } as const;

export function loadPrompt(file: string, root: string = process.cwd()): string {
  if (!/^[a-z0-9-]+\.v[0-9]+$/.test(file)) throw new Error("loadPrompt: bad prompt name");
  return readFileSync(path.join(root, "prompts", `${file}.md`), "utf8");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The prompt** — `prompts/studio-extract.v1.md`

```markdown
Bạn giúp thầy/cô dạy tiếng Anh biến MỘT đề kiểm tra và ĐÁP ÁN của thầy/cô thành bài làm trên app cho một học sinh.
Bạn chỉ đọc hai tài liệu đính kèm ("ĐỀ" và "ĐÁP ÁN của thầy/cô"). Không có thông tin nào về học sinh, và bạn không cần thông tin đó.

Quy tắc:
1. Trích ĐỦ mọi câu có số in trên đề, kể cả câu nằm trong đoạn văn (vd. "(13)"). `n` = đúng số in trên đề; không tự đánh số lại.
   Giữ đúng phần (Part A, B, …): `parts[].key` là chữ / số của phần, `name` là tên phần, `instructions` là lời dặn của phần (tiếng Anh như trên đề).
2. `kind`: mcq = chọn A/B/C/D; gap = điền một từ / cụm ngắn (open cloze, phrasal verbs, idioms); word_form = dạng từ từ chữ IN HOA;
   transform = viết lại câu với từ cho sẵn; short = trả lời ngắn có đáp án; open = câu mở (viết tự do).
3. `out_of_app = true` cho câu không làm được bằng chữ trên app: phần nghe (cần âm thanh), câu dựa vào hình / bản đồ / bảng chỉ có ở dạng ảnh, nói.
4. Bài đọc dùng cho nhiều câu → `passages[]` (`ref` P1, P2…, `title`, `text` = nguyên văn bài đọc). Câu thuộc bài đọc ghi `passage_ref`.
   Đoạn văn điền từ (cloze) cũng là một bài đọc; `stem` của mỗi câu là câu có chỗ trống (dùng ____).
5. `options`: chỉ cho mcq, `letter` A–H và `text` (không lặp lại chữ cái trong `text`).
6. `key` LẤY TỪ ĐÁP ÁN CỦA THẦY/CÔ — không tự giải, không đoán. mcq: chỉ chữ cái. Câu khác: đúng chữ trong đáp án.
   Đáp án ghi nhiều cách đúng (vd. "chấp nhận X", "X / Y") → cách đầu vào `key`, các cách còn lại vào `accepted`.
   Không thấy đáp án, đáp án lệch đề, hoặc chữ khó đọc → `key = null`, `needs_review = true`, `review_reason` nói rõ (tiếng Việt, ngắn).
7. Nhãn: mỗi câu đúng MỘT nhãn.
   - Câu hỏi về bài đọc (ý chính, chi tiết, suy luận, từ trong ngữ cảnh…) → `reading_skill_key` từ danh sách KỸ NĂNG ĐỌC, `tag_key = null`.
   - Câu khác → `tag_key` từ danh sách NHÃN, `reading_skill_key = null`.
   - Không có nhãn nào hợp → chọn nhãn gần nhất và đặt `needs_review = true`.
8. `target_id`: chỉ chọn trong danh sách KỸ NĂNG khi câu đo đúng kỹ năng đó; không chắc → `null`.
9. `explanation_vi`: MỘT câu tiếng Việt ≤ 25 chữ, dễ hiểu với học sinh lớp 6–9, nói vì sao đáp án đúng. Không nhắc điểm, không "x/y", không %.
10. `needs_review = true` cả khi: có nhiều đáp án hợp lý, câu viết lại có nhiều cách đúng, hoặc bạn không chắc phần / loại câu.
11. Gọi tool `save_exercise` ĐÚNG MỘT LẦN với toàn bộ đề.

NHÃN (key — tên):
{{TAGS}}

KỸ NĂNG ĐỌC (key — tên):
{{READING_TAGS}}

KỸ NĂNG (mã — tên):
{{SKILLS}}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `lib/studio/extractTool.ts`**

```ts
/** The tool `save_exercise` (forced, spec §5.3) and the request of one extraction: the two documents + the lists + the prompt. Pure. */
import type { AiDocument, ToolCall, ToolSpec } from "@/lib/ai/request";
import { aiTagList, type StudioContext } from "./context";
import { STUDIO_ITEM_KINDS } from "./schema";

export const TEST_DOC_TITLE = "ĐỀ";
export const KEY_DOC_TITLE = "ĐÁP ÁN của thầy/cô";
export const EXTRACT_MAX_TOKENS = 32000;

const nullableEnum = (values: string[]) => ({ type: ["string", "null"], enum: [...values, null] });

export function saveExerciseTool(ctx: StudioContext): ToolSpec {
  const tags = aiTagList(ctx);
  const str = { type: "string" };
  return {
    name: "save_exercise",
    description: "Save the structured exercise extracted from the test and the tutor's answer key.",
    input_schema: {
      type: "object",
      required: ["parts", "passages", "items"],
      properties: {
        title: str,
        parts: { type: "array", items: { type: "object", required: ["key", "name", "instructions"], properties: { key: str, name: str, instructions: str } } },
        passages: { type: "array", items: { type: "object", required: ["ref", "title", "text"], properties: { ref: str, title: str, text: str } } },
        items: {
          type: "array",
          items: {
            type: "object",
            required: ["n", "part", "kind", "out_of_app", "stem", "options", "passage_ref", "key", "accepted", "tag_key", "target_id", "reading_skill_key", "explanation_vi", "needs_review", "review_reason"],
            properties: {
              n: { type: "integer", description: "The number printed on the test (never renumber)." },
              part: str,
              kind: { type: "string", enum: [...STUDIO_ITEM_KINDS] },
              out_of_app: { type: "boolean" },
              stem: str,
              options: { type: "array", items: { type: "object", required: ["letter", "text"], properties: { letter: str, text: str } } },
              passage_ref: { type: ["string", "null"] },
              key: { type: ["string", "null"], description: "From the tutor's answer key only: the option letter for mcq, else the exact words." },
              accepted: { type: "array", items: str },
              tag_key: nullableEnum(tags.filter((t) => !t.reading).map((t) => t.key)),
              target_id: nullableEnum(ctx.skills.map((s) => s.id)),
              reading_skill_key: nullableEnum(tags.filter((t) => t.reading).map((t) => t.key)),
              explanation_vi: str,
              needs_review: { type: "boolean" },
              review_reason: { type: ["string", "null"] },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  };
}

/** The prompt with the child's lists in place: keys and names only — nothing about the child (spec §5.2). */
export function fillPrompt(template: string, ctx: StudioContext): string {
  const tags = aiTagList(ctx);
  const lines = (xs: { a: string; b: string }[]) => (xs.length ? xs.map((x) => `- ${x.a} — ${x.b}`).join("\n") : "- (không có)");
  return template
    .replace("{{TAGS}}", lines(tags.filter((t) => !t.reading).map((t) => ({ a: t.key, b: t.label }))))
    .replace("{{READING_TAGS}}", lines(tags.filter((t) => t.reading).map((t) => ({ a: t.key, b: t.label }))))
    .replace("{{SKILLS}}", lines(ctx.skills.map((s) => ({ a: s.id, b: s.label }))));
}

export function buildExtractCall(o: { model: string; toolChoice: "tool" | "auto"; prompt: string; ctx: StudioContext; docs: AiDocument[] }): ToolCall {
  return { model: o.model, documents: o.docs, instructions: fillPrompt(o.prompt, o.ctx), tool: saveExerciseTool(o.ctx), maxTokens: EXTRACT_MAX_TOKENS, toolChoice: o.toolChoice };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: `lib/studio/extract.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * The background job of "Cho AI đọc đề" (spec §3 step 2, §5): check the gate again, read the files (PDF as a document, .docx as text),
 * call the AI at most 3 times (logging EVERY call in ai_runs with its estimated cost), normalise the answer into a draft and save it — or
 * mark the draft ai_failed with the last error code so the screen offers "Soạn tay". Never sends anything about the child. Framework-free.
 */
import { refusalStatus, type GateState } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { estimateCostUsd } from "@/lib/ai/pricing";
import { AiCallError, type AiDocument, type ToolCall, type ToolResult, type Usage } from "@/lib/ai/request";
import type { StudioContext } from "./context";
import { DocxError } from "./docx";
import { buildExtractCall, KEY_DOC_TITLE, TEST_DOC_TITLE } from "./extractTool";
import { normalizeExtract } from "./normalize";
import { aiExtractSchema, type StudioDraft } from "./schema";
import { DOCX_MIME } from "@/lib/storage/rules";

export type RunLog = {
  familyId: string; draftId: string | null; model: string | null; promptVersion: string | null; inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number;
  costUsd: number; status: "ok" | "error" | "refused_cap" | "refused_off"; errorCode: string | null; byMemberId: string;
};
export interface ExtractStore {
  gate(learnerId: string): Promise<GateState>;
  logRun(r: RunLog): Promise<string>;
  finish(draftId: string, payload: StudioDraft, runId: string): Promise<{ applied: boolean }>;
  fail(draftId: string, errorCode: string, runId: string | null): Promise<{ applied: boolean }>;
}
export type StoredFile = { path: string; mime: string };
export type ExtractJob = { draftId: string; familyId: string; learnerId: string; byMemberId: string; testFile: StoredFile; keyFile: StoredFile | null };
export type ExtractDeps = {
  store: ExtractStore;
  download: (path: string) => Promise<Uint8Array>;
  docxToText: (bytes: Uint8Array) => Promise<string>;
  context: () => Promise<StudioContext>;
  call: (c: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResult>;
  model: string;
  toolChoice: "tool" | "auto";
  prompt: { text: string; version: string };
  maxTries?: number;
  /** Whole-job budget (the route's maxDuration is 300 s). */
  budgetMs?: number;
  sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
  now?: () => number;
};
export type ExtractOutcome = { status: "ok" | "failed" | "refused"; code: string | null; tries: number };

const BACKOFF_MS = [1_000, 3_000];

async function toDocument(deps: ExtractDeps, f: StoredFile, title: string): Promise<AiDocument> {
  const bytes = await deps.download(f.path);
  if (f.mime === DOCX_MIME) return { kind: "text", title, text: await deps.docxToText(bytes) };
  return { kind: "pdf", title, base64: Buffer.from(bytes).toString("base64") };
}

export async function runExtraction(deps: ExtractDeps, job: ExtractJob): Promise<ExtractOutcome> {
  const now = deps.now ?? (() => Date.now());
  const sleep = deps.sleep ?? ((ms: number) => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)));
  const started = now();
  const log = (status: RunLog["status"], errorCode: string | null, usage: Usage, model: string | null) =>
    deps.store.logRun({
      familyId: job.familyId, draftId: job.draftId, model, promptVersion: deps.prompt.version, inputTokens: usage.inputTokens,
      outputTokens: usage.outputTokens, costUsd: model ? estimateCostUsd(model, usage.inputTokens, usage.outputTokens) : 0, status, errorCode,
      byMemberId: job.byMemberId,
    });

  const gate = await deps.store.gate(job.learnerId);
  if (!gate.allowed && gate.reason) {
    await log(refusalStatus(gate.reason), gate.reason, { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0 }, null);
    await deps.store.fail(job.draftId, `ai_${gate.reason}`, null);
    return { status: "refused", code: `ai_${gate.reason}`, tries: 0 };
  }

  let docs: AiDocument[];
  try {
    docs = [await toDocument(deps, job.testFile, TEST_DOC_TITLE)];
    if (job.keyFile) docs.push(await toDocument(deps, job.keyFile, KEY_DOC_TITLE));
  } catch (err) {
    const code = err instanceof DocxError ? err.code : "file_unreadable";
    await deps.store.fail(job.draftId, code, null);
    return { status: "failed", code, tries: 0 };
  }
  const ctx = await deps.context();

  const maxTries = deps.maxTries ?? 3;
  let toolChoice = deps.toolChoice;
  let lastCode = "ai_failed";
  let lastRun: string | null = null;
  let tries = 0;
  for (let t = 1; t <= maxTries; t += 1) {
    if (t > 1) await sleep(BACKOFF_MS[t - 2] ?? 3_000);
    if (now() - started > (deps.budgetMs ?? 280_000)) {
      lastCode = "timeout";
      break;
    }
    tries = t;
    try {
      const r = await deps.call(buildExtractCall({ model: deps.model, toolChoice, prompt: deps.prompt.text, ctx, docs }));
      const parsed = aiExtractSchema.safeParse(r.input);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        lastRun = await log("error", "bad_output", r.usage, r.model);
        lastCode = "bad_output";
        continue;
      }
      const runId = await log("ok", null, r.usage, r.model);
      await deps.store.finish(job.draftId, normalizeExtract(parsed.data, ctx), runId);
      return { status: "ok", code: null, tries: t };
    } catch (err) {
      const e = err instanceof AiCallError ? err : new AiCallError("network");
      lastRun = await log("error", e.code, e.usage, deps.model);
      lastCode = e.code;
      if (e.code === "tool_choice" && toolChoice === "tool") {
        toolChoice = "auto";
        continue;
      }
      if (e.code === "http_401" || e.code === "http_400") break; // retrying cannot help
    }
    const g = await deps.store.gate(job.learnerId);
    if (!g.allowed && g.reason) {
      lastCode = `ai_${g.reason}`;
      break;
    }
  }
  await deps.store.fail(job.draftId, lastCode, lastRun);
  return { status: "failed", code: lastCode, tries };
}
```

  Note on the test "≤ 3 tries … fail(job.draftId, 'http_5xx', 'run-1')": the fake `logRun` always answers `"run-1"`, so `lastRun` is `"run-1"`.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/studio/requests.ts`, `lib/studio/types.ts`, `lib/studio/shape.ts`, `lib/studio/handlers.ts`, `lib/studio/supabaseStore.ts`, `lib/studio/route.ts`, `lib/studio/load.ts`, `tests/unit/studioHandlers.test.ts`
- Create (thin routes): `app/api/learners/[id]/studio/files/route.ts`, `app/api/learners/[id]/studio/drafts/route.ts`, `app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/route.ts`, `app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/approve/route.ts`, `app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/assign/route.ts`, `app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/discard/route.ts`, `app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/edit/route.ts`, `app/api/settings/ai/route.ts`, `app/api/review/studio/route.ts`, `app/api/review/studio/attempts/[id]/route.ts`, `app/api/student/activities/[id]/studio-review/route.ts`
- Modify: `lib/auth/requireAccess.ts`, `tests/unit/requireAccess.test.ts`, `lib/inquiries/supabaseStore.ts` (L18–28), `lib/work/handlers.ts`, `lib/work/supabaseStore.ts`, `lib/work/types.ts`, `tests/unit/workHandlers.test.ts` (+ any other test file whose fake `WorkStore` must now implement `getMyStudioReview`: `grep -rln "getMyActivityNote" tests/unit`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `requireAccess` / `requireStaff`, `ReviewDbError` + `HandlerResult` (`lib/review/handlers.ts` L38, L84), `verifyStoredFile` + `FILE_ERRORS` (`lib/storage/verify.ts`), `buildPath`, `checkFile`, `createFileStorage`, `engineHook` (`lib/engine/hook.ts`), `logAppError`, `readJson` (`lib/inquiries/route.ts`), `loadPickerMap`, `loadTags`, `allowRealLearners`, Tasks 5–9.
- Produces:
  - Actions `"studio:read"` and `"studio:write"` for **tutor and parent** (not students).
  - HTTP API (all JSON, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, errors `{error, message}`):

| Method · path | Who | Body / answer |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/learners/:id/studio/files` | `studio:write` | `{purpose: studio_test\|studio_key, mime, bytes}` → 201 `{file_id, upload_url, mime}` · 400 `file_type`/`file_too_big`/`file_empty` · 429 `upload_limit` |
| `GET /api/learners/:id/studio/drafts` | `studio:read` | → 200 `{drafts: DraftListItem[]}` |
| `POST /api/learners/:id/studio/drafts` | `studio:write` | `{title, mode: ai\|manual, test_file, key_file, client_id}` → 201 `{id, status}` · 409 `ai_off`/`ai_no_consent`/`ai_cap` · 400 `test_file_required`, `file_missing`, `file_mismatch` (route `maxDuration = 300`) |
| `GET /api/studio/drafts/:id[?view=status]` | `studio:read` | → 200 `{draft, context, issues, summary, can_approve, gate}` or `{status, error_code}`; `draft.aiCostUsd` and `gate` only for the parent |
| `PUT /api/studio/drafts/:id` | `studio:write` | `{title, payload, base_updated_at}` → 200 `{updated_at}` · 409 `draft_changed` · 400 `not_editable` |
| `POST /api/studio/drafts/:id/approve` | `studio:write` | `{base_updated_at}` → 200 `{template_id, version}` · 400 `{error: "blocked", issues}` · 409 `draft_changed` |
| `POST /api/studio/drafts/:id/assign` | `studio:write` | `{start_on, due_on, client_id}` → 201/200 `{instance_id, activity_id, replayed}` · 400 `start_after_due` / `not_approved` |
| `POST /api/studio/drafts/:id/discard` | `studio:write` | → 200 |
| `POST /api/studio/drafts/:id/edit` | `studio:write` | `{client_id}` → 201 `{id}` (draft that replaces an approved test) |
| `GET/PUT /api/settings/ai` | parent (`requireStaff` + role) | PUT `{enabled}` → 200 `AiSettingsView` · 403 `parent_only` |
| `GET /api/review/studio` | staff (`requireStaff`) | → 200 `PendingInbox` (rows the database lets this member decide) |
| `POST /api/review/studio/attempts/:id` | `studio:write` on the attempt's learner | `{items: [{item_id, result}], client_id}` → 200 `{decided, pending, result}`; then the S7a engine runs (fail-soft) |
| `GET /api/student/activities/:id/studio-review` | the student (`work:submit`) | → 200 `{review: StudioReviewView \| null}` |
