```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { AccessStore, Learner, Member, Relationship } from "@/lib/auth/requireAccess";
import { ReviewDbError } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import {
  handleApprove, handleCreateDraft, handleDecide, handleGetDraft, handlePending, handleSetAiSettings, handleAiSettings, handleStudioUploadUrl,
  type StudioDeps, type StudioStore,
} from "@/lib/studio/handlers";
import { normalizeExtract } from "@/lib/studio/normalize";
import { aiExtractSchema } from "@/lib/studio/schema";
import { CTX_B, FAKE_EXTRACT } from "./fixtures/studio";

const FAMILY = "f0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const AN = "a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const NGUYEN = "b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DRAFT = "d0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const ATTEMPT = "c0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const FILE = "e0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000f1";
const mk = (id: string, role: Member["role"]): Member => ({ id: `m-${id}`, userId: `u-${id}`, familyId: FAMILY, role, displayName: id });
const members = [mk("parent", "parent"), mk("tutorAn", "tutor"), mk("tutorNguyen", "tutor"), mk("studentNguyen", "student")];
const learners: Learner[] = [
  { id: AN, familyId: FAMILY, memberId: null, alias: "An", trackId: "A", isTest: true, status: "active" },
  { id: NGUYEN, familyId: FAMILY, memberId: "m-studentNguyen", alias: "Nguyên", trackId: "B", isTest: true, status: "active" },
];
const rels: Relationship[] = [
  { memberId: "m-tutorAn", learnerId: AN, relation: "tutor", validFrom: new Date("2026-01-01"), validTo: null },
  { memberId: "m-tutorNguyen", learnerId: NGUYEN, relation: "tutor", validFrom: new Date("2026-01-01"), validTo: null },
];
const access: AccessStore = {
  getMemberByUserId: async (u) => members.find((m) => m.userId === u) ?? null,
  getLearner: async (id) => learners.find((l) => l.id === id) ?? null,
  getRelationships: async (m, l) => rels.filter((r) => r.memberId === m && r.learnerId === l),
};
const payload = normalizeExtract(aiExtractSchema.parse(FAKE_EXTRACT), CTX_B);
const DRAFT_ROW = {
  id: DRAFT, learner_id: NGUYEN, family_id: FAMILY, track_id: "B", title: "Unit 5", status: "draft", error_code: null, payload,
  updated_at: "2026-11-05 03:00:00.123456+00", created_at: "2026-11-05T03:00:00Z", approved_at: null, approved_by_member: null,
  template_id: null, template_version: null, frame_id: null, supersedes_draft_id: null, superseded_by: null, stale: false,
  test_file: { id: FILE, mime: "application/pdf", path: `${FAMILY}/${NGUYEN}/${FILE}.pdf` }, key_file: null, ai_cost_usd: 0.12, assigned: [],
};
const GATE_ON = { family_id: FAMILY, enabled: true, consent: true, spent_usd: 1.5, monthly_cap_usd: 10, soft_cap_usd: 8, soft_reached: false, allowed: true, reason: null };

function store(over: Partial<StudioStore> = {}): StudioStore {
  return {
    getDraftLearnerId: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (id === DRAFT ? NGUYEN : null)),
    getAttemptLearnerId: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (id === ATTEMPT ? NGUYEN : null)),
    issueUploadTicket: vi.fn(async () => ({ issued: 1 })),
    aiGate: vi.fn(async () => GATE_ON),
    aiSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ enabled: true, monthly_cap_usd: 10, soft_cap_usd: 8, spent_usd: 1.5, runs_month: 2, soft_reached: false, updated_at: null, learners: [] })),
    setAiSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ enabled: false, monthly_cap_usd: 10, soft_cap_usd: 8, spent_usd: 1.5, runs_month: 2, soft_reached: false, updated_at: null, learners: [] })),
    logAiRun: vi.fn(async () => "run-1"),
    createDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: DRAFT, status: "extracting", replayed: false })),
    editDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: DRAFT, replayed: false })),
    draft: vi.fn(async () => DRAFT_ROW),
    drafts: vi.fn(async () => []),
    saveDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ updated_at: "2026-11-05 03:01:00.000001+00" })),
    finishExtraction: vi.fn(async () => ({ applied: true })),
    failExtraction: vi.fn(async () => ({ applied: true })),
    discardDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: DRAFT, status: "discarded" })),
    approveDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ template_id: "B-ST-000001", version: 1, frame_id: "x" })),
    assign: vi.fn(async () => ({ instance_id: "i", activity_id: "a", replayed: false })),
    pending: vi.fn(async () => ({ count: 0, items: [] })),
    decide: vi.fn(async () => ({ decided: 1, pending: 0, result: { recorded: true, correct: 5, in_app: 8 } })),
    ...over,
  };
}
function deps(user: string | null, s: StudioStore = store(), extra: Partial<StudioDeps> = {}): StudioDeps & { jobs: (() => Promise<void>)[] } {
  const jobs: (() => Promise<void>)[] = [];
  return {
    jobs,
    user: user ? { id: `u-${user}` } : null,
    store: s,
    storage: {
      createUpload: vi.fn(async (p: string) => ({ url: `https://upload/${p}` })),
      stat: vi.fn(async () => ({ bytes: 1234, mime: "application/pdf" })),
      createDownload: vi.fn(async () => "https://dl"),
      removeFolder: vi.fn(async () => 0),
      download: vi.fn(async () => new Uint8Array([1])),
    },
    access: { store: access, allowRealLearners: false, now: new Date("2026-11-05T08:00:00Z") },
    schedule: (job) => jobs.push(job),
    loadContext: async () => CTX_B,
    getAi: () => ({ call: async () => ({ input: FAKE_EXTRACT, usage: { inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1 }, model: "m" }), model: "m", toolChoice: "tool", prompt: { text: "{{TAGS}}", version: "studio-extract.v1" } }),
    docxToText: async () => "x",
    newId: () => FILE,
    ...extra,
  };
}
const pdf = { id: FILE, mime: "application/pdf", bytes: 1234 };

describe("Studio API (requireAccess first; tutor or parent of the child)", () => {
  it("students and the other child's tutor get nothing", async () => {
    expect((await handleGetDraft(deps("studentNguyen"), DRAFT)).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await handleGetDraft(deps("tutorAn"), DRAFT)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await handleGetDraft(deps(null), DRAFT)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await handleGetDraft(deps("parent"), "not-a-uuid")).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await handleStudioUploadUrl(deps("tutorAn"), NGUYEN, { purpose: "studio_test", mime: "application/pdf", bytes: 10 })).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("upload: Word only for Studio files, a fresh path built from ids", async () => {
    const d = deps("tutorNguyen");
    const r = await handleStudioUploadUrl(d, NGUYEN, { purpose: "studio_key", mime: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document", bytes: 10 });
    expect(r.status).toBe(201);
    expect((r.body as { upload_url: string }).upload_url).toBe(`https://upload/${FAMILY}/${NGUYEN}/${FILE}.docx`);
    expect((await handleStudioUploadUrl(d, NGUYEN, { purpose: "studio_test", mime: "image/png", bytes: 10 })).body).toMatchObject({ error: "file_type" });
    expect((await handleStudioUploadUrl(d, NGUYEN, { purpose: "studio_test", mime: "application/msword", bytes: 10 })).body).toMatchObject({ error: "file_type" });
  });

  it("create with AI: gate refused → 409 with the reason and a refused run in the ledger; allowed → extraction scheduled after the answer", async () => {
    const off = store({ aiGate: vi.fn(async () => ({ ...GATE_ON, allowed: false, reason: "cap", spent_usd: 10 })) });
    const refused = await handleCreateDraft(deps("parent", off), NGUYEN, { title: "Unit 5", mode: "ai", test_file: pdf, key_file: null, client_id: "client-0001" });
    expect(refused).toMatchObject({ status: 409, body: { error: "ai_cap" } });
    expect(off.logAiRun).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ status: "refused_cap", costUsd: 0 }));
    expect(off.createDraft).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    const s = store();
    const d = deps("tutorNguyen", s);
    const ok = await handleCreateDraft(d, NGUYEN, { title: "Unit 5", mode: "ai", test_file: pdf, key_file: null, client_id: "client-0002" });
    expect(ok).toMatchObject({ status: 201, body: { id: DRAFT, status: "extracting" } });
    expect(d.jobs).toHaveLength(1);
    await d.jobs[0]!();
    expect(s.finishExtraction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    // manual: no gate, no job
    const m = deps("tutorNguyen", store({ createDraft: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: DRAFT, status: "draft", replayed: false })) }));
    expect((await handleCreateDraft(m, NGUYEN, { title: "Unit 5", mode: "manual", test_file: null, key_file: null, client_id: "client-0003" })).status).toBe(201);
    expect(m.jobs).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("the draft view: tutors never see AI cost or the month's spend; the parent does; stored paths never leave the server", async () => {
    const tutor = (await handleGetDraft(deps("tutorNguyen"), DRAFT)).body as { draft: { aiCostUsd: number | null }; gate: unknown; can_approve: boolean };
    expect(tutor.draft.aiCostUsd).toBeNull();
    expect(tutor.gate).toBeNull();
    expect(tutor.can_approve).toBe(false); // item 8 has no key
    expect(JSON.stringify(tutor)).not.toContain(`${FAMILY}/${NGUYEN}/`);
    const parent = (await handleGetDraft(deps("parent"), DRAFT)).body as { draft: { aiCostUsd: number | null }; gate: { spentUsd: number } };
    expect(parent.draft.aiCostUsd).toBe(0.12);
    expect(parent.gate.spentUsd).toBe(1.5);
  });

  it("a stale extraction is marked failed when read", async () => {
    const s = store({ draft: vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...DRAFT_ROW, status: "extracting", stale: true }).mockResolvedValue({ ...DRAFT_ROW, status: "ai_failed", error_code: "timeout" }) });
    const r = await handleGetDraft(deps("tutorNguyen", s), DRAFT, { view: "status" });
    expect(s.failExtraction).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ draftId: DRAFT, errorCode: "timeout", runId: null });
    expect(r.body).toEqual({ status: "ai_failed", error_code: "timeout" });
  });

  it("approve: blocked drafts get the blocking issues; a fixed draft is built and approved", async () => {
    const blocked = await handleApprove(deps("tutorNguyen"), DRAFT, { base_updated_at: DRAFT_ROW.updated_at });
    expect(blocked.status).toBe(400);
    expect(blocked.body).toMatchObject({ error: "blocked", issues: [{ n: 8, code: "missing_key", blocking: true }] });
    const fixed = { ...DRAFT_ROW, payload: { ...payload, items: payload.items.map((i) => (i.n === 8 ? { ...i, key: "is said to be" } : i)) } };
    const s = store({ draft: vi.fn(async () => fixed) });
    expect((await handleApprove(deps("parent", s), DRAFT, { base_updated_at: DRAFT_ROW.updated_at })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(s.approveDraft).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ draftId: DRAFT, base: DRAFT_ROW.updated_at, byMemberId: "m-parent" }));
    expect((await handleApprove(deps("parent", s), DRAFT, { base_updated_at: "other" })).body).toMatchObject({ error: "draft_changed" });
  });

  it("verdicts run the engine after a write; database tags become error codes", async () => {
    const afterWrite = vi.fn(async () => {});
    const ok = await handleDecide(deps("tutorNguyen", store(), { afterWrite }), ATTEMPT, { items: [{ item_id: "q8", result: "correct" }], client_id: "client-0009" });
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    expect(afterWrite).toHaveBeenCalledWith(NGUYEN, null);
    const s = store({ decide: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ReviewDbError("23505", "eng_decide_studio_items: item q8 already has another verdict (already_decided)"); }) });
    expect((await handleDecide(deps("parent", s), ATTEMPT, { items: [{ item_id: "q8", result: "incorrect" }], client_id: "client-0010" })).body).toMatchObject({ error: "already_decided" });
    expect((await handleDecide(deps("tutorAn"), ATTEMPT, { items: [{ item_id: "q8", result: "correct" }], client_id: "client-0011" })).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("AI settings: parent only", async () => {
    expect((await handleAiSettings(deps("tutorNguyen"))).body).toMatchObject({ error: "parent_only" });
    expect((await handleAiSettings(deps("parent"))).status).toBe(200);
    const s = store();
    expect((await handleSetAiSettings(deps("parent", s), { enabled: false })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(s.setAiSettings).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ enabled: false, noticeVersion: "ai-studio-v1", byMemberId: "m-parent" });
    expect((await handleSetAiSettings(deps("studentNguyen"), { enabled: true })).status).toBe(403);
  });

  it("pending inbox: staff only, real learners hidden on previews", async () => {
    const s = store();
    expect((await handlePending(deps("parent", s))).status).toBe(200);
    expect(s.pending).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ byMemberId: "m-parent", allowReal: false });
    expect((await handlePending(deps("studentNguyen"))).status).toBe(403);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Request bodies of the Studio routes (zod, strict). Files are {id, mime, bytes}: the path is always built by the server. */
import { z } from "zod";
import { FILE_ID_RE } from "@/lib/storage/rules";
import { studioDraftSchema, STUDIO_LIMITS } from "./schema";

const clientId = z.string().trim().min(8).max(100);
const isoDate = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
const fileMeta = z.strictObject({ id: z.string().regex(FILE_ID_RE), mime: z.string().max(200), bytes: z.number().int() });
const base = z.string().min(10).max(64);

export const studioUploadBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  purpose: z.enum(["studio_test", "studio_key"]),
  mime: z.string().max(200),
  bytes: z.number().int(),
});
export const createDraftBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(STUDIO_LIMITS.title),
  mode: z.enum(["ai", "manual"]),
  test_file: fileMeta.nullable(),
  key_file: fileMeta.nullable(),
  client_id: clientId,
});
export const saveDraftBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(STUDIO_LIMITS.title),
  payload: studioDraftSchema,
  base_updated_at: base,
});
export const approveBodySchema = z.strictObject({ base_updated_at: base });
export const assignStudioBodySchema = z.strictObject({ start_on: isoDate, due_on: isoDate, client_id: clientId });
export const editBodySchema = z.strictObject({ client_id: clientId });
export const aiSettingsBodySchema = z.strictObject({ enabled: z.boolean() });
export const decideBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  items: z.array(z.strictObject({ item_id: z.string().regex(/^q[1-9][0-9]{0,2}$/), result: z.enum(["correct", "incorrect"]) })).min(1).max(300),
  client_id: clientId,
});
export type FileMetaBody = z.infer<typeof fileMeta>;
```

```ts
// lib/studio/types.ts
/** Typed views of the Studio functions of migration 0024 (camelCase, already validated by shape.ts). */
import type { StudioDraft } from "./schema";

export type DraftStatus = "extracting" | "ai_failed" | "draft" | "approved" | "discarded";
export type DraftView = {
  id: string; learnerId: string; familyId: string; trackId: string; title: string; status: DraftStatus; errorCode: string | null;
  payload: StudioDraft; updatedAt: string; approvedAt: string | null; templateId: string | null; templateVersion: number | null;
  supersedesDraftId: string | null; supersededBy: string | null; stale: boolean;
  testFile: { id: string; mime: string } | null; keyFile: { id: string; mime: string } | null;
  /** Parent only (spec §7); null for a tutor. */
  aiCostUsd: number | null;
  assigned: { instanceId: string; activityId: string; dueOn: string; startOn: string | null; assignedAt: string }[];
};
/** Server-only extra of a draft: where its files are stored (never sent to a browser). */
export type DraftFiles = { test: { path: string; mime: string } | null; key: { path: string; mime: string } | null };
export type DraftListItem = { id: string; title: string; status: DraftStatus; updatedAt: string; templateId: string | null; templateVersion: number | null; superseded: boolean; assigned: number };
export type PendingItem = { itemId: string; n: number; kind: string; stem: string; expected: string | null; accepted: string[]; given: string };
export type PendingAttempt = {
  attemptId: string; learnerId: string; alias: string; isTest: boolean; activityId: string; title: string; submittedAt: string; attemptNo: number;
  waitingDays: number; items: PendingItem[];
};
export type PendingInbox = { count: number; attempts: PendingAttempt[] };
export type StudioReviewView = {
  attemptNo: number; submittedAt: string;
  wrong: { n: number; stem: string; given: string; expected: string | null; explanationVi: string | null }[];
  pending: number[];
};
export type AiSettingsView = {
  enabled: boolean; monthlyCapUsd: number; softCapUsd: number; spentUsd: number; runsMonth: number; softReached: boolean; updatedAt: string | null;
  learners: { id: string; alias: string; isTest: boolean; aiText: boolean | null }[];
};
```

```ts
// lib/studio/shape.ts
/** Parse the jsonb of the Studio functions (0024). Anything unexpected throws (→ 500, logged) instead of showing a half-right screen. */
import { z } from "zod";
import { studioDraftSchema } from "./schema";
import type { AiSettingsView, DraftFiles, DraftListItem, DraftView, PendingInbox, StudioReviewView } from "./types";

const num = z.union([z.number(), z.string()]).transform(Number);
const status = z.enum(["extracting", "ai_failed", "draft", "approved", "discarded"]);
const fileRow = z.object({ id: z.string(), mime: z.string(), path: z.string() }).nullable();
const draftRow = z.object({
  id: z.string(), learner_id: z.string(), family_id: z.string(), track_id: z.string(), title: z.string(), status, error_code: z.string().nullable(),
  payload: studioDraftSchema, updated_at: z.string(), approved_at: z.string().nullable(), template_id: z.string().nullable(),
  template_version: z.number().int().nullable(), supersedes_draft_id: z.string().nullable(), superseded_by: z.string().nullable(), stale: z.boolean(),
  test_file: fileRow, key_file: fileRow, ai_cost_usd: num,
  assigned: z.array(z.object({ instance_id: z.string(), activity_id: z.string(), due_on: z.string(), start_on: z.string().nullable(), assigned_at: z.string() })),
});

export function parseDraft(raw: unknown, opts: { showCost: boolean }): { view: DraftView; files: DraftFiles } {
  const d = draftRow.parse(raw);
  return {
    view: {
      id: d.id, learnerId: d.learner_id, familyId: d.family_id, trackId: d.track_id, title: d.title, status: d.status, errorCode: d.error_code,
      payload: d.payload, updatedAt: d.updated_at, approvedAt: d.approved_at, templateId: d.template_id, templateVersion: d.template_version,
      supersedesDraftId: d.supersedes_draft_id, supersededBy: d.superseded_by, stale: d.stale,
      testFile: d.test_file ? { id: d.test_file.id, mime: d.test_file.mime } : null,
      keyFile: d.key_file ? { id: d.key_file.id, mime: d.key_file.mime } : null,
      aiCostUsd: opts.showCost ? d.ai_cost_usd : null,
      assigned: d.assigned.map((a) => ({ instanceId: a.instance_id, activityId: a.activity_id, dueOn: a.due_on, startOn: a.start_on, assignedAt: a.assigned_at })),
    },
    files: {
      test: d.test_file ? { path: d.test_file.path, mime: d.test_file.mime } : null,
      key: d.key_file ? { path: d.key_file.path, mime: d.key_file.mime } : null,
    },
  };
}

export function parseDraftList(raw: unknown): DraftListItem[] {
  return z
    .array(z.object({ id: z.string(), title: z.string(), status, updated_at: z.string(), template_id: z.string().nullable(), template_version: z.number().int().nullable(), superseded: z.boolean(), assigned: num }))
    .parse(raw)
    .map((d) => ({ id: d.id, title: d.title, status: d.status, updatedAt: d.updated_at, templateId: d.template_id, templateVersion: d.template_version, superseded: d.superseded, assigned: d.assigned }));
}

export function parsePending(raw: unknown): PendingInbox {
  const p = z
    .object({
      count: num,
      items: z.array(z.object({
        attempt_id: z.string(), learner_id: z.string(), alias: z.string(), is_test: z.boolean(), activity_id: z.string(), title: z.string(),
        submitted_at: z.string(), attempt_no: z.number().int(), waiting_days: z.number().int(),
        items: z.array(z.object({ item_id: z.string(), n: z.number().int(), kind: z.string(), stem: z.string(), expected: z.string().nullable(), accepted: z.array(z.string()), given: z.string() })),
      })),
    })
    .parse(raw);
  return {
    count: p.count,
    attempts: p.items.map((a) => ({
      attemptId: a.attempt_id, learnerId: a.learner_id, alias: a.alias, isTest: a.is_test, activityId: a.activity_id, title: a.title, submittedAt: a.submitted_at,
      attemptNo: a.attempt_no, waitingDays: a.waiting_days,
      items: a.items.map((i) => ({ itemId: i.item_id, n: i.n, kind: i.kind, stem: i.stem, expected: i.expected, accepted: i.accepted, given: i.given })),
    })),
  };
}

/** null / {} → null (not a Studio step, nothing submitted). Strict keys: anything else the database might add never reaches the child. */
export function parseStudioReview(raw: unknown): StudioReviewView | null {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined || (typeof raw === "object" && Object.keys(raw as object).length === 0)) return null;
  const r = z
    .strictObject({
      attempt_no: z.number().int(),
      submitted_at: z.string(),
      wrong: z.array(z.strictObject({ n: z.number().int(), stem: z.string(), given: z.string(), expected: z.string().nullable(), explanation_vi: z.string().nullable() })),
      pending: z.array(z.number().int()),
    })
    .parse(raw);
  return { attemptNo: r.attempt_no, submittedAt: r.submitted_at, wrong: r.wrong.map((w) => ({ n: w.n, stem: w.stem, given: w.given, expected: w.expected, explanationVi: w.explanation_vi })), pending: r.pending };
}

export function parseAiSettings(raw: unknown): AiSettingsView {
  const s = z
    .object({
      enabled: z.boolean(), monthly_cap_usd: num, soft_cap_usd: num, spent_usd: num, runs_month: num, soft_reached: z.boolean(), updated_at: z.string().nullable(),
      learners: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), alias: z.string(), is_test: z.boolean(), ai_text: z.boolean().nullable() })),
    })
    .parse(raw);
  return {
    enabled: s.enabled, monthlyCapUsd: s.monthly_cap_usd, softCapUsd: s.soft_cap_usd, spentUsd: s.spent_usd, runsMonth: s.runs_month, softReached: s.soft_reached,
    updatedAt: s.updated_at, learners: s.learners.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, alias: l.alias, isTest: l.is_test, aiText: l.ai_text })),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `lib/studio/handlers.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Framework-free handlers of the Studio B1 routes. Same order everywhere: requireAccess / requireStaff FIRST (401 / 404 outside scope /
 * 403 wrong role), then the body (400), then the injectable stores (service-role functions that check the actor again). A tutor never
 * receives AI cost or the month's spend (spec §7). Stored file paths never leave the server.
 */
import { AccessError, requireAccess, requireStaff, type AccessDeps, type AccessGrant, type AccessUser, type Action, type Member } from "@/lib/auth/requireAccess";
import { parseGate, refusalStatus } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { AI_NOTICE_VERSION } from "@/lib/ai/config";
import type { ToolCall, ToolResult } from "@/lib/ai/request";
import { apiErrors } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { EngineHook } from "@/lib/engine/runner";
import { allowRealLearners } from "@/lib/env";
import { ReviewDbError, type HandlerResult } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { buildPath, checkFile, type AllowedMime, type FileKind } from "@/lib/storage/rules";
import type { FileDownloader, FileStorage } from "@/lib/storage/types";
import { FILE_ERRORS, verifyStoredFile } from "@/lib/storage/verify";
import { buildStudioBuild } from "./buildTemplate";
import type { StudioContext } from "./context";
import { runExtraction, type ExtractStore, type RunLog } from "./extract";
import {
  aiSettingsBodySchema, approveBodySchema, assignStudioBodySchema, createDraftBodySchema, decideBodySchema, editBodySchema, saveDraftBodySchema,
  studioUploadBodySchema, type FileMetaBody,
} from "./requests";
import type { StudioDraft } from "./schema";
import { parseAiSettings, parseDraft, parseDraftList, parsePending } from "./shape";
import { summarizeDraft } from "./summary";
import { blockingIssues, validateDraft } from "./validate";

export interface StudioStore {
  getDraftLearnerId(draftId: string): Promise<string | null>;
  getAttemptLearnerId(attemptId: string): Promise<string | null>;
  issueUploadTicket(a: { learnerId: string; byMemberId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  aiGate(learnerId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  aiSettings(byMemberId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  setAiSettings(a: { enabled: boolean; noticeVersion: string; byMemberId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  logAiRun(r: RunLog): Promise<string>;
  createDraft(a: { learnerId: string; title: string; testFile: FileMetaBody | null; keyFile: FileMetaBody | null; extract: boolean; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  editDraft(a: { draftId: string; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  draft(draftId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  drafts(learnerId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  saveDraft(a: { draftId: string; title: string; payload: StudioDraft; base: string; byMemberId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  finishExtraction(a: { draftId: string; payload: StudioDraft; runId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  failExtraction(a: { draftId: string; errorCode: string; runId: string | null }): Promise<unknown>;
  discardDraft(a: { draftId: string; byMemberId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  approveDraft(a: { draftId: string; base: string; build: unknown; byMemberId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  assign(a: { draftId: string; startOn: string; dueOn: string; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
  pending(a: { byMemberId: string; allowReal: boolean }): Promise<unknown>;
  decide(a: { attemptId: string; items: { item_id: string; result: string }[]; byMemberId: string; clientId: string }): Promise<unknown>;
}

export type StudioAi = { call: (c: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResult>; model: string; toolChoice: "tool" | "auto"; prompt: { text: string; version: string } };
export type StudioDeps = {
  user: AccessUser;
  store: StudioStore;
  storage: FileStorage & FileDownloader;
  access?: Partial<AccessDeps>;
  /** Runs work after the answer was sent (Next `after()` in the route; tests collect the jobs). */
  schedule: (job: () => Promise<void>) => void;
  /** Tags (± 1 level), reading tags and skills of a learner (lib/studio/load.ts). */
  loadContext: (learnerId: string, trackId: string) => Promise<StudioContext>;
  /** Built only when a route needs the AI (reads the prompt file). */
  getAi: () => StudioAi;
  docxToText: (bytes: Uint8Array) => Promise<string>;
  afterWrite?: EngineHook;
  log?: (err: unknown) => Promise<void>;
  newId?: () => string;
};

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
type FailStatus = 400 | 401 | 403 | 404 | 409 | 429;
const fail = (status: FailStatus, error: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): HandlerResult => ({ status, body: { error, message: apiErrors[status], ...extra } });
type Guard = { ok: true; grant: AccessGrant } | { ok: false; result: HandlerResult };

async function guard(deps: StudioDeps, learnerId: string, action: Action): Promise<Guard> {
  try {
    return { ok: true, grant: await requireAccess(deps.user, learnerId, action, deps.access) };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof AccessError) return { ok: false, result: fail(err.status, err.message) };
    throw err;
  }
}
async function guardDraft(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, action: Action): Promise<Guard> {
  if (!deps.user?.id) return { ok: false, result: fail(401, "unauthenticated") };
  if (!UUID_RE.test(draftId)) return { ok: false, result: fail(404, "not_found") };
  const learnerId = await deps.store.getDraftLearnerId(draftId);
  if (!learnerId) return { ok: false, result: fail(404, "not_found") };
  return guard(deps, learnerId, action);
}
async function staff(deps: StudioDeps): Promise<{ ok: true; member: Member } | { ok: false; result: HandlerResult }> {
  try {
    return { ok: true, member: await requireStaff(deps.user, deps.access) };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof AccessError) return { ok: false, result: fail(err.status, err.message) };
    throw err;
  }
}

/** Database errors → HTTP. The "(tag)" at the end of a message becomes the error code the screens explain (lib/copy/vi.ts studioErrors). */
export function fromStudioDbError(err: unknown): HandlerResult {
  if (err instanceof ReviewDbError) {
    const tag = /\(([a-z_]+)\)\s*$/.exec(err.detail)?.[1] ?? null;
    switch (err.code) {
      case "42501":
        return fail(403, tag === "parent_only" ? "parent_only" : "forbidden");
      case "P0002":
        return fail(404, "not_found");
      case "23505":
        return fail(409, tag ?? "conflict");
      case "54000":
        return fail(429, "upload_limit");
      case "23514":
      case "22023":
      case "22004":
      case "22P02":
        return fail(400, tag ?? "invalid");
    }
  }
  throw err;
}

const extractStoreOf = (s: StudioStore): ExtractStore => ({
  gate: async (l) => parseGate(await s.aiGate(l)),
  logRun: (r) => s.logAiRun(r),
  finish: async (draftId, payload, runId) => (await s.finishExtraction({ draftId, payload, runId })) as { applied: boolean },
  fail: async (draftId, errorCode, runId) => (await s.failExtraction({ draftId, errorCode, runId })) as { applied: boolean },
});

// ---- Files ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function handleStudioUploadUrl(deps: StudioDeps, learnerId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = studioUploadBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  const c = checkFile(p.data.purpose, p.data.mime, p.data.bytes);
  if (!c.ok) return fail(400, FILE_ERRORS[c.reason]);
  try {
    await deps.store.issueUploadTicket({ learnerId: g.grant.learner.id, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id });
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
  const fileId = (deps.newId ?? (() => crypto.randomUUID()))();
  const mime = p.data.mime as AllowedMime;
  const { url } = await deps.storage.createUpload(buildPath({ familyId: g.grant.learner.familyId, learnerId: g.grant.learner.id, fileId, mime }));
  return { status: 201, body: { file_id: fileId, upload_url: url, mime } };
}

// ---- Drafts --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function handleListDrafts(deps: StudioDeps, learnerId: string): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "studio:read");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  return { status: 200, body: { drafts: parseDraftList(await deps.store.drafts(learnerId)) } };
}

export async function handleCreateDraft(deps: StudioDeps, learnerId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = createDraftBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  const b = p.data;
  const { learner, member } = g.grant;
  if (b.mode === "ai" && !b.test_file) return fail(400, "test_file_required");
  const owner = { familyId: learner.familyId, learnerId: learner.id };
  for (const [kind, f] of [["studio_test", b.test_file], ["studio_key", b.key_file]] as [FileKind, FileMetaBody | null][]) {
    if (!f) continue;
    const bad = await verifyStoredFile(deps.storage, owner, kind, { id: f.id, mime: f.mime as AllowedMime, bytes: f.bytes });
    if (bad) return fail(400, bad);
  }
  let ctx: StudioContext | null = null;
  if (b.mode === "ai") {
    const gate = parseGate(await deps.store.aiGate(learner.id));
    if (!gate.allowed && gate.reason) {
      await deps.store.logAiRun({
        familyId: learner.familyId, draftId: null, model: null, promptVersion: null, inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, costUsd: 0,
        status: refusalStatus(gate.reason), errorCode: gate.reason, byMemberId: member.id,
      });
      return fail(409, `ai_${gate.reason}`);
    }
    ctx = await deps.loadContext(learner.id, learner.trackId); // read in the request: the background job has no session
  }
  let made: { id: string; status: string; replayed: boolean };
  try {
    made = (await deps.store.createDraft({
      learnerId: learner.id, title: b.title, testFile: b.test_file, keyFile: b.key_file, extract: b.mode === "ai", byMemberId: member.id, clientId: b.client_id,
    })) as typeof made;
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
  if (made.status === "extracting" && !made.replayed && ctx && b.test_file) {
    const ai = deps.getAi();
    const path = (f: FileMetaBody) => ({ path: buildPath({ ...owner, fileId: f.id, mime: f.mime as AllowedMime }), mime: f.mime });
    const job = {
      draftId: made.id, familyId: learner.familyId, learnerId: learner.id, byMemberId: member.id,
      testFile: path(b.test_file), keyFile: b.key_file ? path(b.key_file) : null,
    };
    const context = ctx;
    deps.schedule(async () => {
      try {
        await runExtraction(
          { store: extractStoreOf(deps.store), download: (p2) => deps.storage.download(p2), docxToText: deps.docxToText, context: async () => context, call: ai.call, model: ai.model, toolChoice: ai.toolChoice, prompt: ai.prompt },
          job,
        );
      } catch (err) {
        await deps.log?.(err);
        await deps.store.failExtraction({ draftId: made.id, errorCode: "ai_failed", runId: null }).catch(() => undefined);
      }
    });
  }
  return { status: made.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: { id: made.id, status: made.status } };
}

export async function handleGetDraft(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, opts: { view?: "status" } = {}): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:read");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const showCost = g.grant.member.role === "parent";
  let { view } = parseDraft(await deps.store.draft(draftId), { showCost });
  if (view.stale) {
    await deps.store.failExtraction({ draftId, errorCode: "timeout", runId: null });
    ({ view } = parseDraft(await deps.store.draft(draftId), { showCost }));
  }
  if (opts.view === "status") return { status: 200, body: { status: view.status, error_code: view.errorCode } };
  const context = await deps.loadContext(g.grant.learner.id, g.grant.learner.trackId);
  const issues = validateDraft(view.payload, context);
  return {
    status: 200,
    body: {
      draft: view,
      context,
      issues,
      summary: summarizeDraft(view.payload, issues),
      can_approve: view.status === "draft" && blockingIssues(issues).length === 0,
      gate: showCost ? parseGate(await deps.store.aiGate(g.grant.learner.id)) : null,
    },
  };
}

export async function handleSaveDraft(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = saveDraftBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.saveDraft({ draftId, title: p.data.title, payload: p.data.payload, base: p.data.base_updated_at, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id })) as { updated_at: string };
    return { status: 200, body: { updated_at: r.updated_at } };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleApprove(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = approveBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  const { view } = parseDraft(await deps.store.draft(draftId), { showCost: false });
  if (view.updatedAt !== p.data.base_updated_at) return fail(409, "draft_changed");
  if (view.status !== "draft") return fail(400, "not_draft");
  const blocking = blockingIssues(validateDraft(view.payload, await deps.loadContext(g.grant.learner.id, g.grant.learner.trackId)));
  if (blocking.length > 0) return fail(400, "blocked", { issues: blocking });
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.approveDraft({ draftId, base: view.updatedAt, build: buildStudioBuild(view.payload), byMemberId: g.grant.member.id })) as { template_id: string; version: number };
    return { status: 200, body: { template_id: r.template_id, version: r.version } };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleAssign(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = assignStudioBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.assign({ draftId, startOn: p.data.start_on, dueOn: p.data.due_on, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: p.data.client_id })) as { instance_id: string; activity_id: string; replayed: boolean };
    return { status: r.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: r };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleDiscard(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  try {
    return { status: 200, body: await deps.store.discardDraft({ draftId, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id }) };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

export async function handleEditApproved(deps: StudioDeps, draftId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const g = await guardDraft(deps, draftId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = editBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  try {
    const r = (await deps.store.editDraft({ draftId, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: p.data.client_id })) as { id: string; replayed: boolean };
    return { status: r.replayed ? 200 : 201, body: { id: r.id } };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

// ---- AI settings (parent) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function handleAiSettings(deps: StudioDeps): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const s = await staff(deps);
  if (!s.ok) return s.result;
  if (s.member.role !== "parent") return fail(403, "parent_only");
  return { status: 200, body: { settings: parseAiSettings(await deps.store.aiSettings(s.member.id)) } };
}

export async function handleSetAiSettings(deps: StudioDeps, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const s = await staff(deps);
  if (!s.ok) return s.result;
  if (s.member.role !== "parent") return fail(403, "parent_only");
  const p = aiSettingsBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  try {
    return { status: 200, body: { settings: parseAiSettings(await deps.store.setAiSettings({ enabled: p.data.enabled, noticeVersion: AI_NOTICE_VERSION, byMemberId: s.member.id })) } };
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
}

// ---- "Cần thầy/cô xem" -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function handlePending(deps: StudioDeps): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  const s = await staff(deps);
  if (!s.ok) return s.result;
  return { status: 200, body: parsePending(await deps.store.pending({ byMemberId: s.member.id, allowReal: deps.access?.allowRealLearners ?? allowRealLearners() })) };
}

export async function handleDecide(deps: StudioDeps, attemptId: string, raw: unknown): Promise<HandlerResult> {
  if (!deps.user?.id) return fail(401, "unauthenticated");
  if (!UUID_RE.test(attemptId)) return fail(404, "not_found");
  const learnerId = await deps.store.getAttemptLearnerId(attemptId);
  if (!learnerId) return fail(404, "not_found");
  const g = await guard(deps, learnerId, "studio:write");
  if (!g.ok) return g.result;
  const p = decideBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!p.success) return fail(400, "invalid_body");
  let r: { decided: number; pending: number; result: { recorded: boolean; correct?: number; in_app?: number } };
  try {
    r = (await deps.store.decide({ attemptId, items: p.data.items, byMemberId: g.grant.member.id, clientId: p.data.client_id })) as typeof r;
  } catch (err) {
    return fromStudioDbError(err);
  }
  if (r.decided > 0) await deps.afterWrite?.(learnerId, null);
  return {
    status: 200,
    body: { decided: r.decided, pending: r.pending, result: { recorded: r.result.recorded, correct: r.result.correct ?? null, in_app: r.result.in_app ?? null } },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: `lib/studio/supabaseStore.ts`**

```ts
import "server-only";
import { ReviewDbError } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import type { StudioStore } from "./handlers";

/** Studio functions of migration 0024 through the service role; only after the handler ran requireAccess / requireStaff. */
export function createSupabaseStudioStore(): StudioStore {
  const call = async (fn: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<unknown> => {
    const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc(fn, args);
    if (error) throw new ReviewDbError(error.code ?? "unknown", `${fn}: ${error.message ?? error.code ?? "error"}`);
    return data;
  };
  const learnerOf = async (table: "studio_drafts" | "attempts", id: string) => {
    const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().from(table).select("learner_id").eq("id", id).maybeSingle<{ learner_id: string }>();
    if (error) throw new ReviewDbError(error.code ?? "unknown", `${table} lookup: ${error.message ?? error.code ?? "error"}`);
    return data?.learner_id ?? null;
  };
  return {
    getDraftLearnerId: (id) => learnerOf("studio_drafts", id),
    getAttemptLearnerId: (id) => learnerOf("attempts", id),
    issueUploadTicket: (a) => call("eng_issue_upload_ticket", { p_learner: a.learnerId, p_by: a.byMemberId }),
    aiGate: (l) => call("eng_ai_gate", { p_learner: l }),
    aiSettings: (m) => call("eng_ai_settings", { p_by: m }),
    setAiSettings: (a) => call("eng_set_ai_settings", { p_enabled: a.enabled, p_notice_version: a.noticeVersion, p_by: a.byMemberId }),
    logAiRun: async (r) =>
      String(
        await call("eng_log_ai_run", {
          p_family: r.familyId, p_draft: r.draftId, p_model: r.model, p_prompt_version: r.promptVersion, p_input: r.inputTokens, p_output: r.outputTokens,
          p_cost: r.costUsd, p_status: r.status, p_error: r.errorCode, p_by: r.byMemberId,
        }),
      ),
    createDraft: (a) =>
      call("eng_create_studio_draft", { p_learner: a.learnerId, p_title: a.title, p_test_file: a.testFile, p_key_file: a.keyFile, p_extract: a.extract, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
    editDraft: (a) => call("eng_edit_studio_draft", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
    draft: (id) => call("eng_studio_draft", { p_draft: id }),
    drafts: (l) => call("eng_studio_drafts", { p_learner: l }),
    saveDraft: (a) => call("eng_save_studio_draft", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_title: a.title, p_payload: a.payload, p_base: a.base, p_by: a.byMemberId }),
    finishExtraction: (a) => call("eng_finish_studio_extraction", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_payload: a.payload, p_run: a.runId }),
    failExtraction: (a) => call("eng_fail_studio_extraction", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_error: a.errorCode, p_run: a.runId }),
    discardDraft: (a) => call("eng_discard_studio_draft", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_by: a.byMemberId }),
    approveDraft: (a) => call("eng_approve_studio_draft", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_base: a.base, p_build: a.build, p_by: a.byMemberId }),
    assign: (a) => call("eng_assign_studio", { p_draft: a.draftId, p_start: a.startOn, p_due: a.dueOn, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
    pending: (a) => call("eng_studio_pending", { p_by: a.byMemberId, p_allow_real: a.allowReal }),
    decide: (a) => call("eng_decide_studio_items", { p_attempt: a.attemptId, p_items: a.items, p_by: a.byMemberId, p_client_id: a.clientId }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: `lib/studio/load.ts` and `lib/studio/route.ts`**

```ts
// lib/studio/load.ts
import "server-only";
import { loadPickerMap } from "@/lib/skillmap/load";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { loadTags } from "@/lib/tutorwork/load";
import { buildStudioContext, type StudioContext } from "./context";

/** Review context of one child (call only after requireAccess / authorizeLearnerPage): the map + current levels, the track's tags, the Studio skills. */
export async function loadStudioContext(learnerId: string, trackId: string): Promise<StudioContext> {
  const [map, tags, skills] = await Promise.all([
    loadPickerMap(learnerId),
    loadTags(trackId),
    createAdminClient()
      .rpc("eng_studio_skills", { p_learner: learnerId })
      .then(({ data, error }) => {
        if (error) throw new Error(`eng_studio_skills failed: ${error.code ?? "error"}`);
        return (data ?? []) as { id: string; title_vi: string }[];
      }),
  ]);
  return buildStudioContext(map, tags, skills);
}
```

```ts
// lib/studio/route.ts
import "server-only";
import { after, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createToolCaller } from "@/lib/ai/anthropic";
import { studioModel, studioToolChoice } from "@/lib/ai/config";
import { loadPrompt, STUDIO_PROMPT } from "@/lib/ai/prompts";
import { apiErrors } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { engineHook } from "@/lib/engine/hook";
import { logAppError } from "@/lib/ops/logAppError";
import type { HandlerResult } from "@/lib/review/handlers";
import { createFileStorage } from "@/lib/storage/server";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { docxToText } from "./docx";
import type { StudioDeps } from "./handlers";
import { loadStudioContext } from "./load";
import { createSupabaseStudioStore } from "./supabaseStore";

const NO_STORE = { "Cache-Control": "private, no-store" };

/** Session → handler → JSON (no-store); 500 logged. Background jobs run in after() (the route sets maxDuration). */
export async function runStudioRoute(routeName: string, run: (deps: StudioDeps) => Promise<HandlerResult>): Promise<NextResponse> {
  let userId: string | null = null;
  try {
    const supabase = await createUserClient();
    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    userId = user?.id ?? null;
    const result = await run({
      user,
      store: createSupabaseStudioStore(),
      storage: createFileStorage(),
      schedule: (job) => after(job),
      loadContext: loadStudioContext,
      getAi: () => ({ call: createToolCaller(), model: studioModel(), toolChoice: studioToolChoice(), prompt: { text: loadPrompt(STUDIO_PROMPT.file), version: STUDIO_PROMPT.version } }),
      docxToText,
      afterWrite: engineHook(routeName, userId),
      log: (error) => logAppError({ route: routeName, code: "studio_extract", error, userId }),
    });
    return NextResponse.json(result.body, { status: result.status, headers: NO_STORE });
  } catch (err) {
    await logAppError({ route: routeName, code: "internal", error: err, userId });
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "internal", message: apiErrors[500] }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

```ts
// app/api/learners/[id]/studio/files/route.ts
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { readJson } from "@/lib/inquiries/route";
import { handleStudioUploadUrl } from "@/lib/studio/handlers";
import { runStudioRoute } from "@/lib/studio/route";

/** POST /api/learners/:id/studio/files - one-time upload link for a Studio test or answer key (PDF or .docx, ≤ 15 MB). */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const body = await readJson(request);
  return runStudioRoute("POST /api/learners/[id]/studio/files", (deps) => handleStudioUploadUrl(deps, id, body));
}
```

```ts
  const body = await readJson(request);

}
```

```ts
// app/api/studio/drafts/[id]/route.ts
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { readJson } from "@/lib/inquiries/route";
import { handleGetDraft, handleSaveDraft } from "@/lib/studio/handlers";
import { runStudioRoute } from "@/lib/studio/route";

/** GET - one draft (?view=status while the AI reads). PUT - save the edited draft (optimistic lock). */
export async function GET(request: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const view = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get("view") === "status" ? "status" : undefined;
  return runStudioRoute("GET /api/studio/drafts/[id]", (deps) => handleGetDraft(deps, id, { view }));
}
export async function PUT(request: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const body = await readJson(request);
  return runStudioRoute("PUT /api/studio/drafts/[id]", (deps) => handleSaveDraft(deps, id, body));
}
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { aiSettingsCopy, studioCopy, studioErrors, studioInboxCopy, studioIssueText, studioStudentCopy, tutorHonorific } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { hasScoreWords } from "@/lib/revision/scoreWords";
import { ISSUE_CODES } from "@/lib/studio/validate";
import { aiFailedText, formatUsd, questionNumber } from "@/lib/studio/view";

/** Every string of a copy object, functions called with sample arguments. */
function strings(o: unknown): string[] {
  if (typeof o === "string") return [o];
  if (typeof o === "function") {
    const f = o as (...a: unknown[]) => unknown;
    const samples: unknown[][] = [[2, 8], [[2, 8]], ["An", 5, 8], ["$1.20", "$10.00"], [null]];
    return samples.flatMap((args) => {
      try {
        return strings(f(...args));
      } catch {
        return []; // a sample of the wrong type for this function
      }
    });
  }
  if (Array.isArray(o)) return o.flatMap(strings);
  if (o && typeof o === "object") return Object.values(o).flatMap(strings);
  return [];
}
const STANDALONE_TEACHER = /(?<![\p{L}/])(thầy|cô)(?![\p{L}/])/iu;

describe("Studio copy (UX_COPY_GUIDE §3.5)", () => {
  it("the child's review never has a score, a ratio, a count of right items or a percentage", () => {
    for (const s of strings(studioStudentCopy)) {
      expect(hasScoreWords(s), s).toBe(false);
      expect(s, s).not.toMatch(/\d+\s*\/\s*\d+|%|điểm|đúng\s+\d/i);
    }
  });
  it("the person who teaches is always 'thầy/cô'; never TOMO", () => {
    for (const s of strings({ studioCopy, studioStudentCopy, studioInboxCopy, aiSettingsCopy, studioErrors, studioIssueText })) {
      expect(s, s).not.toMatch(STANDALONE_TEACHER);
      expect(s, s).not.toMatch(/tomo/i);
    }
    expect(studioInboxCopy.tab).toBe(`Cần ${tutorHonorific} xem`);
  });
  it("every issue code has a text; the guide lists the main labels", () => {
    for (const c of ISSUE_CODES) expect(studioIssueText[c]).toBeTruthy();
    const guide = readFileSync(new URL("../../docs/UX_COPY_GUIDE.md", import.meta.url), "utf8");
    for (const s of [studioCopy.reviewTitle, studioCopy.onlyFlagged, studioCopy.partSkill, studioStudentCopy.title, studioStudentCopy.shouldBe, studioInboxCopy.tab, aiSettingsCopy.enable]) {
      expect(guide, s).toContain(s);
    }
  });
  it("view helpers", () => {
    expect(questionNumber("q12", 0)).toBe(12);
    expect(questionNumber("W4-R2", 3)).toBe(4);
    expect(formatUsd(1.2)).toBe("$1.20");
    expect(aiFailedText("timeout")).toBe(studioCopy.aiFailedReason.timeout);
    expect(aiFailedText("something_new")).toBe(studioCopy.aiFailedReason.ai_failed);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Small text helpers of the Studio screens (pure). */
import { studioCopy, studioErrors } from "@/lib/copy/vi";

/** The printed number of a Studio item (ids q<n>); other items keep their position number (weekly topics). */
export function questionNumber(itemId: string, index: number): number {
  const m = /^q([1-9][0-9]{0,2})$/.exec(itemId);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : index + 1;
}
export const formatUsd = (n: number) => `$${n.toFixed(2)}`;
export const aiFailedText = (code: string | null) => studioCopy.aiFailedReason[code ?? ""] ?? studioCopy.aiFailedReason.ai_failed;
export const studioErrorText = (code: string | null | undefined) => (code && studioErrors[code]) || studioErrors.invalid;
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `app/page.tsx` (parent branch), `lib/studio/load.ts` (add `loadAiSettings`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `handleAiSettings` / `PUT /api/settings/ai`, `authorizeStaffPage` (`lib/review/access.ts`), `aiSettingsCopy`, `formatUsd`.
- Produces: `loadAiSettings(memberId): Promise<AiSettingsView | null>` (null = not readable, e.g. before 0024); `lib/studio/client.ts`: `sendJson<T>(url, method, body)` → `{ok: true, body: T} | {ok: false, status, error}`; `uploadStudioFile(learnerId, purpose, file)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Client helper** — `lib/studio/client.ts`

```ts
"use client";
/** Browser helpers of the Studio screens: JSON calls and the one-time upload of a test / answer file. No secrets, no server imports. */
import { putToSignedUrl } from "@/lib/storage/clientUpload";
import { checkFile, type FileKind } from "@/lib/storage/rules";

export type Sent<T> = { ok: true; status: number; body: T } | { ok: false; status: number; error: string; body: unknown };

export async function sendJson<T>(url: string, method: "POST" | "PUT" | "GET", body?: unknown): Promise<Sent<T>> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method,
      headers: body === undefined ? undefined : { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
      cache: "no-store",
    });
    const json = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as (T & { error?: string }) | null;
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false, status: res.status, error: json?.error ?? "invalid", body: json };
    return { ok: true, status: res.status, body: json as T };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, status: 0, error: "network", body: null };
  }
}

/** Ask the server for a one-time link for this file, PUT the file straight to storage, return {id, mime, bytes} for the draft. */
export async function uploadStudioFile(learnerId: string, purpose: Extract<FileKind, "studio_test" | "studio_key">, file: File) {
  const c = checkFile(purpose, file.type, file.size);
  if (!c.ok) return { ok: false as const, error: c.reason === "too_big" ? "file_too_big" : c.reason === "empty" ? "file_empty" : "file_type" };
  const t = await sendJson<{ file_id: string; upload_url: string; mime: string }>(`/api/learners/${learnerId}/studio/files`, "POST", { purpose, mime: file.type, bytes: file.size });
  if (!t.ok) return { ok: false as const, error: t.error };
  // Same request as the practice uploads (lib/storage/clientUpload.ts): storage records the file's own type, checked again on the server.
  if (!(await putToSignedUrl(t.body.upload_url, file))) return { ok: false as const, error: "file_missing" };
  return { ok: true as const, file: { id: t.body.file_id, mime: file.type, bytes: file.size } };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Loader.** Append to `lib/studio/load.ts`:

```ts
import { parseAiSettings } from "./shape";
import type { AiSettingsView } from "./types";

/** The family's AI settings for the parent (call only after authorizeStaffPage + role parent). null when unreadable (e.g. 0024 not applied). */
export async function loadAiSettings(memberId: string): Promise<AiSettingsView | null> {
  const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc("eng_ai_settings", { p_by: memberId });
  if (error) return null;
  return parseAiSettings(data);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Page + form.**

```tsx
// app/settings/ai/page.tsx
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { AiSettingsForm } from "@/components/settings/AiSettingsForm";
import { aiSettingsCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { authorizeStaffPage } from "@/lib/review/access";
import { loadAiSettings } from "@/lib/studio/load";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Cài đặt AI (spec §3 step 0): parent only. Tutors and students: 404. */
export default async function AiSettingsPage() {
  const supabase = await createUserClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const access = await authorizeStaffPage(user);
  if (access.kind === "login") redirect("/login");
  if (access.kind === "not-found" || access.member.role !== "parent") notFound();
  const settings = await loadAiSettings(access.member.id);
  if (!settings) notFound();
  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader name={access.member.displayName} />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-10" data-testid="ai-settings">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{aiSettingsCopy.title}</h1>
        <AiSettingsForm initial={settings} />
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/settings/AiSettingsForm.tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { aiSettingsCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { sendJson } from "@/lib/studio/client";
import type { AiSettingsView } from "@/lib/studio/types";
import { formatUsd, studioErrorText } from "@/lib/studio/view";

/** The short consent text, "Đồng ý và bật" / "Tắt AI", the month's estimated cost against the cap, and each child's ai_text consent. */
export function AiSettingsForm({ initial }: { initial: AiSettingsView }) {
  const [s, setS] = useState(initial);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  async function toggle(enabled: boolean) {
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    const r = await sendJson<{ settings: AiSettingsView }>("/api/settings/ai", "PUT", { enabled });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(studioErrorText(r.error));
    setS(r.body.settings);
    setMsg(aiSettingsCopy.saved);
  }
  const spent = formatUsd(s.spentUsd);
  const cap = formatUsd(s.monthlyCapUsd);
  return (
    <section className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4">
      <p className="text-[15px]">{aiSettingsCopy.intro}</p>
      <ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-sm text-muted">
        {aiSettingsCopy.points.map((p) => (
          <li key={p}>{p}</li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <p role="status" data-testid="ai-state" className="font-semibold">
        {s.enabled ? aiSettingsCopy.on : aiSettingsCopy.off}
      </p>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        {s.enabled ? (
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void toggle(false)} data-testid="ai-disable" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 font-semibold">
            {aiSettingsCopy.disable}
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void toggle(true)} data-testid="ai-enable" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 font-bold text-white">
            {aiSettingsCopy.enable}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <p data-testid="ai-spent">{aiSettingsCopy.spent(spent, cap)}</p>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted">{aiSettingsCopy.runs(s.runsMonth)}</p>
      {s.spentUsd >= s.monthlyCapUsd ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-[#FBF1DC] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#6B4600]">{aiSettingsCopy.capReached}</p>
      ) : s.softReached ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-[#FBF1DC] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#6B4600]">{aiSettingsCopy.softWarning(spent, cap)}</p>
      ) : null}
      <ul className="text-sm" data-testid="ai-consents">
        {s.learners.map((l) => (
          <li key={l.id}>{aiSettingsCopy.consentLine(l.alias, l.aiText)}</li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {msg ? <p role="status" className="text-sm text-accent">{msg}</p> : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/settings/AiSoftCapBox.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { aiSettingsCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { AiSettingsView } from "@/lib/studio/types";
import { formatUsd } from "@/lib/studio/view";

/** Parent home: shown only when the month's AI spend reached the soft cap (spec §5.5). */
export function AiSoftCapBox({ settings }: { settings: AiSettingsView }) {
  if (!settings.softReached) return null;
  const spent = formatUsd(settings.spentUsd);
  const cap = formatUsd(settings.monthlyCapUsd);
  return (
    <section data-testid="ai-soft-cap" className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-[#E9C46A] bg-[#FBF1DC] px-5 py-4 text-[#6B4600]">
      <h2 className="font-semibold">{aiSettingsCopy.homeBoxTitle}</h2>
      <p className="text-sm">{settings.spentUsd >= settings.monthlyCapUsd ? aiSettingsCopy.capReached : aiSettingsCopy.softWarning(spent, cap)}</p>
      <Link href="/settings/ai" className="mt-1 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm font-semibold underline">
        {aiSettingsCopy.homeBoxLink}
      </Link>
    </section>
  );
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `authorizeLearnerPage(user, learnerId, "studio:write")`, `POST /api/learners/:id/studio/files`, `POST /api/learners/:id/studio/drafts`, `GET /api/studio/drafts/:id?view=status`.
- Produces: `loadStudioGate(learnerId): Promise<{allowed: boolean; reason: GateReason | null}>` (no cost numbers — tutors read this page), `loadDraftList(learnerId)`; page `/studio/new?learner=<id>`; component `ExtractWaiting({draftId})` used by Task 14.

- [ ] **Step 1: Loaders.** Append to `lib/studio/load.ts`:

```ts
import { parseGate, type GateReason } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { parseDraftList } from "./shape";
import type { DraftListItem } from "./types";

/** Whether "Cho AI đọc đề" is offered, and why not. Never the cost (a tutor reads this page). */
export async function loadStudioGate(learnerId: string): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; reason: GateReason | null }> {
  const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc("eng_ai_gate", { p_learner: learnerId });
  if (error) return { allowed: false, reason: "off" };
  const g = parseGate(data);
  return { allowed: g.allowed, reason: g.reason };
}
export async function loadDraftList(learnerId: string): Promise<DraftListItem[]> {
  const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc("eng_studio_drafts", { p_learner: learnerId });
  if (error) throw new Error(`eng_studio_drafts failed: ${error.code ?? "error"}`);
  return parseDraftList(data);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Page.**

```tsx
// app/studio/new/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { DraftList } from "@/components/studio/DraftList";
import { UploadForm } from "@/components/studio/UploadForm";
import { learnerWithTrack, reviewPage, studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { authorizeLearnerPage } from "@/lib/review/access";
import { loadDraftList, loadStudioGate } from "@/lib/studio/load";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Tạo bài từ đề (spec §3 step 1): the child's tutor or parent. ?learner=<id>. Students / outsiders: 404. */
export default async function StudioNewPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ learner?: string }> }) {
  const { learner } = await searchParams;
  const supabase = await createUserClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const access = await authorizeLearnerPage(user, learner ?? "", "studio:write");
  if (access.kind === "login") redirect("/login");
  if (access.kind === "not-found") notFound();
  const l = access.grant.learner;
  const [gate, drafts] = await Promise.all([loadStudioGate(l.id), loadDraftList(l.id)]);
  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader name={access.grant.member.displayName} showTestBadge={l.isTest} />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-10" data-testid="studio-new">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm text-accent underline">
          {reviewPage.back}
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{studioCopy.newTitle}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-muted">
          {studioCopy.learnerLabel} {learnerWithTrack(l.alias, l.trackId)}
        </p>
        <p className="mt-3 text-[15px]">{studioCopy.newIntro}</p>
        <UploadForm learnerId={l.id} aiAllowed={gate.allowed} aiReason={gate.reason} />
        <DraftList drafts={drafts} />
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Upload form.**

```tsx
// components/studio/UploadForm.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { GateReason } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { sendJson, uploadStudioFile } from "@/lib/studio/client";
import { studioErrorText } from "@/lib/studio/view";

const ACCEPT = "application/pdf,.pdf,application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document,.docx";

/** Title + test file + answer file → "Cho AI đọc đề" (when the gate allows) or "Soạn tay". Files go straight to storage. */
export function UploadForm({ learnerId, aiAllowed, aiReason }: { learnerId: string; aiAllowed: boolean; aiReason: GateReason | null }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [test, setTest] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [key, setKey] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<"ai" | "manual" | null>(null);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const clientId = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());

  async function start(mode: "ai" | "manual") {
    if (!title.trim()) return setProblem(studioErrorText("invalid_body"));
    if (mode === "ai" && !test) return setProblem(studioErrorText("test_file_required"));
    setBusy(mode);
    setProblem(null);
    const up = async (f: File | null, purpose: "studio_test" | "studio_key") => (f ? uploadStudioFile(learnerId, purpose, f) : { ok: true as const, file: null });
    const t = await up(test, "studio_test");
    const k = t.ok ? await up(key, "studio_key") : t;
    if (!t.ok || !k.ok) {
      setBusy(null);
      return setProblem(studioErrorText(!t.ok ? t.error : !k.ok ? k.error : null));
    }
    const r = await sendJson<{ id: string; status: string }>(`/api/learners/${learnerId}/studio/drafts`, "POST", {
      title: title.trim(), mode, test_file: t.file, key_file: k.file, client_id: clientId.current,
    });
    setBusy(null);
    if (!r.ok) return setProblem(studioErrorText(r.error));
    router.push(`/studio/drafts/${r.body.id}`);
  }

  return (
    <form className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-5" onSubmit={(e) => e.preventDefault()} noValidate>
      <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm font-semibold">
        {studioCopy.titleLabel}
        <input value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} maxLength={160} placeholder={studioCopy.titlePlaceholder} data-testid="studio-title" className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3 font-normal" />
      </label>
      {(["test", "key"] as const).map((which) => (
        <label key={which} className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm font-semibold">
          {which === "test" ? studioCopy.testFile : studioCopy.keyFile}
          <input type="file" accept={ACCEPT} data-testid={`studio-${which}-file`} onChange={(e) => (which === "test" ? setTest : setKey)(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} className="min-h-11 font-normal" />
          <span className="text-xs font-normal text-muted">{studioCopy.fileHint}</span>
        </label>
      ))}
      {!aiAllowed && aiReason ? (
        <p role="status" data-testid="ai-unavailable" className="rounded-xl bg-[#FBF1DC] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#6B4600]">
          {studioCopy.aiUnavailable[`ai_${aiReason}`]}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {problem ? (
        <p role="alert" data-testid="studio-problem" className="rounded-xl bg-[#FBF1DC] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#6B4600]">
          {problem}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        {aiAllowed ? (
          <button type="button" disabled={busy !== null} onClick={() => void start("ai")} data-testid="studio-start-ai" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-60">
            {busy === "ai" ? studioCopy.uploading : studioCopy.startAi}
          </button>
        ) : null}
        <button type="button" disabled={busy !== null} onClick={() => void start("manual")} data-testid="studio-manual" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-60">
          {busy === "manual" ? studioCopy.uploading : studioCopy.manual}
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">{studioCopy.manualHint}</p>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Waiting screen.**

```tsx
// components/studio/ExtractWaiting.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";

const EVERY_MS = 3000;
const GIVE_UP_MS = 6 * 60 * 1000;

/** "AI đang đọc đề…" (spec §3 step 2): asks the draft's status every 3 s; when it is no longer extracting, the page reloads itself. */
export function ExtractWaiting({ draftId }: { draftId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tooLong, setTooLong] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const started = Date.now();
    let stop = false;
    const timer = setInterval(async () => {
      if (stop) return;
      if (Date.now() - started > GIVE_UP_MS) setTooLong(true);
      const res = await fetch(`/api/studio/drafts/${draftId}?view=status`, { cache: "no-store" }).catch(() => null);
      const body = res && res.ok ? ((await res.json()) as { status: string }) : null;
      if (body && body.status !== "extracting") {
        stop = true;
        clearInterval(timer);
        router.refresh();
      }
    }, EVERY_MS);
    return () => {
      stop = true;
      clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, [draftId, router]);
  return (
    <section role="status" aria-live="polite" data-testid="extract-waiting" className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-5">
      <p className="text-lg font-semibold text-accent">{studioCopy.reading}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{tooLong ? studioCopy.readingTooLong : studioCopy.readingHint}</p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

After Duyệt: the test is locked; a **Giao bài** form with *Ngày làm* and *Hạn nộp*; the list of assignments; "Sửa đề đã duyệt".

```ts
import type { StudioContext } from "./context";
import type { DraftSummary } from "./summary";
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Loader.** Append to `lib/studio/load.ts`:

```ts
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { createFileStorage } from "@/lib/storage/server";
import { docxToText } from "./docx";
import { handleGetDraft, type StudioDeps } from "./handlers";
import { createSupabaseStudioStore } from "./supabaseStore";
import type { DraftPageData } from "./types";

/** The page calls the same handler as GET /api/studio/drafts/:id (one access path). No AI and no background job from a page. */
export async function loadDraftPage(draftId: string): Promise<{ kind: "ok"; data: DraftPageData; isParent: boolean } | { kind: "login" } | { kind: "not-found" }> {
  const supabase = await createUserClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const deps: StudioDeps = {
    user,
    store: createSupabaseStudioStore(),
    storage: createFileStorage(),
    schedule: () => undefined,
    loadContext: loadStudioContext,
    getAi: () => {
      throw new Error("no AI from a page");
    },
    docxToText,
  };
  const res = await handleGetDraft(deps, draftId);
  if (res.status === 401) return { kind: "login" };
  if (res.status !== 200) return { kind: "not-found" };
  const data = res.body as DraftPageData;
  return { kind: "ok", data, isParent: data.gate !== null };
}
```

```tsx
// app/studio/drafts/[id]/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { AssignStudioForm } from "@/components/studio/AssignStudioForm";
import { DraftEditor } from "@/components/studio/DraftEditor";
import { EditApprovedButton } from "@/components/studio/EditApprovedButton";
import { ExtractWaiting } from "@/components/studio/ExtractWaiting";
import { SidePanel } from "@/components/studio/SidePanel";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { todayVn } from "@/lib/inquiries/dates";
import { formatDay } from "@/lib/review/format";
import { loadDraftPage } from "@/lib/studio/load";
import { aiFailedText } from "@/lib/studio/view";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Duyệt đề (board 19) and, once approved, Giao bài. The child's tutor or parent; others 404. */
export default async function DraftPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const r = await loadDraftPage(id);
  if (r.kind === "login") redirect("/login");
  if (r.kind === "not-found") notFound();
  const { draft, summary, gate } = r.data;
  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8" data-testid="studio-draft" data-status={draft.status}>
        <Link href={`/studio/new?learner=${draft.learnerId}`} className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm text-accent underline">
          {studioCopy.newTitle}
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{draft.status === "approved" ? draft.title : studioCopy.reviewTitle}</h1>
        {draft.status === "extracting" ? <ExtractWaiting draftId={draft.id} /> : null}
        {draft.status === "ai_failed" ? (
          <section role="alert" data-testid="ai-failed" className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#E9C46A] bg-[#FBF1DC] p-4 text-[#6B4600]">
            <p className="font-semibold">{studioCopy.aiFailedTitle}</p>
            <p className="text-sm">{aiFailedText(draft.errorCode)} {studioCopy.aiFailedNext}</p>
          </section>
        ) : null}
        {draft.status === "draft" || draft.status === "ai_failed" ? <DraftEditor initial={r.data} /> : null}
        {draft.status === "approved" ? (
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_20rem]">
            <section data-testid="approved-box" className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-5">
              <p className="font-semibold text-accent">{studioCopy.approvedTitle}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted">{studioCopy.approvedNote}</p>
              <ul className="mt-3 text-sm" data-testid="assigned-list">
                {draft.assigned.map((a) => (
                  <li key={a.instanceId}>{studioCopy.assignedLine(formatDay(a.startOn ?? a.dueOn), formatDay(a.dueOn))}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <AssignStudioForm draftId={draft.id} today={todayVn()} />
              {draft.supersededBy ? null : <EditApprovedButton draftId={draft.id} />}
            </section>
            <SidePanel summary={summary} aiCostUsd={draft.aiCostUsd} gate={gate} />
          </div>
        ) : null}
        {draft.status === "discarded" ? <p className="mt-4 text-muted">{studioCopy.statusLabels.discarded}</p> : null}
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```
