```tsx
// components/studio/SidePanel.tsx
import type { GateState } from "@/lib/ai/cap";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { DraftSummary } from "@/lib/studio/summary";
import { formatUsd } from "@/lib/studio/view";

/** "Bài sẽ tạo" (board 19). AI cost lines only when the server sent them (parent, spec §7). */
export function SidePanel({ summary, aiCostUsd, gate }: { summary: DraftSummary; aiCostUsd: number | null; gate: GateState | null }) {
  const row = (label: string, n: number, testId: string) => (
    <li className="flex justify-between gap-3" data-testid={testId}>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <span className="font-semibold">{studioCopy.side.items(n)}</span>
    </li>
  );
  return (
    <aside className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-4 lg:sticky lg:top-4" data-testid="studio-side">
      <h2 className="font-semibold">{studioCopy.side.title}</h2>
      <ul className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
        {row(studioCopy.side.auto, summary.auto, "side-auto")}
        {row(studioCopy.side.mayNeedPerson, summary.mayNeedPerson, "side-person")}
        {row(studioCopy.side.counted, summary.counted, "side-counted")}
        {row(studioCopy.side.signalOnly, summary.signalOnly, "side-signal")}
        {row(studioCopy.side.paperFrame, summary.total, "side-frame")}
      </ul>
      {aiCostUsd !== null ? (
        <p className="mt-3 border-t border-line pt-3 text-sm" data-testid="side-ai-cost">
          {studioCopy.side.aiCost}: {formatUsd(aiCostUsd)}
          {gate ? <span className="block text-muted">{studioCopy.side.aiMonth(formatUsd(gate.spentUsd), formatUsd(gate.monthlyCapUsd))}</span> : null}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </aside>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Item card.**

```tsx
// components/studio/ItemCard.tsx
"use client";

import { studioCopy, studioIssueText } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { tagOf, type StudioContext } from "@/lib/studio/context";
import { STUDIO_ITEM_KINDS, type StudioItem, type StudioPart, type StudioPassage } from "@/lib/studio/schema";
import type { Issue } from "@/lib/studio/validate";

const field = "min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-3 text-[15px]";

/** One item of board 19. `onChange` gets a patch; the editor keeps the draft. */
export function ItemCard({
  item, parts, passages, context, issues, onChange, onRemove,
}: {
  item: StudioItem; parts: StudioPart[]; passages: StudioPassage[]; context: StudioContext; issues: Issue[];
  onChange: (patch: Partial<StudioItem>) => void; onRemove: () => void;
}) {
  const tagValue = item.reading_skill_key ?? item.tag_key ?? "";
  const reading = context.tags.filter((t) => t.reading);
  const near = context.tags.filter((t) => !t.reading && t.near);
  const far = context.tags.filter((t) => !t.reading && !t.near);
  function pickTag(key: string) {
    const t = tagOf(context, key || null);
    if (!t) return onChange({ tag_key: null, reading_skill_key: null });
    if (!t.reading) return onChange({ tag_key: t.key, reading_skill_key: null });
    const skill = item.target_id ?? t.related.find((id) => context.skills.some((s) => s.id === id)) ?? null;
    onChange({ reading_skill_key: t.key, tag_key: null, target_id: skill });
  }
  return (
    <li data-testid={`item-${item.n}`} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white p-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <strong>{studioCopy.itemLabel(item.n)}</strong>
        {issues.filter((i) => i.blocking).map((i) => (
          <span key={i.code} data-testid={`issue-${item.n}-${i.code}`} className="rounded-full bg-[#F8D7D3] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#8A1C12]">
            {studioIssueText[i.code]}
          </span>
        ))}
        {item.needs_review ? (
          <span className="rounded-full bg-[#FBF1DC] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-[#6B4600]" title={item.review_reason ?? undefined}>
            {studioCopy.needsReview}
            {item.review_reason ? `: ${item.review_reason}` : ""}
          </span>
        ) : null}
        <button type="button" onClick={onRemove} className="ml-auto min-h-11 text-sm text-muted underline">{studioCopy.removeItem}</button>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.number}
          <input type="number" min={1} max={300} value={item.n} onChange={(e) => onChange({ n: Number(e.target.value) })} className={field} />
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.part}
          <select value={item.part} onChange={(e) => onChange({ part: e.target.value })} className={field}>
            {parts.map((p) => <option key={p.key} value={p.key}>{p.key} — {p.name}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.kind}
          <select value={item.kind} onChange={(e) => onChange({ kind: e.target.value as StudioItem["kind"], options: e.target.value === "mcq" ? item.options : [] })} className={field}>
            {STUDIO_ITEM_KINDS.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k}>{studioCopy.kinds[k]}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={item.out_of_app} onChange={(e) => onChange({ out_of_app: e.target.checked })} className="h-5 w-5" />
        {studioCopy.outOfApp}
      </label>
      <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
        {studioCopy.stem}
        <textarea lang="en" rows={2} value={item.stem} onChange={(e) => onChange({ stem: e.target.value })} className={`${field} py-2`} />
      </label>
      {item.kind === "mcq" ? (
        <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <legend className="text-xs font-semibold">{studioCopy.options}</legend>
          {item.options.map((o, k) => (
            <div key={k} className="flex gap-2">
              <input aria-label="letter" value={o.letter} maxLength={1} onChange={(e) => onChange({ options: item.options.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, letter: e.target.value.toUpperCase() } : x)) })} className={`${field} w-14`} />
              <input lang="en" value={o.text} onChange={(e) => onChange({ options: item.options.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, text: e.target.value } : x)) })} className={`${field} flex-1`} />
            </div>
          ))}
          <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm text-accent underline" onClick={() => onChange({ options: [...item.options, { letter: String.fromCharCode(65 + item.options.length), text: "" }] })}>
            {studioCopy.addOption}
          </button>
        </fieldset>
      ) : null}
      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {item.kind === "mcq" ? studioCopy.keyMcq : studioCopy.key}
          <input lang="en" data-testid={`item-${item.n}-key`} value={item.key ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onChange({ key: e.target.value.trim() ? e.target.value : null })} className={field} />
        </label>
        {item.kind !== "mcq" ? (
          <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
            {studioCopy.accepted}
            <textarea lang="en" rows={2} placeholder={studioCopy.acceptedHint} value={item.accepted.join("\n")} onChange={(e) => onChange({ accepted: e.target.value.split("\n").map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) })} className={`${field} py-2`} />
          </label>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.tag}
          <select data-testid={`item-${item.n}-tag`} value={tagValue} onChange={(e) => pickTag(e.target.value)} className={field}>
            <option value="">{studioCopy.pickTag}</option>
            {[["reading", reading], ["near", near], ["far", far]].map(([g, list]) =>
              (list as typeof reading).length ? (
                <optgroup key={g as string} label={g === "reading" ? (reading[0]?.group ?? "") : g === "near" ? studioCopy.tag : `${studioCopy.tag} …`}>
                  {(list as typeof reading).map((t) => <option key={t.key} value={t.key}>{t.label}</option>)}
                </optgroup>
              ) : null,
            )}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.skill}
          <select data-testid={`item-${item.n}-skill`} value={item.target_id ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onChange({ target_id: e.target.value || null })} className={field}>
            <option value="">{studioCopy.noSkill}</option>
            {context.skills.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.passageOfItem}
          <select value={item.passage_ref ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onChange({ passage_ref: e.target.value || null })} className={field}>
            <option value="">{studioCopy.noPassage}</option>
            {passages.map((p) => <option key={p.ref} value={p.ref}>{p.ref} — {p.title}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
        {studioCopy.explanation}
        <textarea rows={2} maxLength={300} value={item.explanation_vi} onChange={(e) => onChange({ explanation_vi: e.target.value })} className={`${field} py-2`} />
      </label>
    </li>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Editor.**

```tsx
// components/studio/DraftEditor.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { studioCopy, studioIssueText } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { sendJson } from "@/lib/studio/client";
import type { DraftPageData } from "@/lib/studio/types";
import type { StudioDraft, StudioItem } from "@/lib/studio/schema";
import { sortForReview, summarizeDraft } from "@/lib/studio/summary";
import { blockingIssues, validateDraft } from "@/lib/studio/validate";
import { studioErrorText } from "@/lib/studio/view";
import { ItemCard } from "./ItemCard";
import { SidePanel } from "./SidePanel";

/** Board 19: edit every item, part skill, reading texts; save with the optimistic lock; Duyệt only without blocking issues. */
export function DraftEditor({ initial }: { initial: DraftPageData }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { context } = initial;
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(initial.draft.title);
  const [d, setD] = useState<StudioDraft>(initial.draft.payload);
  const [base, setBase] = useState(initial.draft.updatedAt);
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);
  const [onlyFlagged, setOnlyFlagged] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const issues = useMemo(() => validateDraft(d, context), [d, context]);
  const blocking = blockingIssues(issues);
  const summary = summarizeDraft(d, issues);
  const flagged = new Set(issues.filter((i) => i.n !== null).map((i) => i.n as number));

  const change = (next: StudioDraft) => {
    setD(next);
    setDirty(true);
  };
  const upd = (n: number, patch: Partial<StudioItem>) => change({ ...d, items: d.items.map((i) => (i.n === n ? { ...i, ...patch } : i)) });
  const partSkill = (part: string, target: string | null) =>
    change({ ...d, items: d.items.map((i) => (i.part === part && !i.out_of_app ? { ...i, target_id: target } : i)) });
  const addItem = (part: string) => {
    const n = Math.max(0, ...d.items.map((i) => i.n)) + 1;
    change({
      ...d,
      items: [...d.items, { n, part, kind: "gap", out_of_app: false, stem: "", options: [], passage_ref: null, key: null, accepted: [], tag_key: null, target_id: null, reading_skill_key: null, explanation_vi: "", needs_review: false, review_reason: null }],
    });
  };
  const addPart = () => {
    const key = String.fromCharCode(65 + d.parts.length);
    change({ ...d, parts: [...d.parts, { key, name: key, instructions: "" }] });
  };

  async function save(): Promise<string | null> {
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    const r = await sendJson<{ updated_at: string }>(`/api/studio/drafts/${initial.draft.id}`, "PUT", { title, payload: d, base_updated_at: base });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) {
      setMsg(studioErrorText(r.error));
      return null;
    }
    setBase(r.body.updated_at);
    setDirty(false);
    setMsg(studioCopy.saved(new Date().toLocaleTimeString("vi-VN", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })));
    return r.body.updated_at;
  }
  async function approve() {
    const at = dirty ? await save() : base;
    if (!at) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await sendJson<{ template_id: string }>(`/api/studio/drafts/${initial.draft.id}/approve`, "POST", { base_updated_at: at });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(studioErrorText(r.error));
    router.refresh();
  }
  async function discard() {
    const r = await sendJson(`/api/studio/drafts/${initial.draft.id}/discard`, "POST", {});
    if (r.ok) router.refresh();
    else setMsg(studioErrorText(r.error));
  }

  const ordered = sortForReview(d.items, issues).filter((i) => !onlyFlagged || flagged.has(i.n));
  return (
    <div className="mt-4 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_20rem]">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm font-semibold">
          {studioCopy.titleLabel}
          <input value={title} maxLength={160} onChange={(e) => { setTitle(e.target.value); setDirty(true); }} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3 font-normal" />
        </label>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <input type="checkbox" data-testid="only-flagged" checked={onlyFlagged} onChange={(e) => setOnlyFlagged(e.target.checked)} className="h-5 w-5" />
          {studioCopy.onlyFlagged}
        </label>
        {blocking.filter((i) => i.n === null).map((i) => (
          <p key={i.code} role="alert" data-testid={`issue-draft-${i.code}`} className="rounded-xl bg-[#F8D7D3] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#8A1C12]">
            {studioIssueText[i.code]}
          </p>
        ))}
        {d.parts.map((p, pi) => {
          const items = ordered.filter((i) => i.part === p.key);
          return (
            <section key={p.key} aria-labelledby={`part-${p.key}`} data-testid={`part-${p.key}`} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <h2 id={`part-${p.key}`} className="text-lg font-semibold">{studioCopy.part} {p.key}</h2>
                <input aria-label={studioCopy.partName} value={p.name} onChange={(e) => change({ ...d, parts: d.parts.map((x, j) => (j === pi ? { ...x, name: e.target.value } : x)) })} className="min-h-11 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-line px-3" />
                <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
                  {studioCopy.partSkill}
                  <select data-testid={`part-${p.key}-skill`} defaultValue="__" onChange={(e) => e.target.value !== "__" && partSkill(p.key, e.target.value || null)} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3">
                    <option value="__">{studioCopy.partSkillApply}</option>
                    <option value="">{studioCopy.noSkill}</option>
                    {context.skills.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </label>
              </div>
              <textarea lang="en" aria-label={studioCopy.partInstructions} rows={2} value={p.instructions} onChange={(e) => change({ ...d, parts: d.parts.map((x, j) => (j === pi ? { ...x, instructions: e.target.value } : x)) })} className="rounded-lg border border-line px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {items.map((i) => (
                  <ItemCard key={i.n} item={i} parts={d.parts} passages={d.passages} context={context} issues={issues.filter((x) => x.n === i.n)}
                    onChange={(patch) => upd(i.n, patch)} onRemove={() => change({ ...d, items: d.items.filter((x) => x.n !== i.n) })} />
                ))}
              </ul>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => addItem(p.key)} className="self-start text-sm text-accent underline">{studioCopy.addItem}</button>
            </section>
          );
        })}
        <button type="button" onClick={addPart} className="self-start text-sm text-accent underline">{studioCopy.addPart}</button>
        <section aria-labelledby="passages" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <h2 id="passages" className="text-lg font-semibold">{studioCopy.passages}</h2>
          {d.passages.map((p, k) => (
            <div key={p.ref} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white p-4">
              <input aria-label={studioCopy.passageTitle} value={p.title} onChange={(e) => change({ ...d, passages: d.passages.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, title: e.target.value } : x)) })} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3" />
              <textarea lang="en" aria-label={studioCopy.passageText} rows={6} value={p.text} onChange={(e) => change({ ...d, passages: d.passages.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, text: e.target.value } : x)) })} className="rounded-lg border border-line px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
            </div>
          ))}
          <button type="button" onClick={() => change({ ...d, passages: [...d.passages, { ref: `P${d.passages.length + 1}`, title: `P${d.passages.length + 1}`, text: " " }] })} className="self-start text-sm text-accent underline">{studioCopy.addPassage}</button>
        </section>
        {msg ? <p role="status" data-testid="studio-msg" className="text-sm">{msg}</p> : null}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void save()} data-testid="studio-save" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 font-semibold">{busy ? studioCopy.saving : studioCopy.save}</button>
          <button type="button" disabled={busy || blocking.length > 0} onClick={() => void approve()} data-testid="studio-approve" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-50">{studioCopy.approve}</button>
          <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void discard()} className="min-h-12 px-3 text-sm text-muted underline">{studioCopy.discard}</button>
        </div>
        {blocking.length > 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-[#8A1C12]" data-testid="approve-blocked">{studioCopy.approveBlocked(blocking.length)}</p> : null}
      </div>
      <SidePanel summary={summary} aiCostUsd={initial.draft.aiCostUsd} gate={initial.gate} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

  The "Thêm bài đọc" button seeds `text: " "`, which the strict schema refuses on save (trimmed min 1) — the save then shows "Dữ liệu chưa hợp lệ" until the text is typed. That is intended (no empty reading text is ever stored).

- [ ] **Step 6: Assign + edit-after-approval.**

```tsx
// components/studio/AssignStudioForm.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { addDays } from "@/lib/inquiries/dates";
import { sendJson } from "@/lib/studio/client";
import { studioErrorText } from "@/lib/studio/view";

/** Giao bài (spec §3 step 4): ngày làm + hạn nộp → the child sees it in "Việc tiếp theo" from ngày làm. */
export function AssignStudioForm({ draftId, today }: { draftId: string; today: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [start, setStart] = useState(today);
  const [due, setDue] = useState(addDays(today, 7));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const clientId = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());
  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await sendJson(`/api/studio/drafts/${draftId}/assign`, "POST", { start_on: start, due_on: due, client_id: clientId.current });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setMsg(studioErrorText(r.error));
    clientId.current = crypto.randomUUID();
    router.refresh();
  }
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3" data-testid="assign-form">
      <h3 className="w-full font-semibold">{studioCopy.assignTitle}</h3>
      <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
        {studioCopy.startOn}
        <input type="date" data-testid="assign-start" value={start} onChange={(e) => setStart(e.target.value)} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3" />
      </label>
      <label className="flex flex-col text-xs font-semibold">
        {studioCopy.dueOn}
        <input type="date" data-testid="assign-due" value={due} onChange={(e) => setDue(e.target.value)} className="min-h-11 rounded-lg border border-line px-3" />
      </label>
      <button type="submit" disabled={busy} data-testid="assign-submit" className="min-h-12 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 font-bold text-white">{studioCopy.assign}</button>
      {msg ? <p role="alert" className="w-full text-sm text-[#8A1C12]">{msg}</p> : null}
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/studio/EditApprovedButton.tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useRef } from "react";
import { studioCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { sendJson } from "@/lib/studio/client";

/** "Sửa đề đã duyệt": a new draft that will become version n+1; assigned work keeps its version. */
export function EditApprovedButton({ draftId }: { draftId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const clientId = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());
  return (
    <button type="button" className="mt-4 min-h-11 text-sm text-accent underline" data-testid="edit-approved"
      onClick={async () => {
        const r = await sendJson<{ id: string }>(`/api/studio/drafts/${draftId}/edit`, "POST", { client_id: clientId.current });
        if (r.ok) router.push(`/studio/drafts/${r.body.id}`);
      }}>
      {studioCopy.editApproved}
    </button>
  );
}
```

### Task 15: Hộp duyệt → tab "Cần thầy/cô xem (n)" (board 21)

**Board 21 (approved 05/11):** a third tab in Hộp duyệt "Cần thầy/cô xem (n)"; one card per submitted attempt with items waiting: the test name (source), "Nộp lúc …", "lần đầu / lần làm n", **"chờ n ngày"**; per item: "Câu n", the stem, "Đáp án: …" + "Chấp nhận: …", "<Em> viết: …", two buttons **Đúng / Sai**; "Lưu". "Đúng" means the answer is accepted for later attempts (said once in the intro). - [ ] **Step 1: Tabs.** Replace `components/review/ReviewTabs.tsx` with:

```tsx
// components/review/ReviewTabs.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { skillStateCopy, studioInboxCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";

type Tab = "work" | "skills" | "studio";

/**
 * Hộp duyệt: "Bài làm" (/review, S5), "Kỹ năng" (/review/skills, S7a), "Cần thầy/cô xem" (/review/studio, Studio B1).
 * A count is null when it could not be read (e.g. before its migration): the tab shows without a number.
 */
export function ReviewTabs({ active, skillsCount, studioCount = null }: { active: Tab; skillsCount: number | null; studioCount?: number | null }) {
  const tab = (key: Tab, href: string, label: string) => (
    <Link
      href={href}
      aria-current={active === key ? "page" : undefined}
      data-testid={`review-tab-${key}`}
      className={`inline-flex min-h-11 items-center border-b-2 px-3 text-sm ${active === key ? "border-accent font-semibold text-ink" : "border-transparent text-muted hover:text-ink"}`}
    >
      {label}
    </Link>
  );
  const withCount = (label: string, n: number | null) => (n === null ? label : `${label} ${skillStateCopy.tabCount(n)}`);
  return (
    <nav aria-label={skillStateCopy.tabsLabel} className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line">
      {tab("work", "/review", skillStateCopy.tabWork)}
      {tab("skills", "/review/skills", withCount(skillStateCopy.tabSkills, skillsCount))}
      {tab("studio", "/review/studio", withCount(studioInboxCopy.tab, studioCount))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

  (Keep `skillStateCopy.tabCount` — it renders "(n)". The `review-tab-skills` testid is unchanged.)

```ts
import { allowRealLearners } from "@/lib/env";
import { parsePending } from "./shape";
import type { PendingInbox } from "./types";

/** "Cần thầy/cô xem" of this member (call after authorizeStaffPage). null = unreadable (e.g. 0024 not applied). */
export async function loadStudioPending(memberId: string): Promise<PendingInbox | null> {
  const { data, error } = await createAdminClient().rpc("eng_studio_pending", { p_by: memberId, p_allow_real: allowRealLearners() });
  if (error) return null;
  return parsePending(data);
}
```

```tsx
// app/review/studio/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { ReviewTabs } from "@/components/review/ReviewTabs";
import { StudioPendingCard } from "@/components/review/StudioPendingCard";
import { reviewPage, studioInboxCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { authorizeStaffPage } from "@/lib/review/access";
import { loadSkillInbox } from "@/lib/skills/load";
import { skillTabCount } from "@/lib/skills/view";
import { loadStudioPending } from "@/lib/studio/load";
import { createUserClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** Hộp duyệt → "Cần thầy/cô xem" (board 21): Studio items the app could not check. The child's tutor or parent decides. Students: 404. */
export default async function ReviewStudioPage() {
  const supabase = await createUserClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const access = await authorizeStaffPage(user);
  if (access.kind === "login") redirect("/login");
  if (access.kind === "not-found") notFound();
  const [pending, skillInbox] = await Promise.all([loadStudioPending(access.member.id), loadSkillInbox(access.member.id).catch(() => null)]);
  return (
    <>
      <AppHeader name={access.member.displayName} showTestBadge={pending?.attempts.some((a) => a.isTest) ?? false} />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-10" data-testid="studio-inbox">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm text-accent underline">
          {reviewPage.back}
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{studioInboxCopy.title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{studioInboxCopy.intro}</p>
        <ReviewTabs active="studio" skillsCount={skillTabCount(skillInbox, access.member.role)} studioCount={pending?.count ?? null} />
        {pending === null ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4">{studioInboxCopy.notReady}</p>
        ) : pending.attempts.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 py-4" data-testid="studio-inbox-empty">{studioInboxCopy.empty}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
            {pending.attempts.map((a) => (
              <StudioPendingCard key={a.attemptId} attempt={a} />
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </main>
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Card.**

```tsx
// components/review/StudioPendingCard.tsx
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { studioCopy, studioInboxCopy } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { formatDay } from "@/lib/review/format";
import { sendJson } from "@/lib/studio/client";
import type { PendingAttempt } from "@/lib/studio/types";
import { studioErrorText } from "@/lib/studio/view";
import { formatHm } from "@/lib/work/view";

type Verdict = "correct" | "incorrect";

/** One attempt with items waiting (board 21): Đúng / Sai per item, then Lưu. Adults only. */
export function StudioPendingCard({ attempt }: { attempt: PendingAttempt }) {
  const [verdicts, setVerdicts] = useState<Record<string, Verdict>>({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [line, setLine] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const clientId = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());
  async function save() {
    const items = Object.entries(verdicts).map(([item_id, result]) => ({ item_id, result }));
    if (items.length === 0) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await sendJson<{ pending: number; result: { recorded: boolean; correct: number | null; in_app: number | null } }>(
      `/api/review/studio/attempts/${attempt.attemptId}`, "POST", { items, client_id: clientId.current });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setLine(studioErrorText(r.error));
    clientId.current = crypto.randomUUID();
    if (r.body.result.recorded && r.body.result.correct !== null && r.body.result.in_app !== null) {
      setDone(true);
      setLine(studioInboxCopy.doneLine(attempt.alias, r.body.result.correct, r.body.result.in_app));
    } else {
      setLine(studioInboxCopy.partialLine(r.body.pending));
    }
  }
  return (
    <li data-testid={`studio-card-${attempt.attemptId}`} className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-5">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">{attempt.alias} · {attempt.title}</h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted">
        {studioInboxCopy.submitted(`${formatHm(attempt.submittedAt)} ${formatDay(attempt.submittedAt.slice(0, 10))}`)} · {studioInboxCopy.attempt(attempt.attemptNo)} ·{" "}
        <span data-testid={`studio-wait-${attempt.attemptId}`}>{studioInboxCopy.waiting(attempt.waitingDays)}</span>
      </p>
      {done ? null : (
        <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {attempt.items.map((i) => (
            <li key={i.itemId} className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-xl border border-line p-3" data-testid={`studio-item-${attempt.attemptId}-${i.itemId}`}>
              <p className="font-semibold">{studioCopy.itemLabel(i.n)}. <span lang="en" className="font-normal">{i.stem}</span></p>
              <p className="text-sm">{studioInboxCopy.expected}: <span lang="en">{i.expected ?? "—"}</span>{i.accepted.length ? <> · {studioInboxCopy.accepted}: <span lang="en">{i.accepted.join(" / ")}</span></> : null}</p>
              <p className="text-sm">{studioInboxCopy.wrote(attempt.alias)}: <span lang="en" className="font-semibold">{i.given || studioInboxCopy.blankAnswer}</span></p>
              <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label={studioCopy.itemLabel(i.n)}>
                {(["correct", "incorrect"] as const).map((v) => (
                  <button key={v} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={verdicts[i.itemId] === v} data-testid={`verdict-${attempt.attemptId}-${i.itemId}-${v}`}
                    onClick={() => setVerdicts((x) => ({ ...x, [i.itemId]: v }))}
                    className={`min-h-11 rounded-lg border px-4 text-sm font-semibold ${verdicts[i.itemId] === v ? "border-accent bg-[#E3ECE8] text-[#1F4439]" : "border-line bg-white"}`}>
                    {v === "correct" ? studioInboxCopy.correct : studioInboxCopy.incorrect}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {done ? null : (
        <button type="button" disabled={busy || Object.keys(verdicts).length === 0} onClick={() => void save()} data-testid={`studio-save-${attempt.attemptId}`} className="mt-3 min-h-12 rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-50">
          {busy ? studioInboxCopy.saving : studioInboxCopy.save}
        </button>
      )}
      {line ? <p role="status" data-testid={`studio-line-${attempt.attemptId}`} className="mt-3 text-sm">{line}</p> : null}
    </li>
  );
}
```

```ts
import type { StudioReviewView } from "@/lib/studio/types";

/** Board 20 data of a Studio step for the signed-in child; null for weekly topics, before the first submission, or on any error. */
export async function loadStudioReview(activityId: string): Promise<StudioReviewView | null> {
  const user = await currentUser();
  const res = await handleStudentStudioReview({ user, store: createSupabaseWorkStore() }, activityId).catch(() => null);
  return (res?.body as { review?: StudioReviewView | null } | undefined)?.review ?? null;
}
```

```ts
/** A Studio step has only q<n> items (ids made at Duyệt). Used only to refresh the page after a submission. */
export const isStudioStep = (items: { id: string }[]) => items.length > 0 && items.every((i) => /^q[1-9][0-9]{0,2}$/.test(i.id));
```

```tsx
// components/student/StudioReview.tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { studioStudentCopy, work } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import type { StudioReviewView } from "@/lib/studio/types";
import { formatHm } from "@/lib/work/view";

/** "Câu cần xem lại" (board 20). Wrong items with the right answer and why; the waiting numbers; never a score or a count of right items. */
export function StudioReview({ review, children }: { review: StudioReviewView; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [again, setAgain] = useState(false);
  if (again) return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="studio-review-title" data-testid="studio-review" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-white p-5">
      <p role="status" className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent">{work.submittedAt(formatHm(review.submittedAt))}</p>
      <h2 id="studio-review-title" className="text-xl font-bold">{studioStudentCopy.title}</h2>
      {review.wrong.length === 0 && review.pending.length === 0 ? <p className="text-[15px]">{studioStudentCopy.nothingToReview}</p> : null}
      {review.wrong.length > 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-muted">{studioStudentCopy.intro}</p> : null}
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {review.wrong.map((w) => (
          <li key={w.n} data-testid={`studio-wrong-${w.n}`} className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-xl border border-line p-3">
            <p className="font-semibold">{work.questionLabel(w.n)}. <span lang="en" className="font-normal">{w.stem}</span></p>
            <p className="text-sm">{w.given.trim() ? <>{studioStudentCopy.youWrote}: <span lang="en">{w.given}</span></> : studioStudentCopy.blank}</p>
            {w.expected ? <p className="text-sm">{studioStudentCopy.shouldBe}: <b lang="en">{w.expected}</b></p> : null}
            {w.explanationVi ? <p className="text-sm text-muted">{studioStudentCopy.why}: {w.explanationVi}</p> : null}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {review.pending.length > 0 ? (
        <div data-testid="studio-pending" className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-line p-3 text-sm">
          <p className="font-semibold">{studioStudentCopy.pending(review.pending.length)}</p>
          <p className="text-muted">{studioStudentCopy.pendingList(review.pending)}</p>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <Link href="/" data-testid="studio-home" className="flex min-h-12 items-center rounded-xl bg-accent px-5 text-base font-bold text-white">{studioStudentCopy.home}</Link>
        <button type="button" data-testid="studio-retry" onClick={() => setAgain(true)} className="min-h-12 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white px-5 text-base font-semibold">{studioStudentCopy.retry}</button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import { Client } from "pg";
import { aiSettingsCopy, levelLabels, studioInboxCopy, studioStudentCopy, work } from "@/lib/copy/vi";
import { pgConfigFromUrl } from "../../scripts/lib/pg";
import { makeDocx } from "../unit/fixtures/docx";
import { LEARNER_IDS, loginAs } from "./helpers";

// Studio B1 end to end on the TEST family with AI_FAKE=1 (CI). Serial: each step builds on the previous one.
test.describe.configure({ mode: "serial" });
test.skip(!process.env.DATABASE_URL, "needs DATABASE_URL for the read-only checks");

const PDF = Buffer.from("%PDF-1.4\n1 0 obj<<>>endobj\ntrailer<<>>\n%%EOF\n");
const NGUYEN = LEARNER_IDS.nguyen;
async function q<R extends Record<string, unknown>>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<R[]> {
  const db = new Client(pgConfigFromUrl(process.env.DATABASE_URL as string));
  await db.connect();
  try {
    return (await db.query<R>(sql, params)).rows;
  } finally {
    await db.end();
  }
}
let draftUrl = "";
let activityId = "";
const SCORE = /\d+\s*\/\s*\d+|%/;

test("the parent turns AI on (one ai_text consent per child)", async ({ page }) => {
  await loginAs(page, "parent");
  await page.goto("/settings/ai");
  await page.getByTestId("ai-enable").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("ai-state")).toHaveText(aiSettingsCopy.on);
  expect((await q("select 1 from english.consents where scope = 'ai_text' and granted and learner_id = $1", [NGUYEN])).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
});

test("Nguyên's tutor uploads a PDF + a Word key, the AI draft is blocked by item 8, fixing it allows Duyệt, then Giao", async ({ page }) => {
  await loginAs(page, "tutorNguyen");
  await page.getByTestId(`studio-link-${NGUYEN}`).click();
  await page.getByTestId("studio-title").fill("Destination B1 — Unit 5 (e2e)");
  await page.getByTestId("studio-test-file").setInputFiles({ name: "de.pdf", mimeType: "application/pdf", buffer: PDF });
  await page.getByTestId("studio-key-file").setInputFiles({
    name: "dap-an.docx", mimeType: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document", buffer: makeDocx(["1B 2A 3A 4C 5B"]),
  });
  await page.getByTestId("studio-start-ai").click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/studio\/drafts\/[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
  draftUrl = page.url();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-draft")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "draft", { timeout: 30_000 });
  await expect(page.getByTestId("issue-8-missing_key")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-approve")).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("side-ai-cost")).toHaveCount(0); // a tutor never sees AI cost
  await page.getByTestId("item-8-key").fill("is said to be");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-approve")).toBeEnabled();
  await page.getByTestId("studio-approve").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("approved-box")).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByTestId("assign-submit").click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("assigned-list").locator("li")).toHaveCount(1);
  const row = await q<{ id: string }>(
    `select a.id from english.activity_instances a join english.inquiry_instances i on i.id = a.inquiry_instance_id
     join english.inquiry_templates t on t.id = i.template_id and t.version = i.template_version
     where t.kind = 'studio' and a.learner_id = $1 order by i.assigned_at desc limit 1`, [NGUYEN]);
  activityId = row[0]!.id;
});

async function answer(page: Page, id: string, value: string, choice = false) {
  const box = page.getByTestId(`question-${id}`);
  if (choice) await box.getByLabel(value, { exact: true }).check();
  else await box.getByRole("textbox").fill(value);
}

test("Nguyên does the test: closed items are checked, he sees the wrong ones with the right answer and the waiting ones — never a score", async ({ page }) => {
  await loginAs(page, "studentNguyen");
  await page.goto(`/activities/${activityId}`);
  await answer(page, "q1", "B. had", true);
  await answer(page, "q2", "B. can't", true);
  await answer(page, "q3", "A. off", true);
  await answer(page, "q4", "C. A visit that changed Lan's plans", true);
  await answer(page, "q6", "happyness");
  await answer(page, "q7", "I wish I could");
  await answer(page, "q8", "is said to be a");
  await page.getByTestId("help-declaration").getByLabel(work.help.none).check();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: work.submit }).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-review")).toBeVisible();
  for (const n of [2, 5, 6]) await expect(page.getByTestId(`studio-wrong-${n}`)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-wrong-2")).toContainText("A. must");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-pending")).toContainText(studioStudentCopy.pending(2));
  const text = await page.locator("main").innerText();
  expect(text).not.toMatch(SCORE);
  expect(text).not.toContain("đúng ");
});

test("the tutor decides the two waiting items; the result, the tag marks and the skills are updated", async ({ page }) => {
  await loginAs(page, "tutorNguyen");
  await page.goto("/review/studio");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("review-tab-studio")).toContainText(studioInboxCopy.tab);
  const card = page.locator('[data-testid^="studio-card-"]').first();
  const attemptId = ((await card.getAttribute("data-testid")) ?? "").replace("studio-card-", "");
  await page.getByTestId(`verdict-${attemptId}-q7-correct`).click();
  await page.getByTestId(`verdict-${attemptId}-q8-correct`).click();
  await page.getByTestId(`studio-save-${attemptId}`).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId(`studio-line-${attemptId}`)).toContainText(studioInboxCopy.doneLine("Nguyên (thử)", 5, 8));
  const marks = await q<{ tag_key: string }>("select tag_key from english.tag_marks where learner_id = $1 and source = 'practice' order by item_no", [NGUYEN]);
  expect(marks.map((m) => m.tag_key)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["modal-suy-doan", "doc-inference", "dang-tu"]));
  // Điểm cần chú ý shows the new tag; the skill page shows a level reached through the app's own checks (route 2)
  const label = (await q<{ label_vi: string }>("select label_vi from english.error_tags where key = 'dang-tu' and track_id = 'B'"))[0]!.label_vi;
  await page.goto(`/learners/${NGUYEN}/weak`);
  await expect(page.locator("main")).toContainText(label);
  await page.goto(`/learners/${NGUYEN}/targets`);
  await expect(page.getByTestId("skill-row-B-L2-CLOZE")).toContainText(new RegExp(`${levelLabels.SUPPORTED.label}|${levelLabels.DEVELOPING.label}`));
});

test("after the verdicts the child sees no waiting box; the parent sees the AI cost on the approved test", async ({ page, browser }) => {
  await loginAs(page, "studentNguyen");
  await page.goto(`/activities/${activityId}`);
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-review")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("studio-pending")).toHaveCount(0);
  const parent = await (await browser.newContext()).newPage();
  await loginAs(parent, "parent");
  await parent.goto(draftUrl);
  await expect(parent.getByTestId("side-ai-cost")).toBeVisible();
});
```

  Notes for the builder: the alias of the test learner is "Nguyên (thử)" (`scripts/lib/seed.ts`); if `doneLine` is matched against a different alias, read it from `english.learners`. `work.submit` is the submit button text of `WorkForm` (first submission). If `getByLabel` on radio options is ambiguous, use `box.locator('input[type=radio][value="B. had"]').check()`.

```markdown
## Studio B1 — Đề PDF/Word → bài trong app

**Làm gì**
- Phụ huynh bật AI một lần ở **Cài đặt AI** (`/settings/ai`): văn bản đồng ý ngắn, ghi đồng ý `ai_text` cho từng em, hiện chi phí tháng / trần $10 (báo trước ở $8).
- Gia sư hoặc phụ huynh của em: **Tạo bài từ đề** (thẻ của em → "Tạo bài từ đề") → tải đề + đáp án (PDF hoặc .docx, ≤ 15 MB) → **Cho AI đọc đề** (chạy nền, rời trang được) hoặc **Soạn tay**.
- **Duyệt đề**: sửa từng câu (đề, lựa chọn, loại câu, đáp án, đáp án chấp nhận, nhãn, kỹ năng, giải thích), "Kỹ năng cả phần", lọc "Chỉ câu cần xem"; **Duyệt bị chặn** khi còn lỗi (thiếu đáp án, trắc nghiệm sai, nhãn / kỹ năng không hợp lệ, giải thích có dạng điểm, số câu nhảy, câu chưa có nhãn, không còn câu làm trên app). Duyệt → đề khóa + mẫu chủ đề Studio + khung đề giấy → **Giao bài** (ngày làm + hạn nộp).
- Con làm trên trang bài như chủ đề tuần (bài đọc cạnh câu hỏi trên máy tính). Câu đóng chấm ngay; câu viết lại / trả lời ngắn không khớp đáp án và câu mở → **Hộp duyệt → "Cần thầy/cô xem"** (Đúng / Sai, "chờ n ngày"; "Đúng" = chấp nhận cho lần làm sau).
- Con thấy **"Câu cần xem lại"**: câu sai + câu em viết + đáp án đúng + giải thích; câu chờ "Thầy/cô sẽ xem n câu này". **Không điểm, không x/y, không số câu đúng.**
- Khi mọi câu đã có đúng / sai: **Kết quả bài** (nguồn Studio · Luyện) + **dấu nhãn** câu sai (lần đầu tính, làm lại không tính) → Điểm cần chú ý; engine S7a tính lại (cách 2 — câu tự chấm), `lib/engine` không đổi.
- AI chỉ nhận đề + đáp án của thầy/cô + danh sách nhãn / kỹ năng; không bao giờ tên, bài làm hay điểm của con. AI không chấm bài của con.

**SQL cần phụ huynh áp:** `db/migrations/0024_studio_b1.sql` — áp **TRƯỚC** khi thử preview hoặc gộp (chỉ thêm; cần `0022` + `0023` đã áp). Migration kế tiếp sau này là `0025`.

**Cách thử trên preview**
1. Áp 0024 (workflow "Apply migrations" hoặc SQL Editor). Thêm `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` vào Vercel (Preview + Production) — **không gửi khóa qua chat**; đặt trần $10 cho workspace trên Anthropic Console. (Muốn thử không tốn tiền: đặt `AI_FAKE=1` cho Preview.)
2. Đăng nhập phụ huynh → "Cài đặt AI" → **Đồng ý và bật**.
3. Đăng nhập gia sư của Nguyên (thử) → thẻ Nguyên → **Tạo bài từ đề** → tải đề Destination + đáp án → **Cho AI đọc đề** → chờ "AI đang đọc đề…".
4. Ở **Duyệt đề**: sửa các câu có chip đỏ, chọn "Kỹ năng cả phần" nếu cần → **Duyệt** → **Giao bài**.
5. Đăng nhập Nguyên (thử) → Việc tiếp theo → làm bài → nộp → xem "Câu cần xem lại" (không có điểm).
6. Gia sư → Hộp duyệt → "Cần thầy/cô xem" → Đúng / Sai → dòng "Đã xong bài này…".
7. Xem Điểm cần chú ý và Kỹ năng đang theo dõi của Nguyên; phụ huynh xem chi phí AI ở Cài đặt AI.

**Cần phụ huynh quyết (bảng P1–P12 trong kế hoạch):** P1 (kỹ năng gắn được = bộ hiện hành ∩ kỹ năng mà danh mục tính "ACT-SOURCE-QA"; §17), P4 (câu bỏ trống hiện trong "Câu cần xem lại"; §17), P7 (bảng giá ước tính $3 / $15 mỗi triệu token — kiểm lại), P11 (§17), và các dòng còn lại để biết.

**Tiêu chí đạt (spec §0)**
- [ ] Một đề Destination (PDF hoặc .docx) + đáp án duyệt xong ≤ 10 phút, sửa ≤ 5 câu.
- [ ] Con làm trên app; câu đóng chấm ngay; con thấy câu sai + đáp án đúng + giải thích, không thấy điểm hay số câu đúng.
- [ ] Hết câu chờ: Kết quả bài + dấu nhãn được ghi; Điểm cần chú ý cập nhật; tab Kỹ năng phản ánh câu tự chấm mà engine S7a không phải sửa.
- [ ] Chi phí AI trong tháng ≤ $10 (ghi chi phí thật của lần thử vào PROGRESS).
- [ ] CI xanh (typecheck, lint, unit, DB, E2E với `AI_FAKE=1`); review Opus xong.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)

https://claude.ai/code/session_015ozhb22xjdX1tG2koXkZk1
```

## Self-review against the spec

| Spec | Where in this plan |
|---|---|
| §0 criteria 1–4 | Flow Tasks 12–16; criterion 2 = Task 4 (`eng_my_studio_review`) + Task 16 + e2e Task 17; criterion 3 = Tasks 2 (engine input), 4 (result + tag marks), 7 (DB end-to-end incl. `recomputeLearner`), 17; criterion 4 = Tasks 3 (gate, VN month), 9 (cap in the runner, refused runs), 12 (parent sees spend). |
| §1 B-Q1 (split: B1 only) | No *Kiểm tra* timer, no "Kiểm tra chính thức", no "Vì sao mình sai?" chip anywhere. |
| §1 B-Q2 (tutor or parent of the child; tutor sees every test) | `eng_actor_may_manage` in every write (Task 3–4), actions `studio:*` for both (Task 10), `eng_is_staff_of(learner_id)` RLS (Task 1–2); S7a level rules untouched. |
| §1 B-Q3 (AI proposes tag + skill; ± 1 level; reading default from `related`; "Kỹ năng cả phần") | Tasks 5 (context), 6 (normalize default skill), 9 (lists sent), 14 (part skill select). |
| §1 B-Q4 (one-step topic on the S4 rails) | Task 3 approve builds a 1-step `ACT-SOURCE-QA` PRACTICE template; S4 submit/autosave/receipt unchanged; no `studio_attempts` tables. |
| §2 / §3 rows 0–9 | 0 Task 12 · 1 Task 13 · 2 Tasks 9–10 (after + polling) · 3 Task 14 · 4 Task 14 (assign) · 5 Task 16 (layout, numbers) · 6 Tasks 2, 4, 15 · 7 Tasks 4, 16 · 8 Task 4 (trigger + verdict path) · 9 Task 7 (retry: accepted answers, `counts = false`). |
| §4.1 tables | Task 1 (all six; extra columns explained in D1). |
| §4.2 extensions | `inquiry_templates` Task 1–2; `sources` Task 3 (`ST-<template>-<ref>`, author "Thầy/cô"); keys Task 3/6; `eng_compute_auto_checks` Task 2 (Studio only); `eng_engine_inputs` Task 2 (same JSON); `results` + `tag_marks` Task 4 (+ P3 column); `test_frames` Task 3; `files` Task 1–2, 8; purge Task 1. |
| §4.3 `eng_my_studio_review` | Task 4 (+ strict parser Task 10, UI Task 16). |
| §4.4 payload | Task 5 `studioDraftSchema`; template rules Task 6 `buildStudioBuild`. |
| §5.1–§5.7 AI | Task 9 (server-only call, model env, versioned prompt, PDF document / .docx text, tool `save_exercise`, key from the tutor's file, Vietnamese ≤ 25 words, validation, cap + soft cap, pricing in one place, `AI_FAKE`). P6/P7 explain the two deviations. |
| §6 hard cases | numbering / missing key → Task 5 blocking; many answers → `needs_review`; listening / picture → `out_of_app`; `.doc` / image / > 15 MB → Task 8 `checkFile` + copy; AI fails / off / cap / no consent → Tasks 9, 13, 14; network / double submit → S4 rails; edit after assign → Task 3 `eng_edit_studio_draft` + Task 7 test; "Đúng" for later attempts only → Task 2/4 + Task 7 retry test; waiting long → Task 4 `waiting_days`, Task 15; another child → trigger `eng_check_studio_instance` (Task 1); who decides → Task 4; purge → Task 1 + Task 7. |
| §7 who sees what | DB: Task 1–2 RLS + Task 7 matrix; API: Task 10 (cost stripped for tutors, child parser strict); UI: Tasks 14 (side panel), 16; e2e Task 17. |
| §8 tests | Unit: schema (T5), validate (T5), .docx (T8), build (T6), cap / month (T9), cost (T9), ± 1 tag list (T9 `thanh-ngu`), copy (T11). DB: T7 (child reads, review only wrong, topic `{}`, template readers, approved immutable, Studio-only checking, decisions → engine, retry not first, result idempotent + counts, role matrix, purge, migration twice in T1). E2E: T17. |
| §9 parent tasks | PR body (Task 18). |
| §10 items | "How each item of spec §10 was resolved" (top). |

Names used across tasks were checked against each other: `eng_*` signatures of Task 3–4 match `lib/studio/supabaseStore.ts`; `StudioDeps.getAi` / `loadContext(learnerId, trackId)` match `route.ts` and `load.ts`; `DraftPageData` lives in `types.ts`; `questionNumber` / `isStudioStep` / `formatUsd` / `studioErrorText` live in `lib/studio/view.ts`; error codes produced by the SQL `(tag)`s (`draft_changed`, `not_editable`, `not_draft`, `not_approved`, `start_after_due`, `not_pending_item`, `already_decided`, `duplicate_item`, `bad_items`, `parent_only`, `no_target_set`, `no_app_items`, `test_file_required`, `bad_title`, `bad_build`, `not_studio`) all have a text in `studioErrors` except `bad_title`, `bad_build`, `duplicate_item`, `bad_items`, `not_studio` — those fall back to "Dữ liệu chưa hợp lệ." on purpose (the UI never sends them).
